---

## Complete SignaturePad Specification Summary

**File:** `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/spec/components/specialized/signature-pad.md`  
**Tier:** Stateful (§ Frontmatter)  
**Foundation deps:** architecture, accessibility, interactions  
**Shared deps:** (none)

---

### 1. State Machine (§1)

#### 1.1 States (§1.1)

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum State {
    /// No strokes, canvas is blank.
    Idle,
    /// The user is actively drawing (pointer/touch down).
    Drawing,
    /// At least one stroke has been completed.
    Completed,
}
```

Three states:
- **Idle**: initial state; no strokes on canvas
- **Drawing**: entered on `DrawStart` (pointer/touch down); user actively drawing
- **Completed**: entered on `DrawEnd` (pointer/touch up); at least one stroke persisted

#### 1.2 Events (§1.2)

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Event {
    /// Pointer/touch down on the canvas.
    DrawStart {
        x: f64,
        y: f64,
        pressure: f64,
    },
    /// Pointer/touch move while drawing.
    DrawMove {
        x: f64,
        y: f64,
        pressure: f64,
    },
    /// Pointer/touch up, ending a stroke.
    DrawEnd,
    /// Undo the last stroke.
    Undo,
    /// Clear all strokes.
    Clear,
    /// Focus entered the canvas.
    Focus,
    /// Focus left the canvas.
    Blur,
}
```

Seven event types; `DrawStart`, `DrawMove` carry coordinates and pressure; `Focus`/`Blur` pass through regardless of disabled/readonly (§1.5).

#### 1.3 Context (§1.3)

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Context {
    /// The accumulated signature data.
    pub data: Bindable<SignatureData>,
    /// The stroke currently being drawn (None if not drawing).
    pub current_stroke: Option<SignatureStroke>,
    /// Whether the pad is disabled.
    pub disabled: bool,
    /// Whether the pad is read-only (shows existing signature but cannot modify).
    pub readonly: bool,
    /// Stroke color (CSS color string).
    pub pen_color: String,
    /// Stroke width in pixels.
    pub pen_width: f64,
    /// Minimum distance between points to record (for performance).
    pub min_distance: f64,
    /// Whether the canvas has focus.
    pub focused: bool,
    /// Locale for internationalized messages.
    pub locale: Locale,
    /// Resolved translatable messages.
    pub messages: Messages,
    /// Component instance IDs.
    pub ids: ComponentIds,
}
```

**Key fields:**
- `data`: `Bindable<SignatureData>` — accumulated signature strokes (supports controlled/uncontrolled modes)
- `current_stroke: Option<SignatureStroke>` — in-flight stroke during Drawing state
- `disabled`, `readonly`: gates event processing (§1.5, line 318–320)
- `pen_color`: CSS color string; default `"#000000"`
- `pen_width`: pixels; default `2.0`
- `min_distance`: minimum distance threshold for recording points (default `3.0`)
- `focused`: canvas focus state (set by `Focus`/`Blur` events)
- `locale`, `messages`, `ids`: i18n and ID generation

#### 1.4 Props (§1.4)

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, HasId)]
pub struct Props {
    /// Component instance ID.
    pub id: String,
    /// Controlled signature data.
    pub data: Option<SignatureData>,
    /// Default data for uncontrolled mode.
    pub default_data: SignatureData,
    /// Disabled state.
    pub disabled: bool,
    /// Read-only state.
    pub readonly: bool,
    /// Pen color.
    pub pen_color: String,
    /// Pen width in pixels.
    pub pen_width: f64,
    /// Minimum distance between recorded points.
    pub min_distance: f64,
    /// Name for form submission.
    pub name: Option<String>,
}

impl Default for Props {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            id: String::new(),
            data: None,
            default_data: SignatureData::default(),
            disabled: false,
            readonly: false,
            pen_color: "#000000".into(),
            pen_width: 2.0,
            min_distance: 3.0,
            name: None,
        }
    }
}
```

**Defaults:**
- `pen_color`: `"#000000"` (black)
- `pen_width`: `2.0` pixels
- `min_distance`: `3.0` (points within this distance are culled; §1.5 line 356)
- `disabled`, `readonly`: `false`
- `data`: `None` (uncontrolled mode uses `default_data`)

#### 1.5 Full Machine Implementation (§1.5)

```rust
pub struct Machine;

impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
    type State = State;
    type Event = Event;
    type Context = Context;
    type Props = Props;
    type Messages = Messages;
    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;

    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) 
        -> (Self::State, Self::Context) { ... }

    fn transition(
        state: &Self::State,
        event: &Self::Event,
        ctx: &Self::Context,
        _props: &Self::Props,
    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> { ... }

    fn connect(
        state: &'a Self::State,
        ctx: &'a Self::Context,
        props: &'a Self::Props,
        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
    ) -> Self::Api<'a> { ... }
}
```

**Initialization (lines 270–299):**
- State: `Idle` if data is empty, else `Completed`
- Data: controlled if `props.data.is_some()`, else uncontrolled from `props.default_data`
- ComponentIds generated from `props.id`

**Transitions (lines 301–406):**

| State | Event | Target | Side Effects |
|-------|-------|--------|--------------|
| Idle \| Completed | `DrawStart { x, y, pressure }` | Drawing | Create new `SignatureStroke` with initial point; attach `"drawing-listeners"` effect to track pointer drag and call `DrawMove`/`DrawEnd` |
| Drawing | `DrawMove { x, y, pressure }` | — | Append point to `current_stroke` if distance ≥ `min_distance` (lines 346–363) |
| Drawing | `DrawEnd` | Completed | Move `current_stroke` to `data.strokes` if ≥ 2 points; fire `"announce-provided"` effect (line 376) |
| Completed | `Undo` | — | Pop last stroke from `data.strokes` |
| Any | `Clear` | Idle | Clear all strokes, reset `current_stroke` to `None`; fire `"announce-cleared"` effect (line 397) |
| Any | `Focus` \| `Blur` | — | Update `ctx.focused` regardless of disabled/readonly (lines 309–314) |
| *disabled \| readonly* | *any other* | — | No transition (line 319) |

**Effects:**
- `"drawing-listeners"` (lines 334–341): Uses `use_platform_effects()` to track pointer drag; sends `DrawMove` and `DrawEnd` events
- `"announce-provided"` (lines 376–379): Screen reader announcement via `platform.announce(ctx.messages.signature_provided(&ctx.locale))`
- `"announce-cleared"` (lines 397–400): Screen reader announcement via `platform.announce(ctx.messages.signature_cleared(&ctx.locale))`

---

### 2. Anatomy (§2)

#### 2.1 Part Enum (§1.6, line 424)

```rust
#[derive(ComponentPart)]
#[scope = "signature-pad"]
pub enum Part {
    Root,
    Canvas,
    ClearTrigger,
    UndoTrigger,
    Label,
    Guide,
    HiddenInput,
}
```

Seven parts (ordered by spec §2 tree):
1. **Root**: wrapper `<div>`; state attribute container
2. **Label**: `<label>` element; describes canvas
3. **Canvas**: `<canvas>` element; drawing surface
4. **Guide**: `<div>` (optional); placeholder text when empty
5. **ClearTrigger**: `<button>` (optional); clears all strokes
6. **UndoTrigger**: `<button>` (optional); undoes last stroke
7. **HiddenInput**: `<input type="hidden">` (optional); form submission with SVG path value

Scope: `"signature-pad"`

---

### 3. ConnectApi / Api Surface (§1.6)

```rust
pub struct Api<'a> {
    state: &'a State,
    ctx: &'a Context,
    props: &'a Props,
    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
}

impl<'a> Api<'a> {
    pub fn is_drawing(&self) -> bool { matches!(self.state, State::Drawing) }
    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool { self.ctx.data.get().is_empty() }
    pub fn data(&self) -> &SignatureData { self.ctx.data.get() }

    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn canvas_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn clear_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn undo_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn guide_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }

    pub fn guide_text(&self) -> String { (self.ctx.messages.guide_text)(&self.ctx.locale) }

    pub fn on_canvas_pointer_down(&self, x: f64, y: f64, pressure: f64) {
        (self.send)(Event::DrawStart { x, y, pressure });
    }
    pub fn on_clear(&self) { (self.send)(Event::Clear); }
    pub fn on_undo(&self) { (self.send)(Event::Undo); }
}

impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
    type Part = Part;
    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap { ... }
}
```

#### 3.1 Query Methods

- `is_drawing() -> bool`: `State::Drawing`
- `is_empty() -> bool`: `data.strokes.is_empty()`
- `data() -> &SignatureData`: reference to current strokes
- `guide_text() -> String`: localized guide placeholder

#### 3.2 Attribute Maps by Part

**Root (lines 446–461):**
- `data-ars-scope="signature-pad"`
- `data-ars-part="root"`
- `id` (from `ComponentIds::id()`)
- `data-ars-state`: `"idle"` | `"drawing"` | `"completed"`
- `data-ars-disabled` (bool attr if disabled)
- `data-ars-readonly` (bool attr if readonly)

**Canvas (lines 463–494):**
- `data-ars-scope="signature-pad"`
- `data-ars-part="canvas"`
- `id` (from `ComponentIds::part("canvas")`)
- `tabindex="0"` (keyboard focusable)
- `class="ars-touch-none"` (CSS class to prevent browser scroll during touch)
- `role`: `"application"` (if interactive) | `"img"` (if disabled or readonly) (lines 473–476)
- `aria-label`: from `messages.canvas_label(&locale)`
- `aria-labelledby`: ID of Label part
- `aria-disabled="true"` (if disabled)
- `aria-readonly="true"` (if readonly)
- `data-ars-focus-visible` (bool attr if focused)

**ClearTrigger (lines 496–509):**
- `data-ars-scope="signature-pad"`
- `data-ars-part="clear-trigger"`
- `id` (from `ComponentIds::part("clear")`)
- `type="button"`
- `aria-label`: from `messages.clear_label(&locale)`
- `disabled` (if `is_empty()` or disabled or readonly)

**UndoTrigger (lines 511–524):**
- `data-ars-scope="signature-pad"`
- `data-ars-part="undo-trigger"`
- `id` (from `ComponentIds::part("undo")`)
- `type="button"`
- `aria-label`: from `messages.undo_label(&locale)`
- `disabled` (if `is_empty()` or disabled or readonly)

**Label (lines 526–533):**
- `data-ars-scope="signature-pad"`
- `data-ars-part="label"`
- `id` (from `ComponentIds::part("label")`)

**Guide (lines 535–545):**
- `data-ars-scope="signature-pad"`
- `data-ars-part="guide"`
- `data-ars-hidden` (bool attr if `!is_empty()` — hidden when strokes present)

**HiddenInput (lines 553–565):**
- `data-ars-scope="signature-pad"`
- `data-ars-part="hidden-input"`
- `type="hidden"`
- `name` (from `props.name` if present)
- `value`: SVG path data from `data.to_svg_path()` (§ Stroke data types below)

#### 3.3 Event Handlers

- `on_canvas_pointer_down(x: f64, y: f64, pressure: f64)`: Sends `Event::DrawStart { x, y, pressure }`
- `on_clear()`: Sends `Event::Clear`
- `on_undo()`: Sends `Event::Undo`

### 4. Accessibility (§3)

#### 4.1 ARIA Attributes (§3.1)

| Attribute | Part | Value | Condition |
|-----------|------|-------|-----------|
| `role="application"` | Canvas | Custom interaction model | When interactive (not disabled/readonly) |
| `role="img"` | Canvas | Visual content role | When disabled or readonly |
| `aria-label` | Canvas | `messages.canvas_label(&locale)` | Always |
| `aria-labelledby` | Canvas | Label part ID | Always |
| `aria-disabled="true"` | Canvas | — | When disabled |
| `aria-readonly="true"` | Canvas | — | When readonly |
| `tabindex="0"` | Canvas | Keyboard focusable | Always |
| `data-ars-focus-visible` | Canvas | Bool attr | When focused via keyboard |
| `aria-label` | ClearTrigger | `messages.clear_label(&locale)` | Always |
| `aria-label` | UndoTrigger | `messages.undo_label(&locale)` | Always |

#### 4.2 Screen Reader Announcements (§3.3)

Visually-hidden live region (assumed `aria-live="polite"` in adapter):
- **"Signature provided"** (via `messages.signature_provided(&locale)`): Fired after `DrawEnd` transition (§1.5, line 376)
- **"Signature cleared"** (via `messages.signature_cleared(&locale)`): Fired after `Clear` transition (§1.5, line 397)

### 5. Internationalization (§4)

#### 5.1 Messages Structure (§4.1)

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Messages {
    /// Accessible label for the canvas.
    pub canvas_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
    /// Accessible label for the clear button.
    pub clear_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
    /// Accessible label for the undo button.
    pub undo_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
    /// Screen reader announcement when signature is provided.
    pub signature_provided: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
    /// Screen reader announcement when signature is cleared.
    pub signature_cleared: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
    /// Guide placeholder text.
    pub guide_text: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
}

impl Default for Messages {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            canvas_label: MessageFn::static_str("Signature pad"),
            clear_label: MessageFn::static_str("Clear signature"),
            undo_label: MessageFn::static_str("Undo last stroke"),
            signature_provided: MessageFn::static_str("Signature provided"),
            signature_cleared: MessageFn::static_str("Signature cleared"),
            guide_text: MessageFn::static_str("Sign here"),
        }
    }
}

impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}
```

**Six message keys** (§4.1, table):

| Key | Default (en-US) | Usage |
|-----|-----------------|-------|
| `canvas_label` | `"Signature pad"` | Canvas `aria-label` |
| `clear_label` | `"Clear signature"` | Clear button `aria-label` |
| `undo_label` | `"Undo last stroke"` | Undo button `aria-label` |
| `guide_text` | `"Sign here"` | Guide part placeholder text; via `guide_text()` method |
| `signature_provided` | `"Signature provided"` | Live region announcement after `DrawEnd` |
| `signature_cleared` | `"Signature cleared"` | Live region announcement after `Clear` |

### 6. Stroke Data Types & Representation (§ Intro, lines 19–123)

#### 6.1 Point Structure

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct SignaturePoint {
    /// The x coordinate of the point.
    pub x: f64,
    /// The y coordinate of the point.
    pub y: f64,
    /// Pressure from 0.0 to 1.0 (for pressure-sensitive input).
    pub pressure: f64,
    /// Timestamp in milliseconds.
    pub timestamp: f64,
}
```

**Four fields per point:**
- `x`, `y`: Absolute coordinates (adapter must transform from page space to canvas space, accounting for CSS scaling and `devicePixelRatio`)
- `pressure`: Normalized 0.0–1.0 (pressure-sensitive input; adapter may simulate if unavailable)
- `timestamp`: Milliseconds (recorded by adapter)

#### 6.2 Stroke Structure

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct SignatureStroke {
    /// The points in the stroke.
    pub points: Vec<SignaturePoint>,
}
```

A continuous pen-down-to-pen-up sequence.

#### 6.3 SignatureData Structure & Methods

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct SignatureData {
    /// The strokes in the signature.
    pub strokes: Vec<SignatureStroke>,
}

impl SignatureData {
    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.strokes.is_empty()
    }

    pub fn to_svg_path(&self) -> String {
        let mut path = String::new();
        for stroke in &self.strokes {
            if stroke.points.is_empty() { continue; }
            path.push_str(format!("M{:.1},{:.1}", stroke.points[0].x, stroke.points[0].y));
            for point in &stroke.points[1..] {
                path.push_str(format!(" L{:.1},{:.1}", point.x, point.y));
            }
        }
        path
    }

    pub fn point_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.strokes.iter().map(|s| s.points.len()).sum()
    }
}
```

**Methods:**
- `is_empty()`: True if no strokes
- `to_svg_path() -> String`: SVG moveto/lineto path string (e.g., `"M0.0,0.0 L1.5,2.3 L3.1,4.2"`) used for HiddenInput value
- `point_count() -> usize`: Total points across all strokes

#### 6.4 Point Validation & Culling

**Distance threshold (§1.5, lines 346–363):**
```rust
if let Some(last) = stroke.points.last() {
    let dx = x - last.x;
    let dy = y - last.y;
    let dist = (dx * dx + dy * dy).sqrt();
    if dist >= ctx.min_distance {
        stroke.points.push(...);
    }
}
```

Points within `min_distance` (default 3.0) are dropped for performance.

**Stroke validation (§1.5, line 370):**
```rust
if stroke.points.len() >= 2 {
    // Add to data.strokes
}
```

Single-point strokes are discarded; minimum 2 points per stroke persisted.

---

### 7. Export to Image Data (§ Intro, lines 72–123)

#### 7.1 Export Format Enum

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SignatureFormat {
    /// PNG format.
    Png,
    /// SVG format.
    Svg,
    /// JPEG format.
    Jpeg,
    /// Base64-encoded PNG format.
    Base64Png,
    /// Raw point data.
    Points,
}
```

Five export formats.

#### 7.2 Export Result Enum

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum SignatureExport {
    /// PNG image bytes.
    Png(Vec<u8>),
    /// SVG markup string.
    Svg(String),
    /// JPEG image bytes.
    Jpeg(Vec<u8>),
    /// Base64-encoded PNG string (suitable for `data:` URIs).
    Base64(String),
    /// Raw point data for vector reconstruction.
    /// Each inner `Vec` is one continuous stroke.
    Points(Vec<Vec<SignaturePoint>>),
}
```

Five result variants matching formats.

#### 7.3 Export Method

```rust
impl SignatureData {
    pub fn export(&self, format: SignatureFormat) -> SignatureExport {
        match format {
            SignatureFormat::Svg => SignatureExport::Svg(self.to_svg_path()),
            SignatureFormat::Points => {
                SignatureExport::Points(
                    self.strokes.iter().map(|s| s.points.clone()).collect()
                )
            }
            // Png, Jpeg, Base64Png require adapter-side canvas rendering.
            _ => unimplemented!("Raster export is handled by the adapter layer"),
        }
    }
}
```

**Core behavior (lines 112–122):**
- **`SignatureFormat::Svg`**: Returns `SignatureExport::Svg(self.to_svg_path())` — SVG path string
- **`SignatureFormat::Points`**: Returns raw `Vec<Vec<SignaturePoint>>` — one inner vector per stroke (for server-side reconstruction)
- **`SignatureFormat::Png`, `Jpeg`, `Base64Png`**: **Adapter-side rendering**; core library does not implement (unimplemented!)

### 8. Pen Color & Width Customization (§1.3, 1.4)

**Props (§1.4, lines 229–234):**
- `pen_color: String` — CSS color string (default: `"#000000"`)
- `pen_width: f64` — pixels (default: `2.0`)

**Context (§1.3, lines 196–199):**
- `pen_color: String` — mirrored from props
- `pen_width: f64` — mirrored from props

**Adapter responsibility:** Render canvas strokes using these values; core does not specify rendering API.

### 10. Clear & Undo Actions (§1.5, 1.6)

#### 10.1 Clear Trigger

**Part:** `Part::ClearTrigger`

**Attributes (lines 496–509):**
- Type: `"button"`
- `aria-label`: `messages.clear_label(&locale)`
- `disabled` if: `is_empty()` OR `disabled` OR `readonly`

**Handler:** `api.on_clear()` → Sends `Event::Clear`

**Transition (§1.5, lines 393–401):**
- From any state
- Clears all strokes (`data.strokes.clear()`)
- Moves to `State::Idle`
- Fires `"announce-cleared"` effect

#### 10.2 Undo Trigger

**Part:** `Part::UndoTrigger`

**Attributes (lines 511–524):**
- Type: `"button"`
- `aria-label`: `messages.undo_label(&locale)`
- `disabled` if: `is_empty()` OR `disabled` OR `readonly`

**Handler:** `api.on_undo()` → Sends `Event::Undo`

**Transition (§1.5, lines 384–389):**
- From `State::Completed` only
- Pops last stroke (`data.strokes.pop()`)
- State unchanged (stays `Completed` or drops to `Idle` if now empty — **spec omits explicit state handling**)

### 11. Effects (Named Constants) (§1.5)

Two named effects defined:

1. **`"drawing-listeners"`** (lines 334–341)
   - **Fired:** On `DrawStart` transition (§1.5, line 334)
   - **Logic:**
     ```rust
     platform.track_pointer_drag(
         Box::new(move |x, y| { 
             send_move.call_if_alive(Event::DrawMove { x, y, pressure: 0.5 }); 
         }),
         Box::new(move || { 
             send_up.call_if_alive(Event::DrawEnd); 
         }),
     )
     ```
   - **Purpose:** Tracks pointer/touch movements and sends `DrawMove` and `DrawEnd` events
   - **Pressure hardcoded:** `0.5` (spec does not explain; adapter may override)

2. **`"announce-provided"`** (lines 376–379)
   - **Fired:** On `DrawEnd` transition
   - **Logic:**
     ```rust
     platform.announce(&(ctx.messages.signature_provided)(&ctx.locale));
     no_cleanup()
     ```
   - **Purpose:** Screen reader announcement: "Signature provided"

3. **`"announce-cleared"`** (lines 397–400)
   - **Fired:** On `Clear` transition
   - **Logic:**
     ```rust
     platform.announce(&(ctx.messages.signature_cleared)(&ctx.locale));
     no_cleanup()
     ```
   - **Purpose:** Screen reader announcement: "Signature cleared"

---

### 12. Canvas Lifecycle & Adapter Concerns (§ Intro, lines 126)

**Core spec requirements for adapter:**

1. **Touch-action CSS:** Canvas must have `class="ars-touch-none"` to prevent browser scroll/pan during touch drawing (line 470)

2. **High-DPI scaling:**
   ```
   canvas.width = clientWidth * devicePixelRatio
   ```

3. **Coordinate transformation:** Touch/pointer coordinates must be transformed from page space to canvas space accounting for **both** CSS scaling **and** `devicePixelRatio`

4. **GPU resource handling:** Adapter should listen for `webglcontextlost`/`webglcontextrestored` events (not required by state machine)

---

### 13. Form Submission (§1.6, line 553)

**HiddenInput Part:**
- Type: `"hidden"`
- `name` (from `props.name`)
- `value`: SVG path data from `data.get().to_svg_path()`

Enables form submission of signature as SVG path string in form data.

### 14. Shared Types & Dependencies

**Foundation deps (Frontmatter):** `architecture`, `accessibility`, `interactions`

**Shared deps (Frontmatter):** (none)

**Related components (Frontmatter):** (none)

**Referenced types in spec (not yet in shared/):**
- `Bindable<T>` — controlled/uncontrolled wrapper
- `ComponentIds` — ID generation helper
- `Locale` — locale identifier
- `Messages` / `MessageFn` — i18n message container
- `TransitionPlan<M>` — state machine transition builder
- `AttrMap` / `HtmlAttr` / `AriaAttr` — attribute container

- `use_platform_effects()` → `platform.track_pointer_drag()`, `platform.announce()`

These are assumed to be defined in foundation/shared modules or `ars_core` crate.

---

### 15. Tier & Summary

**Tier:** **Stateful** (Frontmatter)

**Key characteristics:**
- Three-state machine (Idle → Drawing → Completed)
- Reactive to pointer/touch input
- Stroke accumulation & undo/clear history
- Dual-mode data binding (controlled/uncontrolled)
- Canvas rendering responsibility delegated to adapter
- Full accessibility (ARIA roles, live region announcements, keyboard navigation)
- i18n support via `Messages` trait
- Form submission support via HiddenInput

---

This spec defines a complete, production-ready signature capture component with a clean separation between core state machine logic (Rust, no canvas dependencies) and adapter-side rendering/input handling.

#### **Machine Trait Implementation** (Lines 254-386):
```rust
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Machine;

impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
    type State = State;
    type Event = Event;
    type Context = Context;
    type Props = Props;
    type Messages = Messages;
    type Effect = Effect;
    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;

    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {
        (State::Idle, Context { ... })
    }

    fn transition(
        state: &Self::State,
        event: &Self::Event,
        _ctx: &Self::Context,
        props: &Self::Props,
    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
        match (state, event) {
            (_, Event::Copy) if props.disabled => None,
            (State::Idle, Event::Copy) => Some(copying_plan()),
            (State::Copied | State::Error, Event::Copy) => {
                Some(copying_plan().cancel_effect(Effect::FeedbackTimer))
            }
            // ... more match arms
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    fn on_props_changed(old: &Props, new: &Props) -> Vec<Event> {
        assert_eq!(old.id, new.id, "clipboard::Props.id must remain stable after init");
        let mut events = Vec::new();
        if old.value != new.value {
            events.push(Event::SetValue(new.value.clone()));
        }
        if old.feedback_duration_ms != new.feedback_duration_ms || old.disabled != new.disabled {
            events.push(Event::SetProps);
        }
        events
    }

    fn connect<'a>(
        state: &'a Self::State,
        ctx: &'a Self::Context,
        props: &'a Self::Props,
        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {
        Api { state, ctx, props, send }
    }
}
```

#### **Part Enum with ComponentPart Derive** (Lines 388-409):

#[scope = "clipboard"]

    Root,

}
```

#### **Api/Connect Surface** (Lines 411-602):
The `Api` struct holds references to all dependencies:
```rust
pub struct Api<'a> {
    state: &'a State,
    ctx: &'a Context,
    props: &'a Props,
    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
}

impl Debug for Api<'_> { ... }

impl Api<'_> {
    // State predicates
    #[must_use]
    pub const fn is_copied(&self) -> bool { matches!(self.state, State::Copied) }
    
    // Accessors
    #[must_use]
    pub fn value(&self) -> &str { self.ctx.value.get() }
    
    // Event dispatchers
    pub fn copy(&self) { (self.send)(Event::Copy); }
    
    // Part-specific attr builders
    #[must_use]
    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    
    #[must_use]
    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    
    #[must_use]
    pub fn trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap { ... }
    
    // ... other part attr methods
}

impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
    type Part = Part;

    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
        match part {
            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),
            Part::Trigger => self.trigger_attrs(),
            // ...
        }
    }
}
```

#### **Effect Helper Functions** (Lines 604-622):
```rust
fn copying_plan() -> TransitionPlan<Machine> {
    TransitionPlan::to(State::Copying)
        .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
            ctx.error = None;
        })
        .with_effect(write_text_effect())
}

fn write_text_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {
    PendingEffect::new(Effect::WriteText, |ctx: &Context, props: &Props, send| {
        if let Some(on_copy) = &props.on_copy {
            on_copy((ctx.value.get().clone(), send));
        } else {
            send.call_if_alive(Event::CopyError(CopyFailureReason::ApiUnavailable));
        }
        no_cleanup()
    })
}
```

### 4. Core Types and Traits from ars_core

**Key imports used in Clipboard** (and typical for all components):

| Type/Trait | Purpose |
|------------|---------|
| `ars_core::Machine` | The main trait implemented by each component's `Machine` struct. Defines `init`, `transition`, `on_props_changed`, `initial_effects`, `connect`. |
| `AriaAttr`, `HtmlAttr` | Enum variants for ARIA and HTML attributes (used in `AttrMap`). |
| `AttrMap` | Key-value store for DOM attributes returned by `Api::*_attrs()` methods and `ConnectApi::part_attrs()`. |
| `Bindable<T>` | Wrapper for controlled/uncontrolled state (e.g., `Bindable<String>` in Clipboard). Tracks whether a value is controlled by parent or managed internally. |
| `Callback<Fn>` | Wrapper for an optional callback function (e.g., `Option<Callback<CopyRequestFn>>`). Used for parent-provided behavior. |
| `ComponentIds` | Generates stable `id` attributes for DOM elements and parts (e.g., `ids.id()`, `ids.part("label")`). Created from `Props.id`. |
| `ComponentMessages` | Trait implemented by the `Messages` struct. Marks it as a messages type for locale-aware rendering. |
| `ComponentPart` | Derive macro for the `Part` enum. With `#[scope = "clipboard"]` generates `data_attrs()` returning `[("data-ars-scope", "clipboard"), ("data-ars-part", part_name)]`. |
| `ConnectApi` | Trait implemented by `Api<'a>`. Requires `type Part` and `fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap`. Used as the framework adapter's handle to the component. |
| `Env` | Provides `locale` and `intl_backend`. Injected into `init()` and held in `Context`. |
| `Locale` | BCP-47 language tag. Used for message localization. |
| `MessageFn<F>` | Wrapper for a callable that takes `&Locale` and returns a localized string. Used in `Messages` fields. |
| `PendingEffect<M>` | Effect intent emitted by `transition()`. Can be named (simple marker) or carry custom logic (closure over Context/Props). |
| `TransitionPlan<M>` | Builder for transitions. Returned by `transition()`. Chains `.to()`, `.apply()`, `.with_effect()`, `.cancel_effect()`. |
| `WeakSend<E>`, `StrongSend<E>` | Callback handles for dispatching events from effects. `WeakSend` is safe across threads but may become invalid; `StrongSend` is a strong Arc wrapper. |
| `no_cleanup()` | Signals that an effect has no cancellation logic to run. |

**Additional types from Timer and ColorPicker:**
| Type | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `CssProperty` | Custom properties and standard CSS props set in `AttrMap` via `set_style()`. |
| `Direction` | LTR/RTL text direction for keyboard navigation. |
| `Bindable::controlled()`, `Bindable::uncontrolled()` | Factory methods to initialize controlled vs uncontrolled values. |
| `HasId` | Derive macro (auto-impl) that marks a struct as having a stable `id: String` field. |
| `IntlBackend` | Trait for locale-aware number/date formatting. Injected via `Env::intl_backend`. |

---

### 5. Key Implementation Patterns & Conventions

**Doc comment structure** (Module-level):
```rust
//! Timer component state machine and connect API.
//!
//! The `Timer` implements countdown or stopwatch functionality with start,
//! pause, resume, reset, and restart controls. It ticks at a configurable
//! interval and transitions to a [`State::Completed`] state when a countdown
//! reaches zero.
//!
//! The agnostic core only emits typed `Effect` intents and exposes the `Api`
//! connect surface; framework adapters translate
//! `Effect::TimerInterval` into a real recurring interval (e.g. `setInterval`)
//! that dispatches `Event::Tick`, and translate `Effect::AnnounceCompleted`
//! into a live-region announcement.
```

**Transition helper pattern**:
```rust
// Simple helper for reused transition logic
fn copying_plan() -> TransitionPlan<Machine> {
    TransitionPlan::to(State::Copying)
        .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| ctx.error = None)
        .with_effect(write_text_effect())
}
```

**Attribute building pattern** (for each Part):
```rust
#[must_use]
pub fn trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
    let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
    let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Trigger.data_attrs();
    
    attrs
        .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
        .set(part_attr, part_val)
        .set(HtmlAttr::Type, "button")
        .set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label), self.trigger_label())
        // ... conditionals based on state
        .set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);  // if disabled
    
    attrs
}
```

**State token pattern** (for `data-ars-state`):
```rust
const fn state_str(&self) -> &'static str {
    match self.state {
        State::Idle => "idle",
        State::Running => "running",
        State::Paused => "paused",
        State::Completed => "completed",
    }
}
```

**Test helper leaking pattern**:
```rust
fn api_for(state: State, current: Duration, mode: Mode, target: Duration) -> Api<'static> {
    let props = Box::leak(Box::new(Props::new().id("timer").mode(mode).target(target)));
    let messages = Messages::default();
    let (_, mut ctx) = Machine::init(props, &Env::default(), &messages);
    ctx.current = current;
    let ctx = Box::leak(Box::new(ctx));
    let state = Box::leak(Box::new(state));
    let send = Box::leak(Box::new(|_: Event| {}));
    Api { state, ctx, props, send }
}
```

**Effect with custom logic**:
```rust
fn write_text_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {
    PendingEffect::new(Effect::WriteText, |ctx: &Context, props: &Props, send| {
        if let Some(on_copy) = &props.on_copy {
            on_copy((ctx.value.get().clone(), send));
        } else {
            send.call_if_alive(Event::CopyError(CopyFailureReason::ApiUnavailable));
        }
        no_cleanup()
    })
}
```

pub struct Rect {
    pub x: f64,
    pub y: f64,
    pub width: f64,
    pub height: f64,
}

impl Rect {
    pub fn right(&self) -> f64 { self.x + self.width }
    pub fn bottom(&self) -> f64 { self.y + self.height }
    pub fn center_x(&self) -> f64 { self.x + self.width / 2.0 }
    pub fn center_y(&self) -> f64 { self.y + self.height / 2.0 }
}

### 3. **Platform Effects Pattern (THE CRITICAL PATTERN)**

**Location: `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/crates/ars-core/src/platform.rs` (lines 21-165)**

The `PlatformEffects` trait is your adapter pattern blueprint. Key method for SignaturePad:

```rust
/// Track global pointer events during a drag operation (color sliders,
/// signature pad, image cropper, etc.). Attaches `pointermove` and `pointerup`
/// listeners at the document/window level so the drag continues even when the
/// pointer leaves the originating element.
///
/// Returns a cleanup function that removes both listeners.
fn track_pointer_drag(
    &self,
    on_move: Box<dyn Fn(f64, f64)>,  // (x, y) coordinates
    on_up: Box<dyn FnOnce()>,
) -> Box<dyn FnOnce()>;
```

**How it works:**
- Agnostic code calls `use_platform_effects()` inside an effect closure
- Passes `on_move` and `on_up` callbacks (no DOM API calls inside them!)
- The adapter (ars-dom) implements `track_pointer_drag_impl` and attaches actual event listeners
- Returns cleanup function that removes listeners

**Example from `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/crates/ars-dom/src/platform.rs` (lines 89-95):**

```rust
fn track_pointer_drag(
    &self,
    on_move: Box<dyn Fn(f64, f64)>,
    on_up: Box<dyn FnOnce()>,
) -> Box<dyn FnOnce()> {
    track_pointer_drag_impl(on_move, on_up)
}
```

pub struct Callback<T: ?Sized>(pub(crate) Arc<T>);

impl<Args: 'static, Out: 'static> Callback<dyn Fn(Args) -> Out + Send + Sync> {
    pub fn new(f: impl Fn(Args) -> Out + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self {
        Self(Arc::new(f))
    }
}
```

        move |_ctx: &Context, props: &Props, _send| {

|------|------|----------|-------|

| **Drag tracking** | `track_pointer_drag()` method | `ars-core/platform.rs:105-109` | Call from effect closure, gets callbacks |
| **Consumer callback** | `Option<Callback<dyn Fn(T) + Send + Sync>>` | Slider/ColorSlider Props | Fire in effect, declare in Props |
| **Pen color** | `ColorValue` | `ars-core/color.rs:36-50` | HSL + alpha, has RGB/hex conversion |
| **Pressure value** | `f64` in `[0.0, 1.0]` | Spec already defines | SignaturePoint.pressure |

## **Implementation Pattern for SignaturePad Agnostic Core**

1. **Define core types in `ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs`:**
   - Reuse `SignaturePoint`, `SignatureStroke`, `SignatureData` from spec
   - Define `Props` with callbacks: `on_stroke_end: Option<Callback<dyn Fn(&SignatureData) + Send + Sync>>`

2. **Emit effects using `track_pointer_drag`:**
   ```rust
   let platform = use_platform_effects();
   platform.track_pointer_drag(
       Box::new(|x, y| { /* append point to current stroke */ }),
       Box::new(|| { /* finalize stroke, fire callback */ }),
   )
   ```

3. **Fire consumer callbacks from state machine:**
   ```rust
   fn on_stroke_end_effect(data: SignatureData) -> PendingEffect<Machine> {
       PendingEffect::new(Effect::StrokeEnd, move |_, props, _| {
           if let Some(cb) = &props.on_stroke_end {
               cb(&data);
           }
           no_cleanup()
       })
   }
   ```

4. **Export to image (framework-specific):**
   - Emit `PendingEffect` that adapter fulfills
   - Pass `&SignatureData` to adapter, adapter renders to canvas/SVG, fires callback with bytes/data URI

This keeps all geometry, point collection, and stroke logic in agnostic core; only canvas/SVG rendering lives in adapters.
```

12	# SignaturePad
13	
14	A canvas-based signature capture component that records pointer/touch strokes as vector
15	path data. It supports undo, clear, and export to PNG/SVG formats.
16	
17	The following types define the signature data model:
18	
19	```rust
20	/// A single point in a signature stroke.
21	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
22	pub struct SignaturePoint {
23	    /// The x coordinate of the point.
24	    pub x: f64,
25	    /// The y coordinate of the point.
26	    pub y: f64,
27	    /// Pressure from 0.0 to 1.0 (for pressure-sensitive input).
28	    pub pressure: f64,
29	    /// Timestamp in milliseconds.
30	    pub timestamp: f64,
31	}
32	
33	/// A continuous stroke (pen-down to pen-up).
34	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
35	pub struct SignatureStroke {
36	    /// The points in the stroke.
37	    pub points: Vec<SignaturePoint>,
38	}
39	
40	/// The complete signature data.
41	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]
42	pub struct SignatureData {
43	    /// The strokes in the signature.
44	    pub strokes: Vec<SignatureStroke>,
45	}
46	
47	impl SignatureData {
48	    /// Check if the signature data is empty.
49	    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
50	        self.strokes.is_empty()
51	    }
52	
53	    /// Convert to SVG path data string.
54	    pub fn to_svg_path(&self) -> String {
55	        let mut path = String::new();
56	        for stroke in &self.strokes {
57	            if stroke.points.is_empty() { continue; }
58	            path.push_str(format!("M{:.1},{:.1}", stroke.points[0].x, stroke.points[0].y));
59	            for point in &stroke.points[1..] {
60	                path.push_str(format!(" L{:.1},{:.1}", point.x, point.y));
61	            }
62	        }
63	        path
64	    }
65	
66	    /// Total number of points across all strokes.
67	    pub fn point_count(&self) -> usize {
68	        self.strokes.iter().map(|s| s.points.len()).sum()
69	    }
70	}
71	
72	/// Export format for the signature.
73	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
74	pub enum SignatureFormat {
75	    /// PNG format.
76	    Png,
77	    /// SVG format.
78	    Svg,
79	    /// JPEG format.
80	    Jpeg,
81	    /// Base64-encoded PNG format.
82	    Base64Png,
83	    /// Raw point data.
84	    Points,
85	}
86	
87	/// Exported signature data in the requested format.
88	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
89	pub enum SignatureExport {
90	    /// PNG image bytes.
91	    Png(Vec<u8>),
92	    /// SVG markup string.
93	    Svg(String),
94	    /// JPEG image bytes.
95	    Jpeg(Vec<u8>),
96	    /// Base64-encoded PNG string (suitable for `data:` URIs).
97	    Base64(String),
98	    /// Raw point data for vector reconstruction.
99	    /// Each inner `Vec` is one continuous stroke.
100	    Points(Vec<Vec<SignaturePoint>>),
101	}
102	
103	impl SignatureData {
104	    /// Export the signature in the requested format.
105	    ///
106	    /// For `Png`, `Jpeg`, and `Base64Png`, the adapter renders strokes onto
107	    /// an off-screen canvas and encodes the result. For `Svg`, the core
108	    /// library generates the markup from stroke data. For `Points`, the raw
109	    /// `SignaturePoint` vectors are returned directly, enabling server-side
110	    /// vector reconstruction regardless of display resolution.
111	    pub fn export(&self, format: SignatureFormat) -> SignatureExport {
112	        match format {
113	            SignatureFormat::Svg => SignatureExport::Svg(self.to_svg_path()),
114	            SignatureFormat::Points => {
115	                SignatureExport::Points(
116	                    self.strokes.iter().map(|s| s.points.clone()).collect()
117	                )
118	            }
119	            // Png, Jpeg, Base64Png require adapter-side canvas rendering.
120	            _ => unimplemented!("Raster export is handled by the adapter layer"),
121	        }
122	    }
123	}
124	```
125	
126	**Canvas element lifecycle:** The Canvas element requires `touch-action: none` (via `class="ars-touch-none"`) to prevent browser scroll/pan during touch drawing. High-DPI scaling must be applied by the adapter: `canvas.width = clientWidth * devicePixelRatio`. Touch/pointer coordinates must be transformed from page space to canvas space accounting for both CSS scaling and devicePixelRatio. The adapter should listen for `webglcontextlost`/`webglcontextrestored` events to handle GPU resource pressure gracefully.

256	### 1.5 Full Machine Implementation
257	
258	```rust
259	/// The machine for the `SignaturePad` component.
260	pub struct Machine;
261	
262	impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
263	    type State = State;
264	    type Event = Event;
265	    type Context = Context;
266	    type Props = Props;
267	    type Messages = Messages;
268	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
269	
270	    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {
271	        let data = match &props.data {
272	            Some(d) => Bindable::controlled(d.clone()),
273	            None => Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_data.clone()),
274	        };
275	
276	        let state = if data.get().is_empty() {
277	            State::Idle
278	        } else {
279	            State::Completed
280	        };
281	
282	        let ids = ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id);
283	        let locale = env.locale.clone();
284	        let messages = messages.clone();
285	
286	        (state, Context {
287	            data,
288	            current_stroke: None,
289	            disabled: props.disabled,
290	            readonly: props.readonly,
291	            pen_color: props.pen_color.clone(),
292	            pen_width: props.pen_width,
293	            min_distance: props.min_distance,
294	            focused: false,
295	            locale,
296	            messages,
297	            ids,
298	        })
299	    }
300	
301	    fn transition(
302	        state: &Self::State,
303	        event: &Self::Event,
304	        ctx: &Self::Context,
305	        _props: &Self::Props,
306	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
307	        // Focus/Blur always pass through regardless of disabled/readonly.
308	        match event {
309	            Event::Focus => {
310	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx| ctx.focused = true));
311	            }
312	            Event::Blur => {
313	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx| ctx.focused = false));
314	            }
315	            _ => {}
316	        }
317	
318	        if ctx.disabled || ctx.readonly {
319	            return None;
320	        }
321	
322	        match (state, event) {
323	            // Start drawing
324	            (State::Idle | State::Completed, Event::DrawStart { x, y, pressure }) => {
325	                let x = *x;
326	                let y = *y;
327	                let pressure = *pressure;
328	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Drawing).apply(move |ctx| {
329	                    ctx.current_stroke = Some(SignatureStroke {
330	                        points: vec![SignaturePoint {
331	                            x, y, pressure, timestamp: 0.0,
332	                        }],
333	                    });
334	                }).with_named_effect("drawing-listeners", |_ctx, _props, send| {
335	                    let platform = use_platform_effects();
336	                    let send_move = send.clone();
337	                    let send_up = send.clone();
338	                    platform.track_pointer_drag(
339	                        Box::new(move |x, y| { send_move.call_if_alive(Event::DrawMove { x, y, pressure: 0.5 }); }),
340	                        Box::new(move || { send_up.call_if_alive(Event::DrawEnd); }),
341	                    )
342	                }))
343	            }
344	
345	            // Continue drawing
346	            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawMove { x, y, pressure }) => {
347	                let x = *x;
348	                let y = *y;
349	                let pressure = *pressure;
350	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
351	                    if let Some(ref mut stroke) = ctx.current_stroke {
352	                        if let Some(last) = stroke.points.last() {
353	                            let dx = x - last.x;
354	                            let dy = y - last.y;
355	                            let dist = (dx * dx + dy * dy).sqrt();
356	                            if dist >= ctx.min_distance {
357	                                stroke.points.push(SignaturePoint {
358	                                    x, y, pressure, timestamp: 0.0,
359	                                });
360	                            }
361	                        }
362	                    }
363	                }))
364	            }
365	
366	            // End drawing
367	            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawEnd) => {
368	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed).apply(|ctx| {
369	                    if let Some(stroke) = ctx.current_stroke.take() {
370	                        if stroke.points.len() >= 2 {
371	                            let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();
372	                            data.strokes.push(stroke);
373	                            ctx.data.set(data);
374	                        }
375	                    }
376	                }).with_named_effect("announce-provided", |ctx, _props, _send| {
377	                    let platform = use_platform_effects();
378	                    platform.announce(&(ctx.messages.signature_provided)(&ctx.locale));
379	                    no_cleanup()
380	                }))
381	            }
382	
383	            // Undo last stroke
384	            (State::Completed, Event::Undo) => {
385	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx| {
386	                    let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();
387	                    data.strokes.pop();
388	                    ctx.data.set(data);
389	                }))
390	            }
391	
392	            // Clear all strokes
393	            (_, Event::Clear) => {
394	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx| {
395	                    ctx.data.set(SignatureData::default());
396	                    ctx.current_stroke = None;
397	                }).with_named_effect("announce-cleared", |ctx, _props, _send| {
398	                    let platform = use_platform_effects();
399	                    platform.announce(&(ctx.messages.signature_cleared)(&ctx.locale));
400	                    no_cleanup()
401	                }))
402	            }
403	
404	            _ => None,
405	        }
406	    }
407	
408	    fn connect(
409	        state: &'a Self::State,
410	        ctx: &'a Self::Context,
411	        props: &'a Self::Props,
412	        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
413	    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {
414	        Api { state, ctx, props, send }
415	    }
416	}
417	```
418	
419	### 1.6 Connect / API
420	
421	```rust
422	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
423	#[scope = "signature-pad"]
424	pub enum Part {
425	    Root,
426	    Canvas,
427	    ClearTrigger,
428	    UndoTrigger,
429	    Label,
430	    Guide,
431	    HiddenInput,
432	}
433	
434	pub struct Api<'a> {
435	    state: &'a State,
436	    ctx: &'a Context,
437	    props: &'a Props,
438	    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
439	}
440	
441	impl<'a> Api<'a> {
442	    pub fn is_drawing(&self) -> bool { matches!(self.state, State::Drawing) }
443	    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool { self.ctx.data.get().is_empty() }
444	    pub fn data(&self) -> &SignatureData { self.ctx.data.get() }
445	
446	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
447	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
448	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
449	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
450	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
451	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.id());
452	        let state_str = match self.state {
453	            State::Idle => "idle",
454	            State::Drawing => "drawing",
455	            State::Completed => "completed",
456	        };
457	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"), state_str);
458	        if self.ctx.disabled { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-disabled"), true); }
459	        if self.ctx.readonly { attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-readonly"), true); }
460	        attrs
461	    }
462	
463	    pub fn canvas_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
464	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
465	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Canvas.data_attrs();
466	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
467	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
468	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("canvas"));
469	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::TabIndex, "0");
470	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Class, "ars-touch-none");
471	
472	        // Role depends on state: application when interactive, img when completed+readonly
473	        if self.ctx.readonly || self.ctx.disabled {
474	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "img");
475	        } else {
476	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, "application");
477	        }
478	
479	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),
480	            (self.ctx.messages.canvas_label)(&self.ctx.locale));
481	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::LabelledBy),
482	            self.ctx.ids.part("label"));
483	
484	        if self.ctx.disabled {
485	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Disabled), "true");
486	        }
487	        if self.ctx.readonly {
488	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ReadOnly), "true");
489	        }
490	        if self.ctx.focused {
491	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-focus-visible"), true);
492	        }
493	        attrs
494	    }
495	
496	    pub fn clear_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
497	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
498	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ClearTrigger.data_attrs();
499	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
500	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
501	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("clear"));
502	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "button");
503	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),
504	            (self.ctx.messages.clear_label)(&self.ctx.locale));
505	        if self.is_empty() || self.ctx.disabled || self.ctx.readonly {
506	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Disabled, "true");
507	        }
508	        attrs
509	    }
510	
511	    pub fn undo_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
512	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
513	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::UndoTrigger.data_attrs();
514	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
515	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
516	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("undo"));
517	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "button");
518	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),
519	            (self.ctx.messages.undo_label)(&self.ctx.locale));
520	        if self.is_empty() || self.ctx.disabled || self.ctx.readonly {
521	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Disabled, "true");
522	        }
523	        attrs
524	    }
525	
526	    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
527	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
528	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Label.data_attrs();
529	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
530	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
531	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("label"));
532	        attrs
533	    }
534	
535	    pub fn guide_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
536	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
537	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Guide.data_attrs();
538	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
539	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
540	        // Hide guide when strokes are present
541	        if !self.is_empty() {
542	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-hidden"), true);
543	        }
544	        attrs
545	    }
546	
547	    /// Returns the guide placeholder text (e.g., "Sign here").
548	    /// The adapter renders this inside the `Guide` part.
549	    pub fn guide_text(&self) -> String {
550	        (self.ctx.messages.guide_text)(&self.ctx.locale)
551	    }
552	
553	    pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
554	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
555	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::HiddenInput.data_attrs();
556	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
557	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
558	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Type, "hidden");
559	        if let Some(ref name) = self.props.name {
560	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name);
561	        }
562	        // Value is SVG path data for form submission
563	        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, self.ctx.data.get().to_svg_path());
564	        attrs
565	    }
566	
567	    pub fn on_canvas_pointer_down(&self, x: f64, y: f64, pressure: f64) {
568	        (self.send)(Event::DrawStart { x, y, pressure });
569	    }
570	
571	    pub fn on_clear(&self) {
572	        (self.send)(Event::Clear);
573	    }
574	
575	    pub fn on_undo(&self) {
576	        (self.send)(Event::Undo);
577	    }
578	}
579	
580	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
581	    type Part = Part;
582	
583	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
584	        match part {
585	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
586	            Part::Canvas => self.canvas_attrs(),
587	            Part::ClearTrigger => self.clear_trigger_attrs(),
588	            Part::UndoTrigger => self.undo_trigger_attrs(),
589	            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),
590	            Part::Guide => self.guide_attrs(),
591	            Part::HiddenInput => self.hidden_input_attrs(),
592	        }
593	    }
594	}
595	```
596	
597	## 2. Anatomy
598	
599	```text
600	SignaturePad
601	├── Root            (required)
602	├── Label           (required -- describes the signature area)
603	├── Canvas          (required -- the drawing surface)
604	├── Guide           (optional -- guide line or placeholder text)
605	├── ClearTrigger    (optional -- button to clear all strokes)
606	├── UndoTrigger     (optional -- button to undo the last stroke)
607	└── HiddenInput     (optional -- for form submission, value = SVG path data)
608	```
609	
610	| Part         | Element    | Key Attributes                                                    |
611	| ------------ | ---------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- |
612	| Root         | `<div>`    | `data-ars-state`, `data-ars-disabled`, `data-ars-readonly`        |
613	| Label        | `<label>`  | Labels the canvas area                                            |
614	| Canvas       | `<canvas>` | `role="application"` or `"img"`, `tabindex="0"`, `ars-touch-none` |
615	| Guide        | `<div>`    | Hidden when strokes are present                                   |
616	| ClearTrigger | `<button>` | `aria-label="Clear signature"`, disabled when empty               |
617	| UndoTrigger  | `<button>` | `aria-label="Undo last stroke"`, disabled when empty              |
618	| HiddenInput  | `<input>`  | `type="hidden"`, `name`, `value` (SVG path data)                  |
619	
620	## 3. Accessibility
621	
622	### 3.1 ARIA Roles, States, and Properties
623	
624	| Attribute                | Element      | Value                                       |
625	| ------------------------ | ------------ | ------------------------------------------- |
626	| `role="application"`     | Canvas       | Custom interaction model (when interactive) |
627	| `role="img"`             | Canvas       | Visual content (when disabled/readonly)     |
628	| `aria-label`             | Canvas       | From `messages.canvas_label`                |
629	| `aria-labelledby`        | Canvas       | Label element ID                            |
630	| `aria-disabled="true"`   | Canvas       | When disabled                               |
631	| `aria-readonly="true"`   | Canvas       | When read-only                              |
632	| `aria-label`             | ClearTrigger | From `messages.clear_label`                 |
633	| `aria-label`             | UndoTrigger  | From `messages.undo_label`                  |
634	| `tabindex="0"`           | Canvas       | Keyboard focusable                          |
635	| `data-ars-focus-visible` | Canvas       | When focused via keyboard                   |
636	
637	### 3.2 Keyboard Interaction
638	
639	The canvas uses `role="application"` which opts out of standard screen reader virtual
640	cursor interaction, allowing direct pointer/touch capture. The ClearTrigger and
641	UndoTrigger buttons are standard focusable buttons reachable via Tab.
642	
643	### 3.3 Screen Reader Announcements
644	
645	The `SignaturePad` includes a visually-hidden live region (`aria-live="polite"`) that announces state changes:
646	
647	- "Signature provided" -- after drawing ends (pointer up after strokes)
648	- "Signature cleared" -- after clear action
649	
650	## 4. Internationalization
651	
652	### 4.1 Messages
653	
654	```rust
655	/// The messages for the `SignaturePad` component.
656	#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
657	pub struct Messages {
658	    /// Accessible label for the canvas.
659	    pub canvas_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
660	    /// Accessible label for the clear button.
661	    pub clear_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
662	    /// Accessible label for the undo button.
663	    pub undo_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
664	    /// Screen reader announcement when signature is provided.
665	    pub signature_provided: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
666	    /// Screen reader announcement when signature is cleared.
667	    pub signature_cleared: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
668	    /// Guide placeholder text.
669	    pub guide_text: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,
670	}
671	
672	impl Default for Messages {
673	    fn default() -> Self {
674	        Self {
675	            canvas_label: MessageFn::static_str("Signature pad"),
676	            clear_label: MessageFn::static_str("Clear signature"),
677	            undo_label: MessageFn::static_str("Undo last stroke"),
678	            signature_provided: MessageFn::static_str("Signature provided"),
679	            signature_cleared: MessageFn::static_str("Signature cleared"),
680	            guide_text: MessageFn::static_str("Sign here"),
681	        }
682	    }
683	}
684	
685	impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}
686	```
687	
688	| Key                                | Default (en-US)        | Purpose                    |
689	| ---------------------------------- | ---------------------- | -------------------------- |
690	| `signature_pad.canvas_label`       | `"Signature pad"`      | Canvas aria-label          |
691	| `signature_pad.clear_label`        | `"Clear signature"`    | Clear button label         |
692	| `signature_pad.undo_label`         | `"Undo last stroke"`   | Undo button label          |
693	| `signature_pad.guide_text`         | `"Sign here"`          | Guide placeholder          |
694	| `signature_pad.signature_provided` | `"Signature provided"` | Screen reader announcement |
695	| `signature_pad.signature_cleared`  | `"Signature cleared"`  | Screen reader announcement |
696	
697	RTL: Canvas drawing is direction-agnostic (pointer coordinates are absolute).
698	The layout of Clear/Undo buttons and Label reverses in RTL.
699	
700	## 5. Library Parity
701	
702	> Compared against: Ark UI (`SignaturePad`).
703	
704	### 5.1 Props
705	
706	| Feature                | ars-ui                          | Ark UI                     | Notes                                                    |
707	| ---------------------- | ------------------------------- | -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
708	| `data` / `defaultData` | `data` / `default_data`         | `paths` / `defaultPaths`   | Equivalent (ars-ui uses richer `SignatureData` type)     |
709	| `disabled`             | `disabled`                      | `disabled`                 | Equivalent                                               |
710	| `readOnly`             | `readonly`                      | `readOnly`                 | Equivalent                                               |
711	| `required`             | --                              | `required`                 | Ark-only; form-level concern                             |
712	| `penColor`             | `pen_color`                     | `drawing.size`             | ars-ui has explicit pen color                            |
713	| `penWidth`             | `pen_width`                     | `drawing.size`             | Equivalent                                               |
714	| `simulatePressure`     | (via `SignaturePoint.pressure`) | `drawing.simulatePressure` | ars-ui records real pressure; simulation is adapter-side |
715	| `minDistance`          | `min_distance`                  | --                         | ars-ui has distance threshold                            |
716	| `name`                 | `name`                          | `name`                     | Equivalent                                               |
717	| `translations`         | `messages`                      | `translations`             | Equivalent                                               |
718	
719	**Gaps:** None.
720	
721	### 5.2 Anatomy
722	
723	| Part         | ars-ui         | Ark UI          | Notes                                                 |
724	| ------------ | -------------- | --------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
725	| Root         | `Root`         | `Root`          | Equivalent                                            |
726	| Label        | `Label`        | `Label`         | Equivalent                                            |
727	| Canvas       | `Canvas`       | `Segment` (SVG) | Different rendering: ars-ui uses canvas, Ark uses SVG |
728	| ClearTrigger | `ClearTrigger` | `ClearTrigger`  | Equivalent                                            |
729	| UndoTrigger  | `UndoTrigger`  | --              | ars-ui has undo                                       |
730	| Guide        | `Guide`        | `Guide`         | Equivalent                                            |
731	| HiddenInput  | `HiddenInput`  | `HiddenInput`   | Equivalent                                            |
732	| Control      | --             | `Control`       | Ark has wrapper part                                  |
733	
734	**Gaps:** None. Ark's `Control` is a layout wrapper.
735	
736	### 5.3 Events
737	
738	| Callback | ars-ui                      | Ark UI      | Notes      |
739	| -------- | --------------------------- | ----------- | ---------- |
740	| Draw     | `Event::DrawStart/Move/End` | `onDraw`    | Equivalent |
741	| Draw end | `Event::DrawEnd`            | `onDrawEnd` | Equivalent |
742	
743	**Gaps:** None.
744	
745	### 5.4 Features
746	
747	| Feature              | ars-ui                                  | Ark UI               |
748	| -------------------- | --------------------------------------- | -------------------- |
749	| Stroke capture       | Yes (full path data with pressure/time) | Yes (SVG paths)      |
750	| Undo last stroke     | Yes                                     | No                   |
751	| Clear all            | Yes                                     | Yes                  |
752	| Export to SVG        | Yes                                     | Yes (via getDataUrl) |
753	| Export to PNG/JPEG   | Yes (adapter-side)                      | Yes (via getDataUrl) |
754	| Pressure sensitivity | Yes (recorded per point)                | Yes (simulated)      |
755	| Read-only display    | Yes                                     | Yes                  |
756	| Form submission      | Yes (SVG path data)                     | Yes                  |
757	| SR announcements     | Yes (signature provided/cleared)        | No                   |
758	
759	**Gaps:** None. ars-ui exceeds Ark UI with undo support and richer data model.
760	
761	### 5.5 Summary
762	
763	- **Overall:** Full parity, with additional features.
764	- **Divergences:** ars-ui uses `<canvas>` rendering with `SignatureData` (rich point data with pressure/time); Ark uses `<svg>` with SVG path strings. ars-ui has undo support.
765	- **Recommended additions:** None.
766	
```

Perfect! Now I have enough information to provide a comprehensive API verification report. Let me compile all the findings:

## EXACT CURRENT API OF ARS-CORE STATE-MACHINE ENGINE

### 1. **Machine Trait** (File: `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/crates/ars-core/src/lib.rs`, lines 751-856)

Full trait definition with all associated types:

```rust
pub trait Machine: Sized + 'static {
    type State: Clone + Debug + PartialEq;
    type Event: Clone + Debug;
    type Context: Clone + Debug;
    type Props: Clone + PartialEq + HasId;
    type Messages: ComponentMessages + Clone + Default + 'static;
    type Effect: EffectName;
    type Api<'a>: ConnectApi where Self: 'a;

    fn init(
        props: &Self::Props,
        env: &Env,
        messages: &Self::Messages,
    ) -> (Self::State, Self::Context);

    fn transition(
        state: &Self::State,
        event: &Self::Event,
        context: &Self::Context,
        props: &Self::Props,
    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>>;

    fn connect<'a>(
        state: &'a Self::State,
        context: &'a Self::Context,
        props: &'a Self::Props,
        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
    ) -> Self::Api<'a>;

    fn on_props_changed(_old: &Self::Props, _new: &Self::Props) -> Vec<Self::Event> {
        Vec::new()
    }

    fn initial_effects(
        _state: &Self::State,
        _context: &Self::Context,
        _props: &Self::Props,
    ) -> Vec<PendingEffect<Self>> {
        Vec::new()
    }
}
```

**Key points:**
- `transition` returns `Option<TransitionPlan<Self>>` (None = ignore event)
- `type Effect` MUST exist and satisfy `EffectName` trait (lines 129-156)
- `type Api<'a>` must implement `ConnectApi`
- All methods provided with default implementations except `init`, `transition`, `connect`

---

### 2. **TransitionPlan<M>** (File: `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/crates/ars-core/src/lib.rs`, lines 315-445)

Complete public API:

```rust
pub struct TransitionPlan<M: Machine> {
    pub target: Option<M::State>,
    pub(crate) apply: Option<Box<ApplyFn<M>>>,
    pub(crate) apply_description: Option<&'static str>,
    pub then_send: Vec<M::Event>,
    pub effects: Vec<PendingEffect<M>>,
    pub cancel_effects: Vec<M::Effect>,
}

impl<M: Machine> TransitionPlan<M> {
    // Line 343-344: Creates plan that transitions to new state
    pub const fn to(state: M::State) -> Self

    // Line 360-361: Creates empty plan with no state change
    pub const fn new() -> Self

    // Line 376-387: Adds context mutation; chains with previous if exists
    pub fn apply(mut self, f: impl FnOnce(&mut M::Context) + 'static) -> Self

    // Line 391-400: Context-only mutation without state change
    pub fn context_only(f: impl FnOnce(&mut M::Context) + 'static) -> Self

    // Line 404-407: Enqueues follow-up event after transition
    pub fn then(mut self, event: M::Event) -> Self

    // Line 411-414: Attaches side effect for adapter to manage
    pub fn with_effect(mut self, effect: PendingEffect<M>) -> Self

    // Line 418-424: Build PendingEffect inline from name and closure
    pub fn with_named_effect(
        self,
        name: M::Effect,
        setup: impl FnOnce(&M::Context, &M::Props, WeakSend<M::Event>) -> CleanupFn + 'static,
    ) -> Self

    // Line 431-434: Cancels named effect without replacement
    pub fn cancel_effect(mut self, name: M::Effect) -> Self

    // Line 438-444: Returns short string label for logging
    pub const fn debug_summary(&self) -> &'static str
}
```

**Summary:** All 8 queried methods exist. `with_named_effect` has inline closure signature shown above.

### 3. **PendingEffect<M>** (File: `/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/crates/ars-core/src/lib.rs`, lines 216-291)

```rust
pub struct PendingEffect<M: Machine> {
    pub name: M::Effect,  // Typed identifier for effect intent
    pub target_state: Option<M::State>,
    pub metadata: Option<EffectMetadata>,
    pub(crate) setup: EffectSetupFn<M>,
}

// Internal type alias (line 178-184):
type EffectSetupFn<M> = Box<
    dyn FnOnce(
        &<M as Machine>::Context,
        &<M as Machine>::Props,
        StrongSend<<M as Machine>::Event>,
    ) -> CleanupFn,
>;

impl<M: Machine> PendingEffect<M> {
    // Line 244-257: First argument is M::Effect (the typed effect name)
    pub fn new(
        name: M::Effect,
        setup: impl FnOnce(&M::Context, &M::Props, WeakSend<M::Event>) -> CleanupFn + 'static,
    ) -> Self

    // Line 264-271: Marker-only effect with no-op setup
    pub fn named(name: M::Effect) -> Self

    // Line 275-282: Marker-only effect with typed metadata
    pub fn named_with_metadata(name: M::Effect, metadata: EffectMetadata) -> Self

    // Line 288-290: Execute the effect setup
    pub fn run(self, ctx: &M::Context, props: &M::Props, send: StrongSend<M::Event>) -> CleanupFn
}
```

**Key signature details:**
- `PendingEffect::new(name, setup_closure)` receives `WeakSend<M::Event>` in closure (not `StrongSend`)
- The closure signature: `impl FnOnce(&M::Context, &M::Props, WeakSend<M::Event>) -> CleanupFn`
- `WeakSend<Event>` has method `.call_if_alive(event)` (lines 22-26 in weak_send.rs)
- `StrongSend<E>` is type alias for `Arc<dyn Fn(E) + Send + Sync>` (line 65 in weak_send.rs)

## SUMMARY

All queried APIs are present and correctly implemented. Key findings:

1. **Machine trait** requires `type Effect: EffectName` (no opt-out)
2. **TransitionPlan** fully supports `.to()`, `.context_only()`, `.apply()`, `.with_effect()`, `.with_named_effect()`, `.cancel_effect()`
3. **PendingEffect::new()** receives typed `M::Effect` as first argument; closure gets `WeakSend<M::Event>` (not `StrongSend`)
4. **Bindable** has `.controlled()`, `.uncontrolled()`, `.get()`, `.set()`, `.is_controlled()` (no `.is_empty()`)
5. **ComponentIds** has `.from_id()`, `.id()`, `.part()` plus `.item()` and `.item_part()`
6. **MessageFn** supports `.static_str()` and is callable via `Deref`
7. **HtmlAttr/AriaAttr** confirm all listed variants exist
8. **AttrMap** methods return `&mut Self` for builder-style chaining
9. **Platform effects** NOT a function; `PlatformEffects` trait with `.announce()` and `.track_pointer_drag()` are defined
```

> AGENT

Critical findings. Two spec-vs-reality gaps to resolve before planning:

1. `with_named_effect`'s first arg is a typed `M::Effect`, not a string — and the spec's `Machine` impl omits `type Effect` entirely. So the spec §1.5 won't compile as written.
2. `use_platform_effects()` lives in adapters, not `ars-core` — yet the spec calls it inside agnostic effect closures.

Let me see how sibling components (timer's announce, color_slider's drag tracking) actually plumb the platform in agnostic code, and how `Env` carries it.

261	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
262	pub enum Effect {
263	    /// Adapter starts a recurring interval of [`Context::interval`] that
264	    /// dispatches [`Event::Tick`] on each elapse. Emitted on initial mount when
265	    /// the timer auto-starts (via
266	    /// [`Machine::initial_effects`](ars_core::Machine::initial_effects)) and on
267	    /// every transition into [`State::Running`]. Cancelled whenever the timer
268	    /// leaves [`State::Running`] (pause, completion, reset, or restart); the
269	    /// adapter no-ops the cancellation when no interval is active.
270	    TimerInterval,
271	
272	    /// Adapter announces the [`Messages::completed_announcement`] message into a
273	    /// polite `aria-live` region. Emitted on the countdown transition into
274	    /// [`State::Completed`].
275	    AnnounceCompleted,
276	}

278	/// The machine for the `Timer` component.
279	///
280	/// # Examples
281	///
282	/// Drive a two-second countdown to completion. Each [`Event::Tick`] is
283	/// dispatched by the adapter-owned interval started by
284	/// [`Effect::TimerInterval`]; here we send them directly:
285	///
286	/// ```
287	/// use core::time::Duration;
288	///
289	/// use ars_components::specialized::timer::{Event, Machine, Messages, Props, State};
290	/// use ars_core::{Env, Service};
291	///
292	/// let mut timer = Service::<Machine>::new(
293	///     Props::new()
294	///         .id("egg")
295	///         .target(Duration::from_secs(2))
296	///         .interval(Duration::from_secs(1)),
297	///     &Env::default(),
298	///     &Messages::default(),
299	/// );
300	///
301	/// drop(timer.send(Event::Start));
302	/// assert_eq!(timer.state(), &State::Running);
303	///
304	/// drop(timer.send(Event::Tick)); // 2s -> 1s remaining
305	/// assert_eq!(timer.context().current, Duration::from_secs(1));
306	///
307	/// drop(timer.send(Event::Tick)); // 1s -> 0s: the countdown completes
308	/// assert_eq!(timer.state(), &State::Completed);
309	/// assert_eq!(timer.connect(&|_| {}).formatted_time(), "00:00");
310	/// ```
311	#[derive(Debug)]
312	pub struct Machine;
313	
314	impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
315	    type State = State;
316	    type Event = Event;
317	    type Context = Context;
318	    type Props = Props;
319	    type Messages = Messages;
320	    type Effect = Effect;
321	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
322	
323	    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {
324	        let current = initial_duration(props.mode, props.target);
325	
326	        // A zero-duration countdown is already complete; it must not enter
327	        // `Running` (which would show `00:00` ticking and defer the
328	        // announcement). `auto_start` is ignored in that degenerate case.
329	        let state = if is_instantly_complete(props.mode, props.target) {
330	            State::Completed
331	        } else if props.auto_start {
332	            State::Running
333	        } else {
334	            State::Idle
335	        };
336	
337	        (
338	            state,
339	            Context {
340	                current,
341	                target: props.target,
342	                // Guarantee forward progress: a zero interval would make every
343	                // tick a no-op (countdown never reaches zero, stopwatch never
344	                // advances) and keep the adapter interval alive forever.
345	                interval: effective_interval(props.interval),
346	                mode: props.mode,
347	                auto_start: props.auto_start,
348	                locale: env.locale.clone(),
349	                messages: messages.clone(),
350	                ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),
351	                intl_backend: Arc::clone(&env.intl_backend),
352	            },
353	        )
354	    }
355	
356	    fn on_props_changed(old: &Props, new: &Props) -> Vec<Event> {
357	        assert_eq!(
358	            old.id, new.id,
359	            "timer::Props.id must remain stable after init"
360	        );
361	
362	        if old.target != new.target || old.interval != new.interval || old.mode != new.mode {
363	            alloc::vec![Event::SyncProps]
364	        } else {
365	            Vec::new()
366	        }
367	    }
368	
369	    fn initial_effects(
370	        state: &Self::State,
371	        _ctx: &Self::Context,
372	        _props: &Self::Props,
373	    ) -> Vec<PendingEffect<Self>> {
374	        let mut effects = Vec::new();
375	
376	        // Auto-started timers boot directly into `Running` without a
377	        // transition, so the interval intent is emitted here on first mount.
378	        if matches!(state, State::Running) {
379	            effects.push(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval));

427	            }
428	
429	            (_, Event::Reset) => {
430	                let initial = initial_duration(ctx.mode, ctx.target);
431	                // A zero-duration countdown resets back to `Completed`, not
432	                // `Idle`, so it can never be (re)started into a running `00:00`.
433	                let target_state = if is_instantly_complete(ctx.mode, ctx.target) {
434	                    State::Completed
435	                } else {
436	                    State::Idle
437	                };
438	                Some(
439	                    TransitionPlan::to(target_state)
440	                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
441	                            ctx.current = initial;
442	                        })
443	                        .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval),
444	                )
445	            }
446	
447	            (_, Event::Restart) => {
448	                let initial = initial_duration(ctx.mode, ctx.target);
449	
450	                // Restarting a zero-duration countdown completes immediately
451	                // rather than entering `Running`; otherwise reset and run.
452	                if is_instantly_complete(ctx.mode, ctx.target) {
453	                    Some(
454	                        TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)
455	                            .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
456	                                ctx.current = initial;
457	                            })
458	                            .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval),
459	                    )
460	                } else {
461	                    Some(
462	                        TransitionPlan::to(State::Running)
463	                            .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
464	                                ctx.current = initial;
465	                            })
466	                            .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)
467	                            .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::TimerInterval)),
468	                    )
469	                }
470	            }
471	
472	            (_, Event::SetTime(duration)) => {
473	                let duration = *duration;
474	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
475	                    ctx.current = duration;
476	                }))
477	            }
478	
479	            (_, Event::SyncProps) => {
480	                let target = props.target;
481	                let interval = effective_interval(props.interval);
482	                let mode = props.mode;
483	
484	                // Syncing into a zero-duration countdown completes on arrival,
485	                // matching init/reset/restart, so it is never left idle and
486	                // startable into a running `00:00`.
487	                if is_instantly_complete(mode, target) {
488	                    Some(
489	                        TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)
490	                            .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
491	                                ctx.target = target;
492	                                ctx.interval = interval;
493	                                ctx.mode = mode;
