495	                            })
496	                            .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval),
497	                    )

499	                    // Reconfiguring a completed timer to a now-runnable config
500	                    // must leave `Completed`, otherwise the timer renders
501	                    // `data-ars-state="completed"` with a disabled start trigger
502	                    // (or strands a stopwatch in `Completed`) until manual reset.
503	                    let was_completed = matches!(state, State::Completed);
504	                    // Idle and (newly-runnable) Completed both mirror the new
505	                    // initial; running/paused keep the in-progress `current`.
506	                    let reset_current = matches!(state, State::Idle | State::Completed);
507	                    // A live cadence change must re-arm the adapter interval,
508	                    // otherwise it keeps firing at the old rate while ticks use
509	                    // the new interval.
510	                    let rearm = matches!(state, State::Running) && interval != ctx.interval;
511	
512	                    let apply = move |ctx: &mut Context| {
513	                        ctx.target = target;
514	                        ctx.interval = interval;
515	                        ctx.mode = mode;
516	                        if reset_current {
517	                            ctx.current = initial_duration(mode, target);
518	                        }
519	                    };
520	
521	                    let mut plan = if was_completed {
522	                        TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)
523	                            .apply(apply)
524	                            .cancel_effect(Effect::TimerInterval)
525	                    } else {
526	                        TransitionPlan::context_only(apply)
527	                    };
528	
529	                    if rearm {
530	                        plan = plan

532	                            .with_effect(interval_effect());
533	                    }
534	
535	                    Some(plan)
536	                }
537	            }

> AGENT

I now have everything needed. One decision genuinely deviates from the issue's stated "No spec change required" and is worth confirming before I finalize the plan.

The spec's §1.5 `Machine` impl as written **cannot compile** against the current `ars-core` engine:
- It omits the now-required `type Effect` associated type.
- It calls `use_platform_effects()` inside agnostic effect closures, but that hook lives only in the adapters — agnostic core can't call it.

Every shipped sibling component (timer, clipboard, color-*) handles this by declaring a typed `Effect` enum and emitting **marker** effects (`PendingEffect::named(Effect::X)`) that the adapter interprets to do the real platform work (announce, pointer-drag tracking). The spec predates that convention.

  "content": "# Task #295 — Implement SignaturePad agnostic core\n\n## Context\n\nGitHub issue #295 (Epic #227, Agnostic specialized components) asks for the\nframework-agnostic core of `SignaturePad`: a stateful canvas-based signature\ncapture machine (`Idle`/`Drawing`/`Completed`), stroke data ownership,\npoint/pressure normalization, validation, export, and ARIA/data attrs. Live\ncanvas access, pointer capture, DPR scaling, and raster rasterization belong to\nadapters (out of scope — #295 is core-only, Test tier: Unit).\n\nThe authoritative contract is `spec/components/specialized/signature-pad.md`.\nDuring exploration two pieces of spec §1.5 were found to be **drift that cannot\ncompile** against the current `ars-core` engine, so they must be corrected in\nthis PR (per the mandatory Spec Synchronization rule):\n\n1. The `Machine` impl omits the now-required `type Effect` associated type.\n2. It calls the adapter-only hook `use_platform_effects()` inside agnostic\n   effect closures (announce / pointer-drag), which the agnostic crate cannot do.\n\nResolution (user-confirmed): follow the established sibling convention used by\n`timer`, `clipboard`, and the color components — declare a typed `Effect` enum\nand emit **marker** effects (`PendingEffect::named(Effect::X)`). The adapter (a\nlater task) interprets each marker to perform the real platform work. The spec\n§1.5 is updated to match.\n\n## Reference implementations (match these exactly)\n\n- `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/timer/mod.rs` — the closest analog:\n  typed `Effect` enum with adapter-fulfilled markers (`TimerInterval`,\n  `AnnounceCompleted`), `Box::leak`-based test `Api` builders, `snapshot_attrs`\n  helper, `effect_names(result)` helper reading `SendResult::pending_effects`.\n- `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/clipboard/mod.rs` — Props builder,\n  `on_props_changed` id-stability assert, `ConnectApi` dispatch, snapshot tests.\n\n## Verified ars-core APIs (do not guess)\n\n- `ars_core::Machine` (crates/ars-core/src/lib.rs:751) requires associated types\n  `State, Event, Context, Props, Messages, Effect: EffectName, Api<'a>`.\n- `TransitionPlan::{to, context_only, apply, with_effect, with_named_effect, cancel_effect}`.\n- `PendingEffect::named(M::Effect)` (marker) and `PendingEffect::new(name, |ctx,props,WeakSend| -> CleanupFn)`.\n- `Bindable::{controlled, uncontrolled, get, set}` (no `is_empty`; call `SignatureData::is_empty` via `data.get()`).\n- `ComponentIds::{from_id, id, part}`; `MessageFn::static_str`; called via `(self.ctx.messages.x)(&locale)`.\n- `AttrMap::{set, set_bool}` return `&mut Self`. `HtmlAttr`/`AriaAttr` variants in the spec all exist.\n- `SendResult<M>.pending_effects: Vec<PendingEffect<M>>`, `.cancelled_effects`.\n\n## Files to create / modify\n\n### 1. New: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs`\n\nImplement, in this order (matching spec §-by-§, all public items `///`-doc'd):\n\n- **Data model** (verbatim from spec lines 19–123): `SignaturePoint`,\n  `SignatureStroke`, `SignatureData` (+ `is_empty`, `to_svg_path`,\n  `point_count`, `export`), `SignatureFormat`, `SignatureExport`. Keep\n  `export`'s `unimplemented!` arm for raster formats (adapter-side) — note: this\n  is a documented escape hatch, not deferral.\n- **`State`** enum: `Idle, Drawing, Completed` (spec §1.1).\n- **`Event`** enum: `DrawStart{x,y,pressure}`, `DrawMove{x,y,pressure}`,\n  `DrawEnd, Undo, Clear, Focus, Blur` (spec §1.2).\n- **`Context`** (spec §1.3) — all 11 fields. Derives `Clone, Debug, PartialEq`\n  (no trait-object field, so derive works, unlike timer).\n- **`Props`** (spec §1.4, `#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, HasId)]`) + `Default`\n  + a builder (`new()` + `id/data/default_data/disabled/readonly/pen_color/\n  pen_width/min_distance/name` setters), matching clipboard/timer builder style.\n- **`Messages`** (spec §4.1) — 6 `MessageFn` fields + `Default` + `impl ComponentMessages`.\n- **`Effect`** enum (NEW, replaces spec's string names) —\n  `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]`:\n  - `DrawingListeners` — adapter attaches global pointer-drag listeners\n    (`track_pointer_drag`) that dispatch `Event::DrawMove`/`Event::DrawEnd`.\n  - `AnnounceProvided` — adapter announces `messages.signature_provided` (polite live region).\n  - `AnnounceCleared` — adapter announces `messages.signature_cleared`.\n  Doc each with the adapter contract, mirroring timer's `Effect` doc style.\n- **`Machine`** impl:\n  - `type Effect = Effect;` (the fix).\n  - `init` — verbatim spec logic (controlled/uncontrolled `data`, `Idle` vs\n    `Completed` by emptiness, `ComponentIds::from_id`, locale/messages clone).\n  - `transition` — spec §1.5 logic, but effects become typed markers:\n    - `DrawStart` (from `Idle|Completed`) → `to(Drawing)` + `apply` (seed\n      `current_stroke` with first point) + `with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::DrawingListeners))`.\n    - `DrawMove` (in `Drawing`) → `context_only`, push point only if distance ≥ `min_distance`.\n    - `DrawEnd` (in `Drawing`) → `to(Completed)` + `apply` (commit stroke if ≥2\n      points) + `PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceProvided)`.\n    - `Undo` (in `Completed`) → `context_only`, pop last stroke.\n    - `Clear` (any) → `to(Idle)` + `apply` (reset data + `current_stroke`) +\n      `PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCleared)`.\n    - `Focus`/`Blur` pass through before the disabled/readonly gate (set `focused`).\n    - disabled/readonly gate returns `None` for all other events.\n  - `on_props_changed` — assert `id` stable (clipboard/timer convention); no\n    other sync events needed (Props are init-time config here).\n  - `connect<'a>` — return `Api`.\n- **`Part`** enum `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = \"signature-pad\"]`:\n  `Root, Canvas, ClearTrigger, UndoTrigger, Label, Guide, HiddenInput` (spec §1.6).\n- **`Api<'a>`** + `impl Debug` + query methods (`is_drawing`, `is_empty`,\n  `data`), per-part `*_attrs()` (verbatim spec §1.6 attr logic), `guide_text()`,\n  event dispatchers (`on_canvas_pointer_down`, `on_clear`, `on_undo`), and\n  `impl ConnectApi` dispatch over `Part`.\n- **`#[cfg(test)] mod tests`** (inline, bottom of file) — see Testing below.\n\n### 2. Wire module: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/mod.rs`\nAdd (alphabetical, with `///` doc): `pub mod signature_pad;` between `qr_code`\nand `timer`.\n\n### 3. Spec sync: `spec/components/specialized/signature-pad.md` §1.5\n- Add the `Effect` enum definition + doc.\n- Add `type Effect = Effect;` to the `Machine` impl.\n- Replace `.with_named_effect(\"drawing-listeners\", |..| { use_platform_effects()… })`\n  and the two announce closures with the typed marker form\n  (`.with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::…))`), and add prose noting the\n  adapter fulfills these markers (track_pointer_drag / announce). Fix the\n  `connect` signature to `fn connect<'a>(…)`.\n\n### 4. Spec conformance: `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/specialized.rs`\nAdd `signature_pad_anatomy_matches_spec` using `assert_anatomy(\"signature-pad\", &[…])`\nlisting all 7 parts with their kebab tokens (`root, canvas, clear-trigger,\nundo-trigger, label, guide, hidden-input`), matching existing entries' style.\n\n### 5. Proptest: `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/specialized.rs`\nAdd a `signature_pad_proptests` module (mirror `color_area_proptests`):\n`arb_event()` over the 7 events with bounded coords/pressure; feed random event\nsequences through `Service`; assert invariants — `current_stroke.is_some()` iff\n`Drawing`; `data` strokes each have ≥2 points; `to_svg_path`/`point_count` never\npanic; state ∈ valid set. `#[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]`\nwith `#![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]`.\n\n## Testing (TDD — write first), per issue \"Tests to add first\"\n\nInline unit + snapshot tests in `mod.rs` using `insta::assert_snapshot`, the\n`Box::leak` `api_for(...)` helper, `snapshot_attrs`, and `effect_names(result)`\n(all copied from timer's test module):\n\n- State machine: `Idle → Drawing` on `DrawStart`; `Drawing → Idle` is via\n  `Clear`; `Drawing → Completed` on `DrawEnd`; `DrawStart` emits\n  `Effect::DrawingListeners`.\n- `Clear` resets to `Idle`, empties data, clears `current_stroke`, emits `AnnounceCleared`.\n- Stroke capture: points accumulate as arrays; `min_distance` culling drops\n  near points; <2-point strokes are discarded on `DrawEnd`.\n- `Undo` pops last stroke (only in `Completed`).\n- Export: `export(Svg)` == `to_svg_path()`; `export(Points)` returns per-stroke\n  vecs; `to_svg_path` format (`M..,.. L..,..`); `point_count`.\n- Pen color/width: `init` mirrors props into context; defaults `#000000` / `2.0` / `3.0`.\n- ConnectApi: canvas `role=\"application\"` (interactive) vs `\"img\"`\n  (disabled/readonly), `aria-label`, `aria-labelledby`, `tabindex=0`,\n  `ars-touch-none`, focus-visible.\n- Guide: `data-ars-hidden` when non-empty; `guide_text()`.\n- Clear/Undo triggers: disabled when empty/disabled/readonly; `aria-label`s.\n- HiddenInput: `name`, SVG-path `value`.\n- `data-ars-state` = idle/drawing/completed; `data-ars-disabled/readonly` bool attrs.\n- Focus/Blur update `focused` even when disabled/readonly.\n- Snapshot tests: one per part × each output-affecting branch (idle/drawing/\n  completed, disabled, readonly, empty/non-empty) with committed `.snap` fixtures.\n\n## Mandatory post-steps (per CLAUDE.md)\n\n- Run the `post-implementation-audit` skill (all 3 phases); land every finding here.\n- `cargo insta test --unreferenced=reject` clean; attach `snapshot-reviewed`\n  label to the PR (new `.snap` files).\n- Present for user review before any commit.\n- After approval: `cargo xci-fast`, then commit + PR targeting `main` with\n  `Closes #295`, then run `waiting-for-codex-review`.\n\n## Verification\n\n```bash\n# focused build + tests for the new component\ncargo test -p ars-components signature_pad\n# anatomy conformance\ncargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance signature_pad\n# snapshot integrity\ncargo insta test -p ars-components --unreferenced=reject\n# zero-warning gate + native coverage (pre-push)\ncargo xci-fast\n# spec frontmatter still validates\ncargo xtask spec validate\n```\n"
}

## Approved Plan (edited by user):
# Task #295 — Implement SignaturePad agnostic core

## Context

GitHub issue #295 (Epic #227, Agnostic specialized components) asks for the
framework-agnostic core of `SignaturePad`: a stateful canvas-based signature
capture machine (`Idle`/`Drawing`/`Completed`), stroke data ownership,
point/pressure normalization, validation, export, and ARIA/data attrs. Live
canvas access, pointer capture, DPR scaling, and raster rasterization belong to
adapters (out of scope — #295 is core-only, Test tier: Unit).

The authoritative contract is `spec/components/specialized/signature-pad.md`.
During exploration two pieces of spec §1.5 were found to be **drift that cannot
compile** against the current `ars-core` engine, so they must be corrected in
this PR (per the mandatory Spec Synchronization rule):

1. The `Machine` impl omits the now-required `type Effect` associated type.
2. It calls the adapter-only hook `use_platform_effects()` inside agnostic
   effect closures (announce / pointer-drag), which the agnostic crate cannot do.

Resolution (user-confirmed): follow the established sibling convention used by
`timer`, `clipboard`, and the color components — declare a typed `Effect` enum
and emit **marker** effects (`PendingEffect::named(Effect::X)`). The adapter (a
later task) interprets each marker to perform the real platform work. The spec
§1.5 is updated to match.

## Reference implementations (match these exactly)

- `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/timer/mod.rs` — the closest analog:
  typed `Effect` enum with adapter-fulfilled markers (`TimerInterval`,
  `AnnounceCompleted`), `Box::leak`-based test `Api` builders, `snapshot_attrs`
  helper, `effect_names(result)` helper reading `SendResult::pending_effects`.
- `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/clipboard/mod.rs` — Props builder,
  `on_props_changed` id-stability assert, `ConnectApi` dispatch, snapshot tests.

## Verified ars-core APIs (do not guess)

- `ars_core::Machine` (crates/ars-core/src/lib.rs:751) requires associated types
  `State, Event, Context, Props, Messages, Effect: EffectName, Api<'a>`.
- `TransitionPlan::{to, context_only, apply, with_effect, with_named_effect, cancel_effect}`.
- `PendingEffect::named(M::Effect)` (marker) and `PendingEffect::new(name, |ctx,props,WeakSend| -> CleanupFn)`.
- `Bindable::{controlled, uncontrolled, get, set}` (no `is_empty`; call `SignatureData::is_empty` via `data.get()`).
- `ComponentIds::{from_id, id, part}`; `MessageFn::static_str`; called via `(self.ctx.messages.x)(&locale)`.
- `AttrMap::{set, set_bool}` return `&mut Self`. `HtmlAttr`/`AriaAttr` variants in the spec all exist.
- `SendResult<M>.pending_effects: Vec<PendingEffect<M>>`, `.cancelled_effects`.

## Files to create / modify

### 1. New: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs`

Implement, in this order (matching spec §-by-§, all public items `///`-doc'd):

- **Data model** (verbatim from spec lines 19–123): `SignaturePoint`,
  `SignatureStroke`, `SignatureData` (+ `is_empty`, `to_svg_path`,
  `point_count`, `export`), `SignatureFormat`, `SignatureExport`. Keep
  `export`'s `unimplemented!` arm for raster formats (adapter-side) — note: this
  is a documented escape hatch, not deferral.
- **`State`** enum: `Idle, Drawing, Completed` (spec §1.1).
- **`Event`** enum: `DrawStart{x,y,pressure}`, `DrawMove{x,y,pressure}`,
  `DrawEnd, Undo, Clear, Focus, Blur` (spec §1.2).
- **`Context`** (spec §1.3) — all 11 fields. Derives `Clone, Debug, PartialEq`
  (no trait-object field, so derive works, unlike timer).
- **`Props`** (spec §1.4, `#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, HasId)]`) + `Default`
  + a builder (`new()` + `id/data/default_data/disabled/readonly/pen_color/
  pen_width/min_distance/name` setters), matching clipboard/timer builder style.
- **`Messages`** (spec §4.1) — 6 `MessageFn` fields + `Default` + `impl ComponentMessages`.
- **`Effect`** enum (NEW, replaces spec's string names) —
  `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]`:
  - `DrawingListeners` — adapter attaches global pointer-drag listeners
    (`track_pointer_drag`) that dispatch `Event::DrawMove`/`Event::DrawEnd`.
  - `AnnounceProvided` — adapter announces `messages.signature_provided` (polite live region).
  - `AnnounceCleared` — adapter announces `messages.signature_cleared`.
  Doc each with the adapter contract, mirroring timer's `Effect` doc style.
- **`Machine`** impl:
  - `type Effect = Effect;` (the fix).
  - `init` — verbatim spec logic (controlled/uncontrolled `data`, `Idle` vs
    `Completed` by emptiness, `ComponentIds::from_id`, locale/messages clone).
  - `transition` — spec §1.5 logic, but effects become typed markers:
    - `DrawStart` (from `Idle|Completed`) → `to(Drawing)` + `apply` (seed
      `current_stroke` with first point) + `with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::DrawingListeners))`.
    - `DrawMove` (in `Drawing`) → `context_only`, push point only if distance ≥ `min_distance`.
    - `DrawEnd` (in `Drawing`) → `to(Completed)` + `apply` (commit stroke if ≥2
      points) + `PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceProvided)`.
    - `Undo` (in `Completed`) → `context_only`, pop last stroke.
    - `Clear` (any) → `to(Idle)` + `apply` (reset data + `current_stroke`) +
      `PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCleared)`.
    - `Focus`/`Blur` pass through before the disabled/readonly gate (set `focused`).
    - disabled/readonly gate returns `None` for all other events.
  - `on_props_changed` — assert `id` stable (clipboard/timer convention); no
    other sync events needed (Props are init-time config here).
  - `connect<'a>` — return `Api`.
- **`Part`** enum `#[derive(ComponentPart)] #[scope = "signature-pad"]`:
  `Root, Canvas, ClearTrigger, UndoTrigger, Label, Guide, HiddenInput` (spec §1.6).
- **`Api<'a>`** + `impl Debug` + query methods (`is_drawing`, `is_empty`,
  `data`), per-part `*_attrs()` (verbatim spec §1.6 attr logic), `guide_text()`,
  event dispatchers (`on_canvas_pointer_down`, `on_clear`, `on_undo`), and
  `impl ConnectApi` dispatch over `Part`.
- **`#[cfg(test)] mod tests`** (inline, bottom of file) — see Testing below.

### 2. Wire module: `crates/ars-components/src/specialized/mod.rs`
Add (alphabetical, with `///` doc): `pub mod signature_pad;` between `qr_code`
and `timer`.

### 3. Spec sync: `spec/components/specialized/signature-pad.md` §1.5
- Add the `Effect` enum definition + doc.
- Add `type Effect = Effect;` to the `Machine` impl.
- Replace `.with_named_effect("drawing-listeners", |..| { use_platform_effects()… })`
  and the two announce closures with the typed marker form
  (`.with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::…))`), and add prose noting the
  adapter fulfills these markers (track_pointer_drag / announce). Fix the
  `connect` signature to `fn connect<'a>(…)`.

### 4. Spec conformance: `crates/ars-components/tests/spec_conformance/specialized.rs`
Add `signature_pad_anatomy_matches_spec` using `assert_anatomy("signature-pad", &[…])`
listing all 7 parts with their kebab tokens (`root, canvas, clear-trigger,
undo-trigger, label, guide, hidden-input`), matching existing entries' style.

### 5. Proptest: `crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/specialized.rs`
Add a `signature_pad_proptests` module (mirror `color_area_proptests`):
`arb_event()` over the 7 events with bounded coords/pressure; feed random event
sequences through `Service`; assert invariants — `current_stroke.is_some()` iff
`Drawing`; `data` strokes each have ≥2 points; `to_svg_path`/`point_count` never
panic; state ∈ valid set. `#[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]`
with `#![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]`.

## Testing (TDD — write first), per issue "Tests to add first"

Inline unit + snapshot tests in `mod.rs` using `insta::assert_snapshot`, the
`Box::leak` `api_for(...)` helper, `snapshot_attrs`, and `effect_names(result)`
(all copied from timer's test module):

- State machine: `Idle → Drawing` on `DrawStart`; `Drawing → Idle` is via
  `Clear`; `Drawing → Completed` on `DrawEnd`; `DrawStart` emits
  `Effect::DrawingListeners`.
- `Clear` resets to `Idle`, empties data, clears `current_stroke`, emits `AnnounceCleared`.
- Stroke capture: points accumulate as arrays; `min_distance` culling drops
  near points; <2-point strokes are discarded on `DrawEnd`.
- `Undo` pops last stroke (only in `Completed`).
- Export: `export(Svg)` == `to_svg_path()`; `export(Points)` returns per-stroke
  vecs; `to_svg_path` format (`M..,.. L..,..`); `point_count`.
- Pen color/width: `init` mirrors props into context; defaults `#000000` / `2.0` / `3.0`.
- ConnectApi: canvas `role="application"` (interactive) vs `"img"`
  (disabled/readonly), `aria-label`, `aria-labelledby`, `tabindex=0`,
  `ars-touch-none`, focus-visible.
- Guide: `data-ars-hidden` when non-empty; `guide_text()`.
- Clear/Undo triggers: disabled when empty/disabled/readonly; `aria-label`s.
- HiddenInput: `name`, SVG-path `value`.
- `data-ars-state` = idle/drawing/completed; `data-ars-disabled/readonly` bool attrs.
- Focus/Blur update `focused` even when disabled/readonly.
- Snapshot tests: one per part × each output-affecting branch (idle/drawing/
  completed, disabled, readonly, empty/non-empty) with committed `.snap` fixtures.

## Mandatory post-steps (per CLAUDE.md)

- Run the `post-implementation-audit` skill (all 3 phases); land every finding here.
- `cargo insta test --unreferenced=reject` clean; attach `snapshot-reviewed`
  label to the PR (new `.snap` files).
- Present for user review before any commit.
- After approval: `cargo xci-fast`, then commit + PR targeting `main` with
  `Closes #295`, then run `waiting-for-codex-review`.

## Verification

```bash
# focused build + tests for the new component
cargo test -p ars-components signature_pad
# anatomy conformance
cargo test -p ars-components --test spec_conformance signature_pad
# snapshot integrity
cargo insta test -p ars-components --unreferenced=reject
# zero-warning gate + native coverage (pre-push)
cargo xci-fast
# spec frontmatter still validates
cargo xtask spec validate
```

12	/// Why a clipboard copy operation failed.
13	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
14	#[non_exhaustive]
15	pub enum CopyFailureReason {
16	    /// User denied clipboard access.
17	    PermissionDenied,
18	
19	    /// Not HTTPS; the Clipboard API requires a secure context.
20	    NotSecureContext,
21	
22	    /// Clipboard operation exceeded the platform timeout.
23	    Timeout,
24	
25	    /// Neither `navigator.clipboard` nor a fallback copy API is available.
26	    ApiUnavailable,
27	
28	    /// Unexpected error from the browser API.
29	    Unknown(String),
30	}
31	
32	/// The state of the `Clipboard` component.
33	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
34	pub enum State {
35	    /// Waiting for user copy intent.
36	    Idle,
37	
38	    /// A copy operation has been requested and is awaiting adapter completion.
39	    Copying,
40	
41	    /// Copy succeeded and success feedback is visible.
42	    Copied,
43	
44	    /// Copy failed and error feedback is visible.
45	    Error,
46	}
47	
48	impl Display for State {
49	    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
50	        f.write_str(match self {
51	            Self::Idle => "idle",
52	            Self::Copying => "copying",
53	            Self::Copied => "copied",
54	            Self::Error => "error",
55	        })
56	    }
57	}
58	
59	/// Events for the `Clipboard` component.

388	/// DOM parts of the `Clipboard` component.
389	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
390	#[scope = "clipboard"]
391	pub enum Part {
392	    /// Root wrapper element.
393	    Root,
394	
395	    /// Label describing what will be copied.
396	    Label,
397	
398	    /// Button that initiates copy.
399	    Trigger,
400	
401	    /// Decorative visual state indicator.
402	    Indicator,
403	
404	    /// Live region for copy feedback.
405	    Status,
406	
407	    /// Text display for the value being copied.
408	    ValueText,
409	}
410	
411	/// API for the `Clipboard` component.
412	pub struct Api<'a> {
413	    state: &'a State,
414	    ctx: &'a Context,
415	    props: &'a Props,
416	    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
417	}
418	
419	impl Debug for Api<'_> {
420	    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
421	        f.debug_struct("clipboard::Api")
422	            .field("state", self.state)
423	            .field("ctx", self.ctx)
424	            .field("props", self.props)
425	            .finish_non_exhaustive()
426	    }
427	}
428	
429	impl Api<'_> {
430	    /// Whether copy succeeded and success feedback is visible.
431	    #[must_use]
432	    pub const fn is_copied(&self) -> bool {
433	        matches!(self.state, State::Copied)
434	    }
435	
436	    /// Whether a copy operation is in progress.
437	    #[must_use]
438	    pub const fn is_copying(&self) -> bool {
439	        matches!(self.state, State::Copying)
440	    }
441	
442	    /// Whether copy failed and error feedback is visible.
443	    #[must_use]
444	    pub const fn is_error(&self) -> bool {
445	        matches!(self.state, State::Error)
446	    }
447	
448	    /// Returns the current copy error reason, if any.
449	    #[must_use]
450	    pub const fn error(&self) -> Option<&CopyFailureReason> {
451	        self.ctx.error.as_ref()
452	    }
453	
454	    /// Returns the current text to copy.
455	    #[must_use]
456	    pub fn value(&self) -> &str {
457	        self.ctx.value.get()
458	    }
459	
460	    /// Dispatches copy intent.
461	    pub fn copy(&self) {
462	        (self.send)(Event::Copy);
463	    }

465	    /// Root element attributes.
466	    #[must_use]
467	    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
468	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
469	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();
470	
471	        attrs
472	            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.id())
473	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
474	            .set(part_attr, part_val)
475	            .set(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-state"), self.state_token());
476	
477	        if self.ctx.disabled {

481	        attrs

483

489	
490	        attrs
491	            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part("label"))
492	            .set(HtmlAttr::For, self.ctx.ids.part("trigger"))
493	            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)
494	            .set(part_attr, part_val);

569	
570	    const fn state_token(&self) -> &'static str {
571	        match self.state {
572	            State::Idle => "idle",
573	            State::Copying => "copying",
574	            State::Copied => "copied",
575	            State::Error => "error",
576	        }
577	    }
578	
579	    fn trigger_label(&self) -> String {
580	        match self.state {
581	            State::Idle => (self.ctx.messages.trigger_label)(&self.ctx.locale),
582	            State::Copying => (self.ctx.messages.copying_label)(&self.ctx.locale),
583	            State::Copied => (self.ctx.messages.copied_label)(&self.ctx.locale),
584	            State::Error => (self.ctx.messages.error_label)(&self.ctx.locale),
585	        }
586	    }
587	}
588	
589	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
590	    type Part = Part;
591	
592	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
593	        match part {
594	            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),
595	            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),
596	            Part::Trigger => self.trigger_attrs(),
597	            Part::Indicator => self.indicator_attrs(),
598	            Part::Status => self.status_attrs(),
599	            Part::ValueText => self.value_text_attrs(),
600	        }
601	    }
602	}
603	
604	fn copying_plan() -> TransitionPlan<Machine> {
605	    TransitionPlan::to(State::Copying)
606	        .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
607	            ctx.error = None;
608	        })
609	        .with_effect(write_text_effect())
610	}
611	
612	fn write_text_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {
613	    PendingEffect::new(Effect::WriteText, |ctx: &Context, props: &Props, send| {
614	        if let Some(on_copy) = &props.on_copy {
615	            on_copy((ctx.value.get().clone(), send));
616	        } else {
617	            send.call_if_alive(Event::CopyError(CopyFailureReason::ApiUnavailable));
618	        }
619	
620	        no_cleanup()
621	    })
622	}
