  "content": "//! `SignaturePad` component state machine and connect API.\n//!\n//! The `SignaturePad` captures freehand pointer/touch input as vector path data.\n//! It records strokes as point arrays (with pressure and timestamp), supports\n//! undo and clear, and exposes the accumulated [`SignatureData`] for form\n//! submission (as an SVG path) and export.\n//!\n//! The agnostic core owns the stroke data model, the drawing state machine,\n//! `min_distance` point culling, stroke validation, and the ARIA/`data-ars-*`\n//! attribute surface. It does **not** touch the live `<canvas>`: pointer\n//! capture, device-pixel-ratio scaling, bounding-rect measurement, and raster\n//! rasterization (PNG/JPEG) belong to the framework adapters.\n//!\n//! Like the other component machines, the core only emits typed [`Effect`]\n//! intents; the adapter fulfils them. [`Effect::DrawingListeners`] tells the\n//! adapter to attach global pointer-drag listeners (e.g. via\n//! `PlatformEffects::track_pointer_drag`) that dispatch [`Event::DrawMove`] and\n//! [`Event::DrawEnd`]; [`Effect::AnnounceProvided`] and\n//! [`Effect::AnnounceCleared`] tell the adapter to announce the corresponding\n//! message into a polite `aria-live` region.\n\nuse alloc::{\n    format,\n    string::{String, ToString},\n    vec,\n    vec::Vec,\n};\nuse core::fmt::{self, Debug};\n\nuse ars_core::{\n    AriaAttr, AttrMap, Bindable, ComponentIds, ComponentMessages, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Env,\n    HasId, HtmlAttr, Locale, MessageFn, PendingEffect, TransitionPlan,\n};\n\n/// A single point in a signature stroke.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct SignaturePoint {\n    /// The x coordinate of the point.\n    pub x: f64,\n    /// The y coordinate of the point.\n    pub y: f64,\n    /// Pressure from 0.0 to 1.0 (for pressure-sensitive input).\n    pub pressure: f64,\n    /// Timestamp in milliseconds.\n    pub timestamp: f64,\n}\n\n/// A continuous stroke (pen-down to pen-up).\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct SignatureStroke {\n    /// The points in the stroke.\n    pub points: Vec<SignaturePoint>,\n}\n\n/// The complete signature data.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default)]\npub struct SignatureData {\n    /// The strokes in the signature.\n    pub strokes: Vec<SignatureStroke>,\n}\n\nimpl SignatureData {\n    /// Check if the signature data is empty.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {\n        self.strokes.is_empty()\n    }\n\n    /// Convert to SVG path data string.\n    ///\n    /// Each stroke contributes a `moveto` to its first point followed by a\n    /// `lineto` to each subsequent point. Strokes are concatenated directly,\n    /// so the resulting path describes the whole signature as one `d` value.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn to_svg_path(&self) -> String {\n        let mut path = String::new();\n        for stroke in &self.strokes {\n            let Some(first) = stroke.points.first() else {\n                continue;\n            };\n            path.push_str(&format!(\"M{:.1},{:.1}\", first.x, first.y));\n            for point in &stroke.points[1..] {\n                path.push_str(&format!(\" L{:.1},{:.1}\", point.x, point.y));\n            }\n        }\n        path\n    }\n\n    /// Total number of points across all strokes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn point_count(&self) -> usize {\n        self.strokes.iter().map(|stroke| stroke.points.len()).sum()\n    }\n\n    /// Export the signature in the requested format.\n    ///\n    /// For [`SignatureFormat::Svg`] the core generates the markup from stroke\n    /// data; for [`SignatureFormat::Points`] the raw [`SignaturePoint`] vectors\n    /// are returned directly, enabling server-side vector reconstruction\n    /// regardless of display resolution.\n    ///\n    /// [`SignatureFormat::Png`], [`SignatureFormat::Jpeg`], and\n    /// [`SignatureFormat::Base64Png`] require an off-screen canvas and are\n    /// rendered by the adapter layer; calling `export` with those formats on the\n    /// agnostic core panics. Adapters render strokes onto a canvas and encode the\n    /// result rather than calling this method for raster output.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn export(&self, format: SignatureFormat) -> SignatureExport {\n        match format {\n            SignatureFormat::Svg => SignatureExport::Svg(self.to_svg_path()),\n            SignatureFormat::Points => SignatureExport::Points(\n                self.strokes\n                    .iter()\n                    .map(|stroke| stroke.points.clone())\n                    .collect(),\n            ),\n            // Png, Jpeg, Base64Png require adapter-side canvas rendering.\n            SignatureFormat::Png | SignatureFormat::Jpeg | SignatureFormat::Base64Png => {\n                unimplemented!(\"Raster export is handled by the adapter layer\")\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Export format for the signature.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum SignatureFormat {\n    /// PNG format.\n    Png,\n    /// SVG format.\n    Svg,\n    /// JPEG format.\n    Jpeg,\n    /// Base64-encoded PNG format.\n    Base64Png,\n    /// Raw point data.\n    Points,\n}\n\n/// Exported signature data in the requested format.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub enum SignatureExport {\n    /// PNG image bytes.\n    Png(Vec<u8>),\n    /// SVG markup string.\n    Svg(String),\n    /// JPEG image bytes.\n    Jpeg(Vec<u8>),\n    /// Base64-encoded PNG string (suitable for `data:` URIs).\n    Base64(String),\n    /// Raw point data for vector reconstruction.\n    /// Each inner `Vec` is one continuous stroke.\n    Points(Vec<Vec<SignaturePoint>>),\n}\n\n/// The states for the `SignaturePad` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub enum State {\n    /// No strokes, canvas is blank.\n    Idle,\n    /// The user is actively drawing (pointer/touch down).\n    Drawing,\n    /// At least one stroke has been completed.\n    Completed,\n}\n\n/// The events for the `SignaturePad` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub enum Event {\n    /// Pointer/touch down on the canvas.\n    DrawStart {\n        /// The x coordinate of the draw start.\n        x: f64,\n        /// The y coordinate of the draw start.\n        y: f64,\n        /// The pressure of the draw start.\n        pressure: f64,\n    },\n    /// Pointer/touch move while drawing.\n    DrawMove {\n        /// The x coordinate of the draw move.\n        x: f64,\n        /// The y coordinate of the draw move.\n        y: f64,\n        /// The pressure of the draw move.\n        pressure: f64,\n    },\n    /// Pointer/touch up, ending a stroke.\n    DrawEnd,\n    /// Undo the last stroke.\n    Undo,\n    /// Clear all strokes.\n    Clear,\n    /// Focus entered the canvas.\n    Focus,\n    /// Focus left the canvas.\n    Blur,\n}\n\n/// The context for the `SignaturePad` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct Context {\n    /// The accumulated signature data.\n    pub data: Bindable<SignatureData>,\n    /// The stroke currently being drawn (None if not drawing).\n    pub current_stroke: Option<SignatureStroke>,\n    /// Whether the pad is disabled.\n    pub disabled: bool,\n    /// Whether the pad is read-only (shows existing signature but cannot modify).\n    pub readonly: bool,\n    /// Stroke color (CSS color string).\n    pub pen_color: String,\n    /// Stroke width in pixels.\n    pub pen_width: f64,\n    /// Minimum distance between points to record (for performance).\n    pub min_distance: f64,\n    /// Whether the canvas has focus.\n    pub focused: bool,\n    /// Locale for internationalized messages.\n    pub locale: Locale,\n    /// Resolved translatable messages.\n    pub messages: Messages,\n    /// Component instance IDs.\n    pub ids: ComponentIds,\n}\n\n/// The props for the `SignaturePad` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, HasId)]\npub struct Props {\n    /// Component instance ID.\n    pub id: String,\n    /// Controlled signature data.\n    pub data: Option<SignatureData>,\n    /// Default data for uncontrolled mode.\n    pub default_data: SignatureData,\n    /// Disabled state.\n    pub disabled: bool,\n    /// Read-only state.\n    pub readonly: bool,\n    /// Pen color.\n    pub pen_color: String,\n    /// Pen width in pixels.\n    pub pen_width: f64,\n    /// Minimum distance between recorded points.\n    pub min_distance: f64,\n    /// Name for form submission.\n    pub name: Option<String>,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Props {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id: String::new(),\n            data: None,\n            default_data: SignatureData::default(),\n            disabled: false,\n            readonly: false,\n            pen_color: \"#000000\".into(),\n            pen_width: 2.0,\n            min_distance: 3.0,\n            name: None,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Props {\n    /// Returns a fresh [`Props`] with every field at its [`Default`] value.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self::default()\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`id`](Self::id).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn id(mut self, id: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        self.id = id.into();\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets the controlled [`data`](Self::data).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn data(mut self, data: SignatureData) -> Self {\n        self.data = Some(data);\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`default_data`](Self::default_data) for uncontrolled mode.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn default_data(mut self, default_data: SignatureData) -> Self {\n        self.default_data = default_data;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`disabled`](Self::disabled).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn disabled(mut self, disabled: bool) -> Self {\n        self.disabled = disabled;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`readonly`](Self::readonly).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn readonly(mut self, readonly: bool) -> Self {\n        self.readonly = readonly;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`pen_color`](Self::pen_color).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn pen_color(mut self, pen_color: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        self.pen_color = pen_color.into();\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`pen_width`](Self::pen_width).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn pen_width(mut self, pen_width: f64) -> Self {\n        self.pen_width = pen_width;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`min_distance`](Self::min_distance).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn min_distance(mut self, min_distance: f64) -> Self {\n        self.min_distance = min_distance;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Sets [`name`](Self::name) for form submission.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn name(mut self, name: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        self.name = Some(name.into());\n        self\n    }\n}\n\n/// The messages for the `SignaturePad` component.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]\npub struct Messages {\n    /// Accessible label for the canvas.\n    pub canvas_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n    /// Accessible label for the clear button.\n    pub clear_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n    /// Accessible label for the undo button.\n    pub undo_label: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n    /// Screen reader announcement when signature is provided.\n    pub signature_provided: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n    /// Screen reader announcement when signature is cleared.\n    pub signature_cleared: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n    /// Guide placeholder text.\n    pub guide_text: MessageFn<dyn Fn(&Locale) -> String + Send + Sync>,\n}\n\nimpl Default for Messages {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            canvas_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Signature pad\"),\n            clear_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Clear signature\"),\n            undo_label: MessageFn::static_str(\"Undo last stroke\"),\n            signature_provided: MessageFn::static_str(\"Signature provided\"),\n            signature_cleared: MessageFn::static_str(\"Signature cleared\"),\n            guide_text: MessageFn::static_str(\"Sign here\"),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ComponentMessages for Messages {}\n\n/// Typed effect intents emitted by the signature-pad machine.\n///\n/// The agnostic core never touches the live canvas or the screen reader; it\n/// emits these markers and the framework adapter performs the real work.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub enum Effect {\n    /// Adapter attaches global pointer-drag listeners (e.g. via\n    /// `PlatformEffects::track_pointer_drag`) that dispatch [`Event::DrawMove`]\n    /// while the pointer moves and [`Event::DrawEnd`] on pointer up, so a stroke\n    /// continues even when the pointer leaves the canvas. Emitted on the\n    /// transition into [`State::Drawing`].\n    DrawingListeners,\n\n    /// Adapter announces [`Messages::signature_provided`] into a polite\n    /// `aria-live` region. Emitted on the transition into [`State::Completed`]\n    /// (pointer up after a valid stroke).\n    AnnounceProvided,\n\n    /// Adapter announces [`Messages::signature_cleared`] into a polite\n    /// `aria-live` region. Emitted on the [`Event::Clear`] transition.\n    AnnounceCleared,\n}\n\n/// The machine for the `SignaturePad` component.\n///\n/// # Examples\n///\n/// Draw a single stroke and complete it. In a real app the adapter dispatches\n/// [`Event::DrawMove`]/[`Event::DrawEnd`] from the pointer-drag listeners that\n/// [`Effect::DrawingListeners`] sets up; here we send them directly:\n///\n/// ```\n/// use ars_components::specialized::signature_pad::{Event, Machine, Messages, Props, State};\n/// use ars_core::{Env, Service};\n///\n/// let mut pad = Service::<Machine>::new(\n///     Props::new().id(\"sig\").min_distance(0.0),\n///     &Env::default(),\n///     &Messages::default(),\n/// );\n/// assert_eq!(pad.state(), &State::Idle);\n///\n/// drop(pad.send(Event::DrawStart { x: 0.0, y: 0.0, pressure: 0.5 }));\n/// assert_eq!(pad.state(), &State::Drawing);\n///\n/// drop(pad.send(Event::DrawMove { x: 10.0, y: 10.0, pressure: 0.5 }));\n/// drop(pad.send(Event::DrawEnd));\n/// assert_eq!(pad.state(), &State::Completed);\n/// assert_eq!(pad.context().data.get().strokes.len(), 1);\n/// ```\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct Machine;\n\nimpl ars_core::Machine for Machine {\n    type State = State;\n    type Event = Event;\n    type Context = Context;\n    type Props = Props;\n    type Messages = Messages;\n    type Effect = Effect;\n    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;\n\n    fn init(props: &Self::Props, env: &Env, messages: &Self::Messages) -> (State, Context) {\n        let data = match &props.data {\n            Some(data) => Bindable::controlled(data.clone()),\n            None => Bindable::uncontrolled(props.default_data.clone()),\n        };\n\n        let state = if data.get().is_empty() {\n            State::Idle\n        } else {\n            State::Completed\n        };\n\n        (\n            state,\n            Context {\n                data,\n                current_stroke: None,\n                disabled: props.disabled,\n                readonly: props.readonly,\n                pen_color: props.pen_color.clone(),\n                pen_width: props.pen_width,\n                min_distance: props.min_distance,\n                focused: false,\n                locale: env.locale.clone(),\n                messages: messages.clone(),\n                ids: ComponentIds::from_id(&props.id),\n            },\n        )\n    }\n\n    fn on_props_changed(old: &Props, new: &Props) -> Vec<Event> {\n        assert_eq!(\n            old.id, new.id,\n            \"signature_pad::Props.id must remain stable after init\"\n        );\n\n        Vec::new()\n    }\n\n    fn transition(\n        state: &Self::State,\n        event: &Self::Event,\n        ctx: &Self::Context,\n        _props: &Self::Props,\n    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {\n        // Focus/Blur always pass through regardless of disabled/readonly.\n        match event {\n            Event::Focus => {\n                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.focused = true;\n                }));\n            }\n            Event::Blur => {\n                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.focused = false;\n                }));\n            }\n            _ => {}\n        }\n\n        if ctx.disabled || ctx.readonly {\n            return None;\n        }\n\n        match (state, event) {\n            // Start drawing: seed a new stroke and ask the adapter to wire up\n            // the global pointer-drag listeners that feed DrawMove/DrawEnd.\n            (State::Idle | State::Completed, Event::DrawStart { x, y, pressure }) => {\n                let point = SignaturePoint {\n                    x: *x,\n                    y: *y,\n                    pressure: *pressure,\n                    timestamp: 0.0,\n                };\n                Some(\n                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Drawing)\n                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                            ctx.current_stroke = Some(SignatureStroke {\n                                points: vec![point],\n                            });\n                        })\n                        .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::DrawingListeners)),\n                )\n            }\n\n            // Continue drawing: append the point only when it is at least\n            // `min_distance` away from the previous one (cull jitter).\n            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawMove { x, y, pressure }) => {\n                let point = SignaturePoint {\n                    x: *x,\n                    y: *y,\n                    pressure: *pressure,\n                    timestamp: 0.0,\n                };\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    let min_distance = ctx.min_distance;\n                    if let Some(stroke) = ctx.current_stroke.as_mut()\n                        && let Some(last) = stroke.points.last()\n                    {\n                        let dx = point.x - last.x;\n                        let dy = point.y - last.y;\n                        if (dx * dx + dy * dy).sqrt() >= min_distance {\n                            stroke.points.push(point);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }))\n            }\n\n            // End drawing: commit the in-flight stroke if it has enough points.\n            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawEnd) => Some(\n                TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)\n                    .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                        if let Some(stroke) = ctx.current_stroke.take()\n                            && stroke.points.len() >= 2\n                        {\n                            let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();\n                            data.strokes.push(stroke);\n                            ctx.data.set(data);\n                        }\n                    })\n                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceProvided)),\n            ),\n\n            // Undo the last completed stroke.\n            (State::Completed, Event::Undo) => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();\n                    data.strokes.pop();\n                    ctx.data.set(data);\n                }))\n            }\n\n            // Clear all strokes from any state.\n            (_, Event::Clear) => Some(\n                TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)\n                    .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                        ctx.data.set(SignatureData::default());\n                        ctx.current_stroke = None;\n                    })\n                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCleared)),\n            ),\n\n            _ => None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn connect<'a>(\n        state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// DOM parts of the `SignaturePad` component.\n#[derive(ComponentPart)]\n#[scope = \"signature-pad\"]\npub enum Part {\n    /// Root wrapper element.\n    Root,\n\n    /// The `<canvas>` drawing surface.\n    Canvas,\n\n    /// Button that clears all strokes.\n    ClearTrigger,\n\n    /// Button that undoes the last stroke.\n    UndoTrigger,\n\n    /// Label describing the signature area.\n    Label,\n\n    /// Guide line or placeholder text (hidden once strokes exist).\n    Guide,\n\n    /// Hidden input carrying the SVG path data for form submission.\n    HiddenInput,\n}\n\n/// API for the `SignaturePad` component.\npub struct Api<'a> {\n    state: &'a State,\n    ctx: &'a Context,\n    props: &'a Props,\n    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),\n}\n\nimpl Debug for Api<'_> {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        f.debug_struct(\"signature_pad::Api\")\n            .field(\"state\", self.state)\n            .field(\"ctx\", self.ctx)\n            .field(\"props\", self.props)\n            .finish_non_exhaustive()\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Api<'_> {\n    /// Whether the user is actively drawing a stroke.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub const fn is_drawing(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.state, State::Drawing)\n    }\n\n    /// Whether the signature is empty (no committed strokes).\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {\n        self.ctx.data.get().is_empty()\n    }\n\n    /// The accumulated signature data.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn data(&self) -> &SignatureData {\n        self.ctx.data.get()\n    }\n\n    /// Root element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn root_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Root.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.id())\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-state\"), self.state_token());\n\n        if self.ctx.disabled {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-disabled\"), true);\n        }\n        if self.ctx.readonly {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-readonly\"), true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Canvas element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn canvas_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Canvas.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part(\"canvas\"))\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::TabIndex, \"0\")\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Class, \"ars-touch-none\");\n\n        // `application` opts the canvas out of the screen-reader virtual cursor\n        // so it can capture raw pointer/touch input; a non-interactive pad is a\n        // static image instead.\n        if self.ctx.readonly || self.ctx.disabled {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"img\");\n        } else {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Role, \"application\");\n        }\n\n        attrs\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                (self.ctx.messages.canvas_label)(&self.ctx.locale),\n            )\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::LabelledBy),\n                self.ctx.ids.part(\"label\"),\n            );\n\n        if self.ctx.disabled {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Disabled), \"true\");\n        }\n        if self.ctx.readonly {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ReadOnly), \"true\");\n        }\n        if self.ctx.focused {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-focus-visible\"), true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Clear-trigger button attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn clear_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::ClearTrigger.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part(\"clear\"))\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"button\")\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                (self.ctx.messages.clear_label)(&self.ctx.locale),\n            );\n\n        if self.is_empty() || self.ctx.disabled || self.ctx.readonly {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Undo-trigger button attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn undo_trigger_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::UndoTrigger.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part(\"undo\"))\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"button\")\n            .set(\n                HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::Label),\n                (self.ctx.messages.undo_label)(&self.ctx.locale),\n            );\n\n        if self.is_empty() || self.ctx.disabled || self.ctx.readonly {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Disabled, true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Label element attributes.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn label_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Label.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Id, self.ctx.ids.part(\"label\"))\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val);\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Guide element attributes. The guide is hidden once strokes are present.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn guide_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Guide.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);\n\n        if !self.is_empty() {\n            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-hidden\"), true);\n        }\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the guide placeholder text (e.g. \"Sign here\").\n    ///\n    /// The adapter renders this inside the [`Part::Guide`] element.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn guide_text(&self) -> String {\n        (self.ctx.messages.guide_text)(&self.ctx.locale)\n    }\n\n    /// Hidden-input attributes. The value is the signature's SVG path data,\n    /// suitable for form submission.\n    #[must_use]\n    pub fn hidden_input_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {\n        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\n        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::HiddenInput.data_attrs();\n\n        attrs\n            .set(scope_attr, scope_val)\n            .set(part_attr, part_val)\n            .set(HtmlAttr::Type, \"hidden\");\n\n        if let Some(name) = &self.props.name {\n            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Name, name.clone());\n        }\n\n        attrs.set(HtmlAttr::Value, self.ctx.data.get().to_svg_path());\n\n        attrs\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatches pointer-down intent on the canvas, beginning a stroke.\n    pub fn on_canvas_pointer_down(&self, x: f64, y: f64, pressure: f64) {\n        (self.send)(Event::DrawStart { x, y, pressure });\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatches clear intent.\n    pub fn on_clear(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::Clear);\n    }\n\n    /// Dispatches undo intent.\n    pub fn on_undo(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::Undo);\n    }\n\n    const fn state_token(&self) -> &'static str {\n        match self.state {\n            State::Idle => \"idle\",\n            State::Drawing => \"drawing\",\n            State::Completed => \"completed\",\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {\n    type Part = Part;\n\n    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {\n        match part {\n            Part::Root => self.root_attrs(),\n            Part::Canvas => self.canvas_attrs(),\n            Part::ClearTrigger => self.clear_trigger_attrs(),\n            Part::UndoTrigger => self.undo_trigger_attrs(),\n            Part::Label => self.label_attrs(),\n            Part::Guide => self.guide_attrs(),\n            Part::HiddenInput => self.hidden_input_attrs(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use alloc::{boxed::Box, vec, vec::Vec};\n\n    use ars_core::{AttrMap, Env, Machine as _, Service};\n    use insta::assert_snapshot;\n\n    use super::*;\n\n    // ───────────────────────── helpers ─────────────────────────\n\n    fn point(x: f64, y: f64) -> SignaturePoint {\n        SignaturePoint {\n            x,\n            y,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n            timestamp: 0.0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn stroke(points: &[(f64, f64)]) -> SignatureStroke {\n        SignatureStroke {\n            points: points.iter().map(|&(x, y)| point(x, y)).collect(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Signature data with `n` two-point strokes.\n    fn data_with(n: usize) -> SignatureData {\n        SignatureData {\n            strokes: (0..n)\n                .map(|i| {\n                    let base = i as f64;\n                    stroke(&[(base, base), (base + 1.0, base + 1.0)])\n                })\n                .collect(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn test_props() -> Props {\n        Props::new().id(\"sig\")\n    }\n\n    fn fresh_service(props: Props) -> Service<Machine> {\n        Service::<Machine>::new(props, &Env::default(), &Messages::default())\n    }\n\n    fn snapshot_attrs(attrs: &AttrMap) -> String {\n        format!(\"{attrs:#?}\")\n    }\n\n    fn effect_names(result: &ars_core::SendResult<Machine>) -> Vec<Effect> {\n        result\n            .pending_effects\n            .iter()\n            .map(|effect| effect.name)\n            .collect()\n    }\n\n    /// Leaks a fully-formed [`Api`] for attribute/accessor assertions.\n    fn api_for(\n        state: State,\n        data: SignatureData,\n        disabled: bool,\n        readonly: bool,\n        focused: bool,\n    ) -> Api<'static> {\n        let props = Box::leak(Box::new(\n            Props::new()\n                .id(\"sig\")\n                .disabled(disabled)\n                .readonly(readonly)\n                .name(\"signature\"),\n        ));\n        let messages = Messages::default();\n        let (_, mut ctx) = Machine::init(props, &Env::default(), &messages);\n        ctx.data.set(data);\n        ctx.focused = focused;\n\n        let ctx = Box::leak(Box::new(ctx));\n        let state = Box::leak(Box::new(state));\n        let send = Box::leak(Box::new(|_: Event| {}));\n\n        Api {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn empty_api(state: State) -> Api<'static> {\n        api_for(state, SignatureData::default(), false, false, false)\n    }\n\n    fn filled_api(state: State) -> Api<'static> {\n        api_for(state, data_with(1), false, false, false)\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── data model ─────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn signature_data_default_is_empty() {\n        let data = SignatureData::default();\n        assert!(data.is_empty());\n        assert_eq!(data.point_count(), 0);\n        assert_eq!(data.to_svg_path(), \"\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn signature_data_to_svg_path_emits_moveto_lineto() {\n        let data = SignatureData {\n            strokes: vec![stroke(&[(0.0, 0.0), (1.5, 2.25), (3.0, 4.0)])],\n        };\n        assert_eq!(data.to_svg_path(), \"M0.0,0.0 L1.5,2.2 L3.0,4.0\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn signature_data_to_svg_path_concatenates_strokes() {\n        let data = SignatureData {\n            strokes: vec![stroke(&[(0.0, 0.0), (1.0, 1.0)]), stroke(&[(5.0, 5.0), (6.0, 6.0)])],\n        };\n        assert_eq!(data.to_svg_path(), \"M0.0,0.0 L1.0,1.0M5.0,5.0 L6.0,6.0\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn signature_data_to_svg_path_skips_empty_strokes() {\n        let data = SignatureData {\n            strokes: vec![\n                SignatureStroke { points: Vec::new() },\n                stroke(&[(2.0, 2.0), (3.0, 3.0)]),\n            ],\n        };\n        assert_eq!(data.to_svg_path(), \"M2.0,2.0 L3.0,3.0\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn signature_data_point_count_sums_all_strokes() {\n        let data = SignatureData {\n            strokes: vec![stroke(&[(0.0, 0.0), (1.0, 1.0)]), stroke(&[(2.0, 2.0)])],\n        };\n        assert_eq!(data.point_count(), 3);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn export_svg_matches_to_svg_path() {\n        let data = data_with(1);\n        assert_eq!(\n            data.export(SignatureFormat::Svg),\n            SignatureExport::Svg(data.to_svg_path())\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn export_points_returns_per_stroke_vectors() {\n        let data = data_with(2);\n        let SignatureExport::Points(strokes) = data.export(SignatureFormat::Points) else {\n            panic!(\"expected Points export\");\n        };\n        assert_eq!(strokes.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(strokes[0], data.strokes[0].points);\n        assert_eq!(strokes[1], data.strokes[1].points);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    #[should_panic(expected = \"Raster export is handled by the adapter layer\")]\n    fn export_png_is_adapter_side() {\n        let _ = data_with(1).export(SignatureFormat::Png);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    #[should_panic(expected = \"Raster export is handled by the adapter layer\")]\n    fn export_jpeg_is_adapter_side() {\n        let _ = data_with(1).export(SignatureFormat::Jpeg);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    #[should_panic(expected = \"Raster export is handled by the adapter layer\")]\n    fn export_base64_png_is_adapter_side() {\n        let _ = data_with(1).export(SignatureFormat::Base64Png);\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── props / init ─────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn props_default_matches_spec() {\n        let props = Props::default();\n        assert_eq!(props.id, \"\");\n        assert_eq!(props.data, None);\n        assert_eq!(props.default_data, SignatureData::default());\n        assert!(!props.disabled);\n        assert!(!props.readonly);\n        assert_eq!(props.pen_color, \"#000000\");\n        assert_eq!(props.pen_width, 2.0);\n        assert_eq!(props.min_distance, 3.0);\n        assert_eq!(props.name, None);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn props_builder_sets_expected_fields() {\n        let props = Props::new()\n            .id(\"sig\")\n            .default_data(data_with(1))\n            .disabled(true)\n            .readonly(true)\n            .pen_color(\"#ff0000\")\n            .pen_width(4.0)\n            .min_distance(1.0)\n            .name(\"autograph\");\n\n        assert_eq!(props.id, \"sig\");\n        assert_eq!(props.default_data, data_with(1));\n        assert!(props.disabled);\n        assert!(props.readonly);\n        assert_eq!(props.pen_color, \"#ff0000\");\n        assert_eq!(props.pen_width, 4.0);\n        assert_eq!(props.min_distance, 1.0);\n        assert_eq!(props.name.as_deref(), Some(\"autograph\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn init_empty_data_starts_idle_uncontrolled() {\n        let service = fresh_service(test_props());\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(service.context().data.get().is_empty());\n        assert!(!service.context().data.is_controlled());\n        assert_eq!(service.context().pen_color, \"#000000\");\n        assert_eq!(service.context().pen_width, 2.0);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().min_distance, 3.0);\n        assert!(!service.context().focused);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().ids.id(), \"sig\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn init_with_default_data_starts_completed() {\n        let service = fresh_service(test_props().default_data(data_with(1)));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().data.get().strokes.len(), 1);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn init_with_controlled_data_starts_completed_and_controlled() {\n        let service = fresh_service(test_props().data(data_with(2)));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert!(service.context().data.is_controlled());\n        assert_eq!(service.context().data.get().strokes.len(), 2);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn init_mirrors_pen_customization_into_context() {\n        let service = fresh_service(test_props().pen_color(\"#0000ff\").pen_width(5.0));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().pen_color, \"#0000ff\");\n        assert_eq!(service.context().pen_width, 5.0);\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── transitions ─────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn draw_start_enters_drawing_and_seeds_stroke() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n        let result = service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 1.0,\n            y: 2.0,\n            pressure: 0.7,\n        });\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Drawing);\n        let stroke = service\n            .context()\n            .current_stroke\n            .as_ref()\n            .expect(\"drawing seeds a current stroke\");\n        assert_eq!(stroke.points.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(stroke.points[0].x, 1.0);\n        assert_eq!(stroke.points[0].y, 2.0);\n        assert_eq!(stroke.points[0].pressure, 0.7);\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&result), vec![Effect::DrawingListeners]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn draw_move_appends_point_beyond_min_distance() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().min_distance(3.0));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawMove {\n            x: 10.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n\n        let stroke = service.context().current_stroke.as_ref().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(stroke.points.len(), 2);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn draw_move_culls_points_within_min_distance() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().min_distance(3.0));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        // 1px away: below the 3px threshold, so dropped.\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawMove {\n            x: 1.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n\n        let stroke = service.context().current_stroke.as_ref().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(stroke.points.len(), 1);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn draw_end_commits_multi_point_stroke_and_announces() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().min_distance(0.0));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawMove {\n            x: 5.0,\n            y: 5.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        let result = service.send(Event::DrawEnd);\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().data.get().strokes.len(), 1);\n        assert!(service.context().current_stroke.is_none());\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&result), vec![Effect::AnnounceProvided]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn draw_end_discards_single_point_stroke() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().min_distance(100.0));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        // Move is culled (within min_distance), leaving a 1-point stroke.\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawMove {\n            x: 1.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawEnd));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert!(service.context().data.get().is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn undo_pops_last_stroke_only_in_completed() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().default_data(data_with(2)));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        drop(service.send(Event::Undo));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().data.get().strokes.len(), 1);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn undo_ignored_when_idle() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n        let result = service.send(Event::Undo);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(result.pending_effects.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn clear_resets_to_idle_and_announces() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().default_data(data_with(2)));\n        let result = service.send(Event::Clear);\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(service.context().data.get().is_empty());\n        assert!(service.context().current_stroke.is_none());\n        assert_eq!(effect_names(&result), vec![Effect::AnnounceCleared]);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn clear_works_while_drawing() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Drawing);\n        drop(service.send(Event::Clear));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(service.context().current_stroke.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn focus_and_blur_update_focused_flag() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n        drop(service.send(Event::Focus));\n        assert!(service.context().focused);\n        drop(service.send(Event::Blur));\n        assert!(!service.context().focused);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn focus_passes_through_when_disabled() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().disabled(true));\n        drop(service.send(Event::Focus));\n        assert!(service.context().focused);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn disabled_pad_ignores_draw_start() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().disabled(true));\n        let result = service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        });\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(result.pending_effects.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn readonly_pad_ignores_draw_start() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().readonly(true));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── Api accessors ─────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn api_query_methods_reflect_state_and_data() {\n        let drawing = empty_api(State::Drawing);\n        assert!(drawing.is_drawing());\n        assert!(drawing.is_empty());\n\n        let completed = filled_api(State::Completed);\n        assert!(!completed.is_drawing());\n        assert!(!completed.is_empty());\n        assert_eq!(completed.data().strokes.len(), 1);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn api_guide_text_uses_messages() {\n        assert_eq!(empty_api(State::Idle).guide_text(), \"Sign here\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn api_event_dispatchers_send_events() {\n        use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\n\n        let captured: Arc<Mutex<Vec<Event>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));\n        let sink = Arc::clone(&captured);\n        let send: Box<dyn Fn(Event)> = Box::new(move |event| sink.lock().unwrap().push(event));\n        let send = Box::leak(send);\n\n        let props = Box::leak(Box::new(test_props()));\n        let messages = Messages::default();\n        let (state, ctx) = Machine::init(props, &Env::default(), &messages);\n        let ctx = Box::leak(Box::new(ctx));\n        let state = Box::leak(Box::new(state));\n        let api = Api {\n            state,\n            ctx,\n            props,\n            send,\n        };\n\n        api.on_canvas_pointer_down(3.0, 4.0, 0.9);\n        api.on_clear();\n        api.on_undo();\n\n        let events = captured.lock().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(\n            *events,\n            vec![\n                Event::DrawStart {\n                    x: 3.0,\n                    y: 4.0,\n                    pressure: 0.9\n                },\n                Event::Clear,\n                Event::Undo,\n            ]\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn canvas_role_is_application_when_interactive() {\n        let api = empty_api(State::Idle);\n        let attrs = api.canvas_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"application\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn canvas_role_is_img_when_readonly_or_disabled() {\n        let readonly = api_for(State::Completed, data_with(1), false, true, false);\n        assert_eq!(readonly.canvas_attrs().get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"img\"));\n\n        let disabled = api_for(State::Idle, SignatureData::default(), true, false, false);\n        assert_eq!(disabled.canvas_attrs().get(&HtmlAttr::Role), Some(\"img\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn clear_and_undo_triggers_disabled_when_empty() {\n        let api = empty_api(State::Idle);\n        assert!(api.clear_trigger_attrs().contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled));\n        assert!(api.undo_trigger_attrs().contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn clear_and_undo_triggers_enabled_when_filled() {\n        let api = filled_api(State::Completed);\n        assert!(!api.clear_trigger_attrs().contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled));\n        assert!(!api.undo_trigger_attrs().contains(&HtmlAttr::Disabled));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn guide_hidden_when_strokes_present() {\n        assert!(\n            !empty_api(State::Idle)\n                .guide_attrs()\n                .contains(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-hidden\"))\n        );\n        assert!(\n            filled_api(State::Completed)\n                .guide_attrs()\n                .contains(&HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-hidden\"))\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn hidden_input_carries_name_and_svg_path() {\n        let api = filled_api(State::Completed);\n        let attrs = api.hidden_input_attrs();\n        assert_eq!(attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Name), Some(\"signature\"));\n        assert_eq!(\n            attrs.get(&HtmlAttr::Value).map(str::to_string),\n            Some(data_with(1).to_svg_path())\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn connect_api_dispatch_matches_direct_attrs() {\n        let api = filled_api(State::Completed);\n        for part in [\n            Part::Root,\n            Part::Canvas,\n            Part::ClearTrigger,\n            Part::UndoTrigger,\n            Part::Label,\n            Part::Guide,\n            Part::HiddenInput,\n        ] {\n            let direct = match part {\n                Part::Root => api.root_attrs(),\n                Part::Canvas => api.canvas_attrs(),\n                Part::ClearTrigger => api.clear_trigger_attrs(),\n                Part::UndoTrigger => api.undo_trigger_attrs(),\n                Part::Label => api.label_attrs(),\n                Part::Guide => api.guide_attrs(),\n                Part::HiddenInput => api.hidden_input_attrs(),\n            };\n            assert_eq!(snapshot_attrs(&api.part_attrs(part)), snapshot_attrs(&direct));\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── snapshots ─────────────────────────\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_root_idle() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&empty_api(State::Idle).root_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_root_drawing() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&empty_api(State::Drawing).root_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_root_completed() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&filled_api(State::Completed).root_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_root_disabled() {\n        let api = api_for(State::Idle, SignatureData::default(), true, false, false);\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&api.root_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_root_readonly() {\n        let api = api_for(State::Completed, data_with(1), false, true, false);\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&api.root_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_canvas_interactive() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&empty_api(State::Idle).canvas_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_canvas_focused() {\n        let api = api_for(State::Idle, SignatureData::default(), false, false, true);\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&api.canvas_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_canvas_disabled() {\n        let api = api_for(State::Idle, SignatureData::default(), true, false, false);\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&api.canvas_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_canvas_readonly() {\n        let api = api_for(State::Completed, data_with(1), false, true, false);\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&api.canvas_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_clear_trigger_empty() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&empty_api(State::Idle).clear_trigger_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_clear_trigger_filled() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(\n            &filled_api(State::Completed).clear_trigger_attrs()\n        ));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_undo_trigger_empty() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&empty_api(State::Idle).undo_trigger_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_undo_trigger_filled() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(\n            &filled_api(State::Completed).undo_trigger_attrs()\n        ));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_label() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&empty_api(State::Idle).label_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_guide_visible() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&empty_api(State::Idle).guide_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_guide_hidden() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(&filled_api(State::Completed).guide_attrs()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn snapshot_hidden_input() {\n        assert_snapshot!(snapshot_attrs(\n            &filled_api(State::Completed).hidden_input_attrs()\n        ));\n    }\n}\n"

1	//! Property-based tests for the specialized components.
2	//!
3	//! The color-machine blocks feed an arbitrary event sequence through a machine
4	//! and assert the component's core invariants always hold: stored values stay
5	//! in range, states stay within the declared set, and `connect()` never panics.
6	//! The stateless `QrCode` block instead pins its prop->attr mapping across the
7	//! whole input space. Run in the nightly `extended-proptest` job; `#[ignore]`d
8	//! in the fast tier.
9	
10	// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
11	// ColorArea
12	// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
13	
14	mod color_area_proptests {
15	    use ars_components::specialized::color_area::{Event, Machine, Props};
16	    use ars_core::{AriaAttr, ColorChannel, Env, HtmlAttr, Service};
17	    use proptest::prelude::*;
18	
19	    fn arb_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = Event> {
20	        prop_oneof![
21	            (0.0f64..1.0, 0.0f64..1.0).prop_map(|(x, y)| Event::DragStart { x, y }),
22	            (0.0f64..1.0, 0.0f64..1.0).prop_map(|(x, y)| Event::DragMove { x, y }),
23	            Just(Event::DragEnd),
24	            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::IncrementX { step }),
25	            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::DecrementX { step }),
26	            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::IncrementY { step }),
27	            (0.0f64..0.5).prop_map(|step| Event::DecrementY { step }),
28	            Just(Event::SetXToMin),
29	            Just(Event::SetXToMax),
30	            Just(Event::SetYToMin),
31	            Just(Event::SetYToMax),
32	            any::<bool>().prop_map(|is_keyboard| Event::Focus { is_keyboard }),
33	            Just(Event::Blur),
34	        ]
35	    }
36	
37	    proptest! {
38	        #![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]
39	
40	        #[test]
41	        #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
42	        fn area_event_sequences_keep_value_valid(
43	            events in prop::collection::vec(arb_event(), 0..64),
44	        ) {
45	            let mut svc = Service::<Machine>::new(
46	                Props { id: "ca".into(), ..Props::default() },
47	                &Env::default(),
48	                &Default::default(),
49	            );
50	
51	            for ev in events {
52	                drop(svc.send(ev));
53	            }
54	
55	            let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});
56	
57	            let value = *api.value();
58	
59	            prop_assert!((0.0..360.0).contains(&value.hue));
60	            prop_assert!((0.0..=1.0).contains(&value.saturation));
61	            prop_assert!((0.0..=1.0).contains(&value.lightness));
62	
63	            // connect output reflects the in-range state: the thumb always
64	            // exposes a non-empty `aria-valuetext` describing the current color.
65	            let thumb = api.thumb_attrs();
66	
67	            prop_assert!(
68	                thumb
69	                    .get(&HtmlAttr::Aria(AriaAttr::ValueText))
70	                    .is_some_and(|text| !text.is_empty())
71	            );
72	
73	            // The two channels stay within their ranges.
74	            for ch in [ColorChannel::Saturation, ColorChannel::Lightness] {
75	                let (min, max) = ars_core::channel_range(ch);
76	
77	                let val = ars_core::channel_value(&value, ch);
78	
79	                prop_assert!(val >= min - 1e-9 && val <= max + 1e-9);
80	            }
81	        }
82	    }
83	}
84	
85	// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
86	// ColorSlider
87	// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
88

                // Stopwatch mode can never reach the completed state; only a
                // countdown Tick reaching zero produces `Completed`. (`SetTime`
                // is unconditional and may set a non-zero value while completed,
                // so completion-at-zero is asserted in the unit lifecycle test.)
                if mode == Mode::Stopwatch {
                    prop_assert_ne!(state, State::Completed);
                }

                // Countdown never exceeds its target (SetTime is bounded to it).
                if mode == Mode::Countdown {
                    prop_assert!(current <= target);
                }

                // `connect()` and its output methods never panic and always
                // produce finite progress and a non-empty formatted time.
                let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});

                prop_assert!(api.progress().is_finite());
                prop_assert!(!api.formatted_time().is_empty());

                drop(api.root_attrs());
                drop(api.progress_attrs());
                drop(api.start_trigger_attrs());
                drop(api.pause_trigger_attrs());
                drop(api.reset_trigger_attrs());
            }

{
  "command": "cd /Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295\ncat >> crates/ars-components/tests/proptest_state_machines/specialized.rs <<'RUST'\n\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// SignaturePad\n// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nmod signature_pad_proptests {\n    use ars_components::specialized::signature_pad::{Event, Machine, Props, State};\n    use ars_core::{Env, Service};\n    use proptest::prelude::*;\n\n    fn arb_event() -> impl Strategy<Value = Event> {\n        prop_oneof![\n            (0.0f64..1000.0, 0.0f64..1000.0, 0.0f64..=1.0)\n                .prop_map(|(x, y, pressure)| Event::DrawStart { x, y, pressure }),\n            (0.0f64..1000.0, 0.0f64..1000.0, 0.0f64..=1.0)\n                .prop_map(|(x, y, pressure)| Event::DrawMove { x, y, pressure }),\n            Just(Event::DrawEnd),\n            Just(Event::Undo),\n            Just(Event::Clear),\n            Just(Event::Focus),\n            Just(Event::Blur),\n        ]\n    }\n\n    proptest! {\n        #![proptest_config(super::super::common::proptest_config())]\n\n        #[test]\n        #[ignore = \"proptest — nightly extended-proptest job\"]\n        fn signature_event_sequences_keep_invariants(\n            events in prop::collection::vec(arb_event(), 0..96),\n        ) {\n            let mut svc = Service::<Machine>::new(\n                // `min_distance` 0 keeps every move point so committed strokes can\n                // grow; the invariants below must still hold for any sequence.\n                Props::new().id(\"sig\").min_distance(0.0),\n                &Env::default(),\n                &Default::default(),\n            );\n\n            for ev in events {\n                drop(svc.send(ev));\n            }\n\n            let state = *svc.state();\n            let ctx = svc.context();\n\n            // `current_stroke` is in flight iff we are drawing.\n            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.current_stroke.is_some(), state == State::Drawing);\n\n            // Idle implies no committed strokes; a non-empty signature is never\n            // left in `Idle`.\n            if state == State::Idle {\n                prop_assert!(ctx.data.get().is_empty());\n            }\n\n            // Every committed stroke has at least two points (single-point\n            // strokes are discarded on `DrawEnd`).\n            for stroke in &ctx.data.get().strokes {\n                prop_assert!(stroke.points.len() >= 2);\n            }\n\n            // The data helpers and `connect()` output never panic and stay\n            // mutually consistent.\n            let data = ctx.data.get();\n            prop_assert_eq!(data.is_empty(), data.point_count() == 0);\n            let _ = data.to_svg_path();\n\n            let api = svc.connect(&|_| {});\n            drop(api.root_attrs());\n            drop(api.canvas_attrs());\n            drop(api.clear_trigger_attrs());\n            drop(api.undo_trigger_attrs());\n            drop(api.guide_attrs());\n            drop(api.hidden_input_attrs());\n        }\n    }\n}\nRUST\necho \"appended\"\ncargo test -p ars-components --test proptest_state_machines signature_pad -- --include-ignored 2>&1 | tail -12",
  "description": "Append signature_pad proptest and run it"
}

//! `spec compile-snippets` — parse Rust code blocks in spec markdown
//! through `syn::parse_file` and surface syntax errors.
//!
//! ## What this catches
//!
//! - Top-level Rust syntax bugs in spec snippets: missing braces,
//!   mistyped `pub fn`, unterminated string literals, unbalanced
//!   generics outside macro bodies, etc.
//! - Coverage across the full spec corpus —
//!   `spec/{components,foundation,leptos-components,dioxus-components,shared,testing}/`.
//!   The pre-existing `lint-code` linter only walked
//!   `spec/components/` and never invoked a Rust parser, so foundation
//!   and per-adapter specs had zero parse coverage.
//!
//! ## What this **does not** catch (important — read this)
//!
//! - **Macro-internal syntax.** `view! { … }` and `rsx! { … }` are
//!   opaque token trees to both `syn` and `rustc`; everything inside
//!   the outer braces is unchecked. The original Leptos `view!`
//!   regression (an inline `|errors: ArcRwSignal<Errors>|` closure
//!   where the macro parser misread `<Errors>` as an HTML tag) sits
//!   *inside* the macro and would not be caught here. The unit test
//!   suites at `crates/ars-{leptos,dioxus}/tests/` are the source of
//!   truth for macro-internal correctness.
//! - **Type / path resolution.** A snippet that names a type defined
//!   nowhere in the workspace still parses syntactically. Use
//!   `cargo check` against a fixture crate for that.
//! - **Borrow-checker / ownership errors.** Same as above —
//!   `syn::parse_file` is purely a parser.
//!
//! ## Opting out
//!
//! Code blocks that intentionally contain partial or pseudo-Rust
//! (signatures shown for documentation purposes, top-level expressions
//! meant to live inside an outer fn body, etc.) opt out by tagging the
//! fence info-string with `rust,no_check` (also recognised:
//! `rust,ignore`, `rust,no_run`, mirroring rustdoc). The extractor
//! honors those markers and skips the block.
//!
//! ## Performance
//!
//! Fast: no compiler invocation. Runs in milliseconds across all 2000+
//! Rust blocks in the spec, so it is suitable for inclusion in the
//! `cargo xci` pipeline (see `xtask::ci::Step::SpecCompileSnippets`).

use std::{fs, path::Path};

use crate::manifest::{Error, SpecRoot};

/// CLI entry point.
///
/// Always returns `Ok(String)`. The text either begins with `ok:` (no
/// findings) or with `N finding(s):` followed by per-finding lines.
///
/// When `fix` is `true`, each failing block has its opening fence rewritten
/// from `` ```rust `` (or `` ```rs ``) to `` ```rust,no_check `` (or
/// `` ```rs,no_check ``) in place; the file is rewritten on disk and the
/// returned text reports how many fences were patched.
///
/// # Errors
///
/// Returns [`Error::Io`] when a target file cannot be read or written.
pub fn execute(root: &SpecRoot, fix: bool) -> Result<String, Error> {
    let mut files = Vec::new();

    for sub in [
        "components",
        "foundation",
        "leptos-components",
        "dioxus-components",
        "shared",
        "testing",
    ] {
        let dir = root.path.join(sub);

        collect_md_files(&dir, &mut files).map_err(Error::Io)?;
    }

    files.sort();

    let display_root = fs::canonicalize(&root.path).unwrap_or_else(|_| root.path.clone());

    let mut findings = Vec::new();
    let mut blocks_checked = 0usize;
    let mut fences_patched = 0usize;

    for path in &files {
        let source = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(Error::Io)?;

        let display = path
```

  "old_string": "### 1.5 Full Machine Implementation\n\n```rust\n/// The machine for the `SignaturePad` component.\npub struct Machine;\n\nimpl ars_core::Machine for Machine {\n    type State = State;\n    type Event = Event;\n    type Context = Context;\n    type Props = Props;\n    type Messages = Messages;\n    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;",
  "new_string": "### 1.5 Full Machine Implementation\n\nThe agnostic core never touches the live canvas or the screen reader. It emits\ntyped [`Effect`] markers and the framework adapter performs the real work:\n`DrawingListeners` tells the adapter to attach global pointer-drag listeners\n(e.g. `PlatformEffects::track_pointer_drag`) that dispatch `DrawMove`/`DrawEnd`\neven when the pointer leaves the canvas, and `AnnounceProvided`/`AnnounceCleared`\ntell it to announce the corresponding message into a polite `aria-live` region.\n\n```rust\n/// Typed effect intents emitted by the signature-pad machine.\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub enum Effect {\n    /// Adapter attaches global pointer-drag listeners that dispatch\n    /// `Event::DrawMove`/`Event::DrawEnd`. Emitted on entry to `Drawing`.\n    DrawingListeners,\n    /// Adapter announces `messages.signature_provided`. Emitted on entry to\n    /// `Completed`.\n    AnnounceProvided,\n    /// Adapter announces `messages.signature_cleared`. Emitted on `Clear`.\n    AnnounceCleared,\n}\n\n/// The machine for the `SignaturePad` component.\npub struct Machine;\n\nimpl ars_core::Machine for Machine {\n    type State = State;\n    type Event = Event;\n    type Context = Context;\n    type Props = Props;\n    type Messages = Messages;\n    type Effect = Effect;\n    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;"
}

336	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx| ctx.focused = false));
337	            }
338	            _ => {}
339	        }
340	
341	        if ctx.disabled || ctx.readonly {
342	            return None;
343	        }
344	
345	        match (state, event) {
346	            // Start drawing
347	            (State::Idle | State::Completed, Event::DrawStart { x, y, pressure }) => {
348	                let x = *x;
349	                let y = *y;
350	                let pressure = *pressure;
351	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Drawing).apply(move |ctx| {
352	                    ctx.current_stroke = Some(SignatureStroke {
353	                        points: vec![SignaturePoint {
354	                            x, y, pressure, timestamp: 0.0,
355	                        }],
356	                    });
357	                }).with_named_effect("drawing-listeners", |_ctx, _props, send| {
358	                    let platform = use_platform_effects();
359	                    let send_move = send.clone();
360	                    let send_up = send.clone();
361	                    platform.track_pointer_drag(
362	                        Box::new(move |x, y| { send_move.call_if_alive(Event::DrawMove { x, y, pressure: 0.5 }); }),
363	                        Box::new(move || { send_up.call_if_alive(Event::DrawEnd); }),
364	                    )
365	                }))
366	            }
367	
368	            // Continue drawing
369	            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawMove { x, y, pressure }) => {
370	                let x = *x;
371	                let y = *y;
372	                let pressure = *pressure;
373	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx| {
374	                    if let Some(ref mut stroke) = ctx.current_stroke {
375	                        if let Some(last) = stroke.points.last() {
376	                            let dx = x - last.x;
377	                            let dy = y - last.y;
378	                            let dist = (dx * dx + dy * dy).sqrt();
379	                            if dist >= ctx.min_distance {
380	                                stroke.points.push(SignaturePoint {
381	                                    x, y, pressure, timestamp: 0.0,
382	                                });
383	                            }
384	                        }
385	                    }
386	                }))
387	            }
388	
389	            // End drawing
390	            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawEnd) => {
391	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed).apply(|ctx| {
392	                    if let Some(stroke) = ctx.current_stroke.take() {
393	                        if stroke.points.len() >= 2 {
394	                            let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();
395	                            data.strokes.push(stroke);
396	                            ctx.data.set(data);
397	                        }
398	                    }
399	                }).with_named_effect("announce-provided", |ctx, _props, _send| {
400	                    let platform = use_platform_effects();
401	                    platform.announce(&(ctx.messages.signature_provided)(&ctx.locale));
402	                    no_cleanup()
403	                }))
404	            }
405	
406	            // Undo last stroke
407	            (State::Completed, Event::Undo) => {
408	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx| {
409	                    let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();
410	                    data.strokes.pop();
411	                    ctx.data.set(data);
412	                }))
413	            }
414	
415	            // Clear all strokes
416	            (_, Event::Clear) => {
417	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle).apply(|ctx| {
418	                    ctx.data.set(SignatureData::default());
419	                    ctx.current_stroke = None;
420	                }).with_named_effect("announce-cleared", |ctx, _props, _send| {
421	                    let platform = use_platform_effects();
422	                    platform.announce(&(ctx.messages.signature_cleared)(&ctx.locale));
423	                    no_cleanup()
424	                }))
425	            }

> AGENT

Beginning the audit. **Phase 1 — spec/implementation drift.** I synced the spec during implementation, so most surfaces already match. Re-reading both end-to-end, I found one genuine silent contract violation worth fixing:

**Finding H1 (both spec and impl wrong):** `State::Completed`'s doc says *"At least one stroke has been completed."* But `Undo` is defined as `context_only` (no state change). Undoing the **last** remaining stroke therefore leaves the machine in `Completed` with zero strokes — contradicting the `State::Completed` contract, and inconsistent with `Clear` (which goes to `Idle`). The `State::Idle` doc is *"No strokes, canvas is blank"* — that's exactly the post-undo-to-empty condition.

Best outcome (dictated by the `State` enum's own prose, no genuine tradeoff): `Undo` transitions to `Idle` when it removes the last stroke, otherwise stays `Completed`. Let me land it (impl + test + proptest invariant + spec).

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01AdzRpNbDJYSEU8FZmwriC4
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "            // Undo the last completed stroke.\n            (State::Completed, Event::Undo) => {\n                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();\n                    data.strokes.pop();\n                    ctx.data.set(data);\n                }))\n            }",
  "new_string": "            // Undo the last completed stroke. Removing the final stroke returns\n            // to `Idle` (a blank canvas is `Idle`, never `Completed`).\n            (State::Completed, Event::Undo) => {\n                let target = if ctx.data.get().strokes.len() <= 1 {\n                    State::Idle\n                } else {\n                    State::Completed\n                };\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();\n                    data.strokes.pop();\n                    ctx.data.set(data);\n                }))\n            }"
}

> AGENT

The new invariant caught a **second instance of the same contract violation (Finding H2)**: `DrawEnd` unconditionally enters `Completed` and announces "Signature provided" — even for a trivial tap whose single-point stroke is discarded, leaving `Completed` + empty data and a spurious announcement. Notably, the issue's own "Tests to add first" says *"Drawing->Idle on pointer up"*, which only makes sense for the no-commit case. Best outcome reconciles both: `DrawEnd` → `Idle` when nothing is committed and the canvas is still blank, `Completed` otherwise, announcing only when a stroke actually commits.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01F17NohmZHM6FsGWC4uphCB
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "            // End drawing: commit the in-flight stroke if it has enough points.\n            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawEnd) => Some(\n                TransitionPlan::to(State::Completed)\n                    .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                        if let Some(stroke) = ctx.current_stroke.take()\n                            && stroke.points.len() >= 2\n                        {\n                            let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();\n                            data.strokes.push(stroke);\n                            ctx.data.set(data);\n                        }\n                    })\n                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceProvided)),\n            ),",
  "new_string": "            // End drawing: commit the in-flight stroke if it has enough points.\n            // A stroke that is too short to commit (a stray tap) leaves the\n            // signature unchanged, so the pad returns to `Idle` when the canvas\n            // is still blank and only announces when a stroke is actually added.\n            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawEnd) => {\n                let commits = ctx\n                    .current_stroke\n                    .as_ref()\n                    .is_some_and(|stroke| stroke.points.len() >= 2);\n                let target = if commits || !ctx.data.get().is_empty() {\n                    State::Completed\n                } else {\n                    State::Idle\n                };\n                let mut plan = TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    if let Some(stroke) = ctx.current_stroke.take()\n                        && stroke.points.len() >= 2\n                    {\n                        let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();\n                        data.strokes.push(stroke);\n                        ctx.data.set(data);\n                    }\n                });\n                if commits {\n                    plan = plan.with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceProvided));\n                }\n                Some(plan)\n            }"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "    #[test]\n    fn draw_end_discards_single_point_stroke() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().min_distance(100.0));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        // Move is culled (within min_distance), leaving a 1-point stroke.\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawMove {\n            x: 1.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawEnd));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert!(service.context().data.get().is_empty());\n    }",
  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn draw_end_discards_single_point_stroke_and_returns_to_idle() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().min_distance(100.0));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        // Move is culled (within min_distance), leaving a 1-point stroke.\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawMove {\n            x: 1.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        let result = service.send(Event::DrawEnd);\n\n        // A blank canvas after a discarded tap is `Idle`, not `Completed`, and\n        // nothing was provided to announce.\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(service.context().data.get().is_empty());\n        assert!(result.pending_effects.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn draw_end_discarded_tap_keeps_prior_signature_completed() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(\n            test_props()\n                .default_data(data_with(1))\n                .min_distance(100.0),\n        );\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        // Culled move leaves a 1-point stroke; the tap is discarded but the\n        // earlier signature remains, so the pad stays `Completed`.\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawMove {\n            x: 1.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        let result = service.send(Event::DrawEnd);\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert_eq!(service.context().data.get().strokes.len(), 1);\n        assert!(result.pending_effects.is_empty());\n    }"
}

41:Canvas, guide, action triggers, and hidden input are adapter-owned. `Canvas` is the only drawing surface and owns the imperative rendering lifecycle.

61:`Canvas` requires a live node after mount for sizing, coordinate transforms, and drawing. No consumer ref composition is part of the base surface.
65:- machine-owned state: stroke list, drawing state, disabled/read-only semantics, and undo/clear availability.

83:SSR renders the structural signature-pad surface but performs no canvas drawing. Canvas sizing, drawing, and export are client-only.
87:Keep at most one active drawing session. Batch canvas redraw work so high-frequency pointer moves do not allocate new structural nodes.
100:3. Add drawing sessions and cleanup.
105:- Do not allow browser panning on the drawing canvas.
112:- Consumers must not assume drawing works during SSR.
118:| structural signature surface  | full support   | full support   | no live drawing on SSR            |
119:| canvas drawing and undo/clear | full support   | client-only    | requires canvas and pointer APIs  |
124:Missing canvas context after mount is a debug warning and graceful interaction fallback. Multiple active drawing sessions are fail-fast.

Catalog columns are parity references (Ark UI / React Aria), not impl-status — Timer/Clipboard show the same `Y | — ` format, so SignaturePad's row is already correct. Manifest is registered. No change there.

**Round 2** surfaces a real gap the first round under-weighted: my `on_props_changed` returns empty, so the **controlled `data` prop never syncs after init** — a controlled-component contract violation — and runtime changes to `disabled`/`readonly`/`pen_color`/etc. are ignored. Every sibling (clipboard, timer) syncs props. Let me check clipboard's exact pattern.

> AGENT

Decisive: **all five** sibling specs (clipboard, timer, color-picker, color-area, angle-slider) specify `on_props_changed` prop-sync. SignaturePad's spec is the lone omission — a spec gap, not a deliberate design. The controlled `data` prop is genuinely non-functional after init without it, and the state-reconciliation rule is already dictated by the Idle/Completed invariant I established in H1/H2. This is convention-alignment with unanimous precedent, so I'll land it (impl + spec + tests). Let me read the current Event enum and transition head.
