It's the auto-derived `Clone`/`Debug` impls — `assert_eq!` only calls `Debug` on failure, so they're unexercised in ars-core's own tests (other crates cover them, but I'll make ars-core self-sufficient). Adding a test that exercises the derives:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01LCpdMddfaCtXNJ9J3sXTFM
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/crates/ars-core/src/raster.rs",
  "old_string": "    #[test]\n    fn raster_error_implements_std_error() {\n        fn assert_error<E: core::error::Error>(_: &E) {}\n        assert_error(&RasterError::Unsupported);\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn raster_error_implements_std_error() {\n        fn assert_error<E: core::error::Error>(_: &E) {}\n        assert_error(&RasterError::Unsupported);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn raster_types_clone_debug_and_compare() {\n        use alloc::format;\n\n        let point = RasterPoint {\n            x: 1.0,\n            y: 2.0,\n            pressure: 0.3,\n        };\n        assert_eq!(point, point.clone());\n        assert!(format!(\"{point:?}\").contains(\"RasterPoint\"));\n\n        let spec = RasterSpec::new(10, 20).background(\"#ffffff\");\n        assert_eq!(spec, spec.clone());\n        assert!(format!(\"{spec:?}\").contains(\"RasterSpec\"));\n\n        let image = RasterImage {\n            format: RasterFormat::Webp,\n            bytes: vec![1, 2, 3],\n        };\n        assert_eq!(image, image.clone());\n        assert!(format!(\"{image:?}\").contains(\"RasterImage\"));\n\n        let error = RasterError::Backend(\"boom\".into());\n        assert_eq!(error, error.clone());\n        assert!(format!(\"{error:?}\").contains(\"Backend\"));\n\n        assert_eq!(format!(\"{:?}\", RasterFormat::Webp), \"Webp\");\n\n        let rasterizer = NullSignatureRasterizer;\n        let _ = rasterizer.clone();\n        assert!(format!(\"{rasterizer:?}\").contains(\"NullSignatureRasterizer\"));\n    }\n}"
}

> AGENT

WebP support is complete and fully verified. Summary of this addition:

## WebP + honest format detection

**`ars-core`** ([raster.rs](crates/ars-core/src/raster.rs)): added `RasterFormat::Webp` plus `RasterFormat::mime()` / `from_mime()` helpers (the round-trip used for honest detection). 8 tests; ars-core aggregate coverage **96.29%** (threshold 75%).

**`ars-dom`** ([signature_raster.rs](crates/ars-dom/src/signature_raster.rs)): WebP routed through the quality encoder; **`RasterImage::format` is now derived from the returned data URL's actual MIME** (`from_mime`), so Safari's silent PNG fallback is reported truthfully as `Png`, never mislabeled `Webp`. Added a 4th wasm browser test asserting format↔magic-byte consistency — **all 4 pass in headless Chrome** (real WebP `RIFF…WEBP` verified).

**Specs**: `RasterFormat` enum (01-architecture §2.2.9), signature-pad export note, 11-dom-utilities §13 (honest-reporting paragraph), both adapter specs — all now say PNG/JPEG/WebP with the Safari caveat.

**Verification**: clippy `-D warnings` clean (native all-features + wasm32); `spec validate` + `compile-snippets` pass; all unit/wasm tests green.

The full feature set is now: vector export (`Svg`/`Points`, total) in the agnostic core, plus injected raster export (`Png`/`Jpeg`/`Webp`, pressure-aware, honest format) via `SignatureRasterizer` with `ars-dom`'s `WebSignatureRasterizer`.

**No commit/push yet.** Ready for the final steps pending your go-ahead:
1. `cargo xci-fast` (full pre-push gate incl. native coverage + error-variant-coverage), fix anything it flags.
2. Commit + PR to `main` (`Closes #295`, `snapshot-reviewed` label).
3. `waiting-for-codex-review` loop until 👍.

> AGENT

Good instinct to check for reuse, but here `String` (CSS color) is the right fit — `ColorValue` would actually hurt. Reasoning:

**Source and sink are both CSS strings.**
- The `SignaturePad` component already models pen color as `pub pen_color: String` ("CSS color string", default `"#000000"`) in both `Props` and `Context` (spec §1.3/§1.4). So the value arrives as a CSS string.
- The canvas sink — `ctx.set_stroke_style_str(&str)` / `set_fill_style_str(&str)` — also takes a CSS string.

So `RasterSpec.pen_color: String` is the same representation at both ends. Inserting `ColorValue` in the middle means: component `String` → `ColorValue` → back to `String` for the canvas — two conversions around a value that's a string the whole way.

**`ColorValue` can't represent what a CSS pen color can.** `ColorValue` is HSL+alpha, and its only string parser is `from_hex` (returns `Option`, hex-only). It can't carry named colors (`"red"`), `rgb()/hsl()` inputs, `currentColor`, or theme tokens — all valid, useful pen colors an author might set. Routing through `ColorValue` would silently drop those (`from_hex` → `None`).

**`ColorValue` earns its place where color is *manipulated*, not passed through.** ColorPicker / ColorArea / ColorSlider use `ColorValue` because they need HSL channels, 2D area mapping, format conversions, channel math. SignaturePad doesn't manipulate the pen color — it's an author-set token forwarded to a CSS API. Different role, different right type. (Layering isn't the blocker — `RasterSpec` is in `ars-core` and could use `ColorValue`; it's the semantic mismatch.)

**Verdict:** keep `pen_color: String` / `background: Option<String>`. It matches how the component already specs pen color, matches the canvas API, and preserves full CSS-color expressiveness.

The only scenario where `ColorValue` would fit is if you wanted pen color **modeled as `ColorValue` end-to-end** — i.e., change the SignaturePad component's `pen_color` field to `ColorValue` too (e.g., to drive it from a ColorPicker), converting to a CSS string only at the canvas boundary via `to_hex(true)`. That's coherent but a spec change to the component, and it trades away named-color/`currentColor` support. Want me to keep `String` (my recommendation), or pursue that end-to-end `ColorValue` redesign?

> DEVELOPER

Should we have a new CssColor type that can represent all the ways color can appear in CSS in a typed manner?

> AGENT

Short answer: a `CssColor` that **exhaustively** types every CSS color is the wrong goal — it's effectively a CSS color engine — but a small typed wrapper *could* make sense later as its own foundation effort. Not in this PR.

## Why exhaustive typing doesn't work

CSS color (Color Module L4/L5) is huge and, worse, partly **unresolvable outside the browser**:
- Families: named (~150) + `transparent`, hex (3/4/6/8), `rgb()/hsl()/hwb()`, `lab()/lch()/oklab()/oklch()`, `color(display-p3 …)`, `color-mix()`, relative color (`rgb(from … )`), system colors (`Canvas`, `ButtonText`).
- Context-dependent tokens: `currentColor`, `var(--token)`, `light-dark()`, system colors — these **only resolve at paint time** against the computed style + custom-property registry. A standalone Rust value can't evaluate them; it can only carry them opaquely.
- It's a **moving target** — `oklch`, relative color, `color-mix` are recent; a closed enum would perpetually lag CSS.

So "all of CSS color, typed" means either reimplementing/maintaining a CSS color parser+resolver (out of scope for a component library) or having an `Other(String)` escape hatch that immediately swallows the long tail — at which point it's "typed where convenient, string otherwise," not exhaustive.

## We already have the right two-tier model

- **`ColorValue` (HSL+alpha)** — for colors we *compute and manipulate* (ColorPicker area/sliders/channels, conversions, contrast). This is where typing earns its keep, and it's already used there.
- **CSS-color `String`** — for colors we *pass straight to the DOM* (pen color, backgrounds). The browser is the authority; a string supports 100% of CSS color **including `var(--brand)`, `currentColor`, theming, and future syntax** that no closed type could.

These cover the two real roles. The split isn't an inconsistency — it's "value vs. pass-through token."

## If you ever do want a typed boundary

The defensible shape is a **small, non-exhaustive** enum, introduced when a concrete consumer needs it (e.g., validating authored colors, or converting at many call sites):

```rust
enum CssColor {
    Value(ColorValue),  // hex/rgb/hsl families — typed & manipulable
    Keyword(NamedColor),// named + currentColor + transparent
    Raw(String),        // escape hatch: var(), oklch(), color-mix(), future syntax
}
```

That's a **cross-cutting vocabulary type** touching `ColorValue`, every color component, and theming — a foundation-level decision deserving its own spec task and review, **not** a rider on the SignaturePad rasterizer PR. Bolting it on here would be scope creep and would churn unrelated APIs.

## Recommendation for #295

Keep `pen_color: String` / `background: Option<String>`. If the typed-`CssColor` idea has legs, I'd capture it as a separate foundation issue (with the `Value | Keyword | Raw` sketch and the "don't try to be exhaustive" rationale) so it's designed deliberately.

Want me to (a) keep `String` and move on to finishing #295, or (b) also write up that future `CssColor` design as a spec note / issue before we wrap?

```
=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gnbwo
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs:637  (reply_to=3345569439)
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Cancel drawing before rejecting disabled events**

When a parent flips `disabled` or `readonly` while the user is already drawing, `SyncProps` only updates the flags and leaves the machine in `State::Drawing` with `current_stroke` set; this guard then rejects the subsequent `DrawEnd` (and even `Clear`), so the pad stays stuck in the drawing state until it is re-enabled and can later commit a stale stroke. The sync path should cancel/finish the in-flight stroke or transition out of `Drawing` before disabled/read-only starts rejecting input.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gnbwq
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs:712  (reply_to=3345569442)
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Emit changes for controlled signature data**

When `Props::data` is set, `Bindable::set` only stages this new stroke list internally while `get()` and all exported/hidden-input attrs continue reading the unchanged controlled value until the parent syncs it back. Since `Props` has no change callback/effect for the adapter to notify the parent, drawing/undo/clear in controlled mode can leave the machine in `Completed` or `Idle` while `api.data()` and form submission still expose the old signature, making the advertised controlled `data` prop unusable.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gnbws
file: crates/ars-dom/src/signature_raster.rs:52  (reply_to=3345569444)
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Reject zero-sized raster exports before encoding**

In browser builds, a `RasterSpec` with `width == 0` or `height == 0` (for example exporting before the canvas has been measured or while it is hidden) makes `toDataURL` return the sentinel `data:,` rather than an encoded image. Because this code does not validate dimensions before calling the encoder, `decode_data_url` accepts that sentinel and returns `Ok(RasterImage { format: requested, bytes: [] })`, so callers can treat an empty byte buffer as a valid PNG/JPEG/WebP export.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gnbwt
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs:1026  (reply_to=3345569445)
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Disable the submitted input when the pad is disabled**

When a disabled signature pad is rendered inside a form with `name` set, this hidden input still carries the name/value and will be submitted even though the rest of the component is disabled. Other form-backed components in the repo mark their hidden inputs disabled for this case, so disabled signatures can unexpectedly submit stale data unless this attr mirrors `ctx.disabled`.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gnbwu
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs:65  (reply_to=3345569447)
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Treat empty strokes as blank signatures**

If a controlled/default value contains only empty strokes (which this module already tolerates by skipping them in `to_svg_path`), `is_empty()` returns false, so initialization enters `Completed`, the guide hides, and clear/undo become enabled even though the submitted/exported path is empty. This makes externally supplied or deserialized empty stroke data look like a real signature; emptiness should be based on recorded points or the input should be normalized before state selection.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.
```

456	/// The agnostic core never touches the live canvas or the screen reader; it
457	/// emits these markers and the framework adapter performs the real work.
458	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
459	pub enum Effect {
460	    /// Adapter attaches global pointer-drag listeners (e.g. via
461	    /// `PlatformEffects::track_pointer_drag`) that dispatch [`Event::DrawMove`]
462	    /// while the pointer moves and [`Event::DrawEnd`] on pointer up, so a stroke
463	    /// continues even when the pointer leaves the canvas. Emitted on the
464	    /// transition into [`State::Drawing`].
465	    DrawingListeners,
466	
467	    /// Adapter announces [`Messages::signature_provided`] into a polite
468	    /// `aria-live` region. Emitted on the transition into [`State::Completed`]
469	    /// (pointer up after a valid stroke).
470	    AnnounceProvided,
471	
472	    /// Adapter announces [`Messages::signature_cleared`] into a polite
473	    /// `aria-live` region. Emitted on the [`Event::Clear`] transition.
474	    AnnounceCleared,
475	}

596	    fn transition(
597	        state: &Self::State,
598	        event: &Self::Event,
599	        ctx: &Self::Context,
600	        props: &Self::Props,
601	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
602	        // Focus/Blur and the prop-sync events always pass through, regardless of
603	        // disabled/read-only (prop sync must be able to re-enable the pad).
604	        match event {
605	            Event::Focus => {
606	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx: &mut Context| {
607	                    ctx.focused = true;
608	                }));
609	            }
610	
611	            Event::Blur => {
612	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx: &mut Context| {
613	                    ctx.focused = false;
614	                }));
615	            }
616	
617	            Event::SyncProps => {
618	                let disabled = props.disabled;
619	                let readonly = props.readonly;
620	                let pen_color = props.pen_color.clone();
621	                let pen_width = props.pen_width;
622	                let min_distance = props.min_distance;
623	                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
624	                    ctx.disabled = disabled;
625	                    ctx.readonly = readonly;
626	                    ctx.pen_color = pen_color;
627	                    ctx.pen_width = pen_width;
628	                    ctx.min_distance = min_distance;
629	                }));
630	            }
631	
632	            Event::SyncData => {
633	                let new_data = props.data.clone();
634	                // Emptiness after the sync: the new controlled value, or — when
635	                // dropping to uncontrolled — the retained local value.
636	
637	                let empty_after = if let Some(data) = &new_data {
638	                    data.is_empty()
639	                } else {
640	                    ctx.data.pending().is_empty()
641	                };
642	
643	                // Reconcile the displayed state with the synced data, but never
644	                // reorder an in-flight stroke.
645	                let plan = if matches!(state, State::Drawing) {
646	                    TransitionPlan::new()
647	                } else {
648	                    TransitionPlan::to(if empty_after {
649	                        State::Idle
650	                    } else {
651	                        State::Completed
652	                    })
653	                };
654	
655	                return Some(plan.apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
656	                    if let Some(data) = new_data {
657	                        ctx.data.set(data.clone());
658	                        ctx.data.sync_controlled(Some(data));
659	                    } else {
660	                        ctx.data.sync_controlled(None);
661	                    }
662	                }));
663	            }
664	
665	            _ => {}
666	        }
667	
668	        if ctx.disabled || ctx.readonly {
669	            return None;
670	        }
671	
672	        match (state, event) {
673	            // Start drawing: seed a new stroke and ask the adapter to wire up
674	            // the global pointer-drag listeners that feed DrawMove/DrawEnd.
675	            (State::Idle | State::Completed, Event::DrawStart { x, y, pressure }) => {
676	                let point = SignaturePoint {
677	                    x: *x,
678	                    y: *y,
679	                    pressure: *pressure,
680	                    timestamp: 0.0,
681	                };
682	
683	                Some(
684	                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Drawing)
685	                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
686	                            ctx.current_stroke = Some(SignatureStroke {
687	                                points: vec![point],
688	                            });
689	                        })
690	                        .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::DrawingListeners)),
691	                )
692	            }
693	
694	            // Continue drawing: append the point only when it is at least
695	            // `min_distance` away from the previous one (cull jitter).
696	            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawMove { x, y, pressure }) => {

  "old_string": "            Event::SyncProps => {\n                let disabled = props.disabled;\n                let readonly = props.readonly;\n                let pen_color = props.pen_color.clone();\n                let pen_width = props.pen_width;\n                let min_distance = props.min_distance;\n                return Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.disabled = disabled;\n                    ctx.readonly = readonly;\n                    ctx.pen_color = pen_color;\n                    ctx.pen_width = pen_width;\n                    ctx.min_distance = min_distance;\n                }));\n            }",
  "new_string": "            Event::SyncProps => {\n                let disabled = props.disabled;\n                let readonly = props.readonly;\n                let pen_color = props.pen_color.clone();\n                let pen_width = props.pen_width;\n                let min_distance = props.min_distance;\n\n                // Becoming non-interactive mid-stroke must not strand the machine\n                // in `Drawing` — the disabled/read-only gate below would then\n                // reject the trailing `DrawEnd`/`Clear`, leaving an in-flight\n                // stroke that could later commit stale. Cancel it and leave\n                // `Drawing` (blank canvas -> `Idle`, otherwise `Completed`).\n                let cancel_drawing =\n                    (disabled || readonly) && matches!(state, State::Drawing);\n                let target = if cancel_drawing {\n                    if ctx.data.get().is_empty() {\n                        State::Idle\n                    } else {\n                        State::Completed\n                    }\n                } else {\n                    *state\n                };\n\n                return Some(TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    ctx.disabled = disabled;\n                    ctx.readonly = readonly;\n                    ctx.pen_color = pen_color;\n                    ctx.pen_width = pen_width;\n                    ctx.min_distance = min_distance;\n                    if cancel_drawing {\n                        ctx.current_stroke = None;\n                    }\n                }));\n            }"
}

715	            // `min_distance` away from the previous one (cull jitter).
716	            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawMove { x, y, pressure }) => {
717	                let point = SignaturePoint {
718	                    x: *x,
719	                    y: *y,
720	                    pressure: *pressure,
721	                    timestamp: 0.0,
722	                };
723	
724	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
725	                    let min_distance = ctx.min_distance;
726	
727	                    if let Some(stroke) = ctx.current_stroke.as_mut()
728	                        && let Some(last) = stroke.points.last()
729	                    {
730	                        let dx = point.x - last.x;
731	                        let dy = point.y - last.y;
732	
733	                        if (dx * dx + dy * dy).sqrt() >= min_distance {
734	                            stroke.points.push(point);
735	                        }
736	                    }
737	                }))
738	            }
739	
740	            // End drawing: commit the in-flight stroke if it has enough points.
741	            // A stroke that is too short to commit (a stray tap) leaves the
742	            // signature unchanged, so the pad returns to `Idle` when the canvas
743	            // is still blank and only announces when a stroke is actually added.
744	            (State::Drawing, Event::DrawEnd) => {
745	                let commits = ctx
746	                    .current_stroke
747	                    .as_ref()
748	                    .is_some_and(|stroke| stroke.points.len() >= 2);
749	
750	                let target = if commits || !ctx.data.get().is_empty() {
751	                    State::Completed
752	                } else {
753	                    State::Idle
754	                };
755	
756	                let mut plan = TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
757	                    if let Some(stroke) = ctx.current_stroke.take()
758	                        && stroke.points.len() >= 2
759	                    {
760	                        let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();
761	
762	                        data.strokes.push(stroke);
763	
764	                        ctx.data.set(data);
765	                    }
766	                });
767	
768	                if commits {
769	                    plan = plan.with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceProvided));
770	                }
771	
772	                Some(plan)
773	            }
774	
775	            // Undo the last completed stroke. Removing the final stroke returns
776	            // to `Idle` (a blank canvas is `Idle`, never `Completed`).
777	            (State::Completed, Event::Undo) => {
778	                let target = if ctx.data.get().strokes.len() <= 1 {
779	                    State::Idle
780	                } else {
781	                    State::Completed
782	                };
783	
784	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
785	                    let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();
786	
787	                    data.strokes.pop();
788	
789	                    ctx.data.set(data);
790	                }))
791	            }
792	
793	            // Clear all strokes from any state.
794	            (_, Event::Clear) => Some(
795	                TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)
796	                    .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
797	                        ctx.data.set(SignatureData::default());
798	                        ctx.current_stroke = None;
799	                    })

Removing the final stroke returns\n            // to `Idle` (a blank canvas is `Idle`, never `Completed`).\n            (State::Completed, Event::Undo) => {\n                let target = if ctx.data.get().strokes.len() <= 1 {\n                    State::Idle\n                } else {\n                    State::Completed\n                };\n\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                    let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();\n\n                    data.strokes.pop();\n\n                    ctx.data.set(data);\n                }))\n            }\n\n            // Clear all strokes from any state.\n            (_, Event::Clear) => Some(\n                TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)\n                    .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                        ctx.data.set(SignatureData::default());\n                        ctx.current_stroke = None;\n                    })\n                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCleared)),\n            ),\n\n            _ => None,\n        }",

Removing the final stroke returns\n            // to `Idle` (a blank canvas is `Idle`, never `Completed`).\n            (State::Completed, Event::Undo) => {\n                let target = if ctx.data.get().strokes.len() <= 1 {\n                    State::Idle\n                } else {\n                    State::Completed\n                };\n\n                Some(\n                    TransitionPlan::to(target)\n                        .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                            let mut data = ctx.data.get().clone();\n\n                            data.strokes.pop();\n\n                            ctx.data.set(data);\n                        })\n                        .with_effect(data_change_effect()),\n                )\n            }\n\n            // Clear all strokes from any state.\n            (_, Event::Clear) => {\n                // Notify the parent only when there was something to clear, so a\n                // clear on an already-blank pad does not emit a spurious change.\n                let had_data = !ctx.data.get().is_empty();\n\n                let mut plan = TransitionPlan::to(State::Idle)\n                    .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {\n                        ctx.data.set(SignatureData::default());\n                        ctx.current_stroke = None;\n                    })\n                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceCleared));\n\n                if had_data {\n                    plan = plan.with_effect(data_change_effect());\n                }\n\n                Some(plan)\n            }\n\n            _ => None,\n        }"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "/// DOM parts of the `SignaturePad` component.\n#[derive(ComponentPart)]",
  "new_string": "/// Builds the [`Effect::DataChange`] effect that notifies\n/// [`Props::on_data_change`] with the new signature data.\n///\n/// Reads the bound value's *pending* (internal) data, which is the value just\n/// committed by the transition — in controlled mode `get()` would still return\n/// the stale parent-owned value until it round-trips back through props.\nfn data_change_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {\n    PendingEffect::new(Effect::DataChange, |ctx: &Context, props: &Props, _send| {\n        if let Some(callback) = &props.on_data_change {\n            callback(ctx.data.pending().clone());\n        }\n\n        no_cleanup()\n    })\n}\n\n/// DOM parts of the `SignaturePad` component.\n#[derive(ComponentPart)]"
}

Now T3 — zero-sized guard in the ars-dom web rasterizer:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01GXceoSLsPkjaD1U4odCywg
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/ericson/Workspace/Rust/ars-ui.worktrees/impl-295/crates/ars-dom/src/signature_raster.rs",
  "old_string": "fn rasterize_impl(\n    strokes: &[Vec<RasterPoint>],\n    spec: &RasterSpec,\n) -> Result<RasterImage, RasterError> {\n    let window = web_sys::window().ok_or_else(|| RasterError::Backend(\"no window\".into()))?;",
  "new_string": "fn rasterize_impl(\n    strokes: &[Vec<RasterPoint>],\n    spec: &RasterSpec,\n) -> Result<RasterImage, RasterError> {\n    // A zero-sized canvas makes `toDataURL` return the `data:,` sentinel rather\n    // than an encoded image, which would otherwise decode to an empty byte\n    // buffer that callers could mistake for a valid export. Reject it up front.\n    if spec.width == 0 || spec.height == 0 {\n        return Err(RasterError::Backend(\n            \"raster spec has zero width or height\".into(),\n        ));\n    }\n\n    let window = web_sys::window().ok_or_else(|| RasterError::Backend(\"no window\".into()))?;"
}
```

drop(service.set_props(test_props().data(data_with(1))));\n\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Drawing);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn set_props_disabling_mid_stroke_cancels_drawing() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props());\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Drawing);\n\n        // Disabling while drawing must cancel the in-flight stroke and leave\n        // Drawing, not strand the machine where the disabled gate rejects DrawEnd.\n        drop(service.set_props(test_props().disabled(true)));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Idle);\n        assert!(service.context().current_stroke.is_none());\n\n        // The (now disabled) trailing DrawEnd is a no-op and cannot commit a\n        // stale stroke.\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawEnd));\n        assert!(service.context().data.get().is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn set_props_readonly_mid_stroke_with_prior_data_returns_to_completed() {\n        let mut service = fresh_service(test_props().default_data(data_with(1)));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 9.0,\n            y: 9.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Drawing);\n\n        drop(service.set_props(test_props().default_data(data_with(1)).readonly(true)));\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n        assert!(service.context().current_stroke.is_none());\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── on_data_change ─────────────────────────\n\n    fn recording_pad() -> (Service<Machine>, std::sync::Arc<std::sync::Mutex<Vec<SignatureData>>>) {\n        use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\n\n        let log: Arc<Mutex<Vec<SignatureData>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));\n        let sink = Arc::clone(&log);\n        let service = Service::<Machine>::new(\n            test_props()\n                .min_distance(0.0)\n                .on_data_change(ars_core::callback(move |data: SignatureData| {\n                    sink.lock().unwrap().push(data);\n                })),\n            &Env::default(),\n            &Messages::default(),\n        );\n        (service, log)\n    }\n\n    fn draw_one_stroke(service: &mut Service<Machine>) {\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawStart {\n            x: 0.0,\n            y: 0.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawMove {\n            x: 5.0,\n            y: 5.0,\n            pressure: 0.5,\n        }));\n        drop(service.send(Event::DrawEnd));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn on_data_change_fires_with_new_data_on_commit_undo_and_clear() {\n        let (mut service, log) = recording_pad();\n\n        draw_one_stroke(&mut service);\n        assert_eq!(log.lock().unwrap().len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(log.lock().unwrap()[0].strokes.len(), 1);\n\n        drop(service.send(Event::Undo));\n        assert_eq!(log.lock().unwrap().len(), 2);\n        assert!(log.lock().unwrap()[1].is_empty());\n\n        // Drawing again then clearing fires once more with the emptied data.\n        draw_one_stroke(&mut service);\n        drop(service.send(Event::Clear));\n        let calls = log.lock().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(calls.len(), 4);\n        assert!(calls[3].is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn clear_on_blank_pad_does_not_fire_data_change() {\n        let (mut service, log) = recording_pad();\n        drop(service.send(Event::Clear));\n        assert!(log.lock().unwrap().is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn controlled_data_change_round_trips_through_callback_and_sync() {\n        // In controlled mode the parent owns `data`: the component proposes the\n        // new value via `on_data_change`, the parent feeds it back through props,\n        // and only then does `api.data()` reflect it.\n        let mut service = Service::<Machine>::new(\n            test_props().min_distance(0.0).data(SignatureData::default()),\n            &Env::default(),\n            &Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        draw_one_stroke(&mut service);\n        // The committed stroke is staged but the controlled value still reads empty.\n        assert!(service.context().data.get().is_empty());\n        let pending = service.context().data.pending().clone();\n        assert_eq!(pending.strokes.len(), 1);\n\n        // Parent applies the proposed data back through props.\n        drop(service.set_props(test_props().min_distance(0.0).data(pending)));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().data.get().strokes.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── Api accessors ─────────────────────────"
}

}

  "new_string": "    /// Sends an event and runs the resulting pending effects, so effect-backed\n    /// callbacks (like `on_data_change`) actually fire — `Service::send` only\n    /// returns the effects for the adapter to run.\n    fn send_run(service: &mut Service<Machine>, event: Event) {\n        use std::sync::Arc;\n\n        let mut result = service.send(event);\n        let send: ars_core::StrongSend<Event> = Arc::new(|_| {});\n        for effect in result.pending_effects.drain(..) {\n            drop(effect.run(service.context(), service.props(), Arc::clone(&send)));\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn draw_one_stroke(service: &mut Service<Machine>) {\n        send_run(\n            service,\n            Event::DrawStart {\n                x: 0.0,\n                y: 0.0,\n                pressure: 0.5,\n            },\n        );\n        send_run(\n            service,\n            Event::DrawMove {\n                x: 5.0,\n                y: 5.0,\n                pressure: 0.5,\n            },\n        );\n        send_run(service, Event::DrawEnd);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn on_data_change_fires_with_new_data_on_commit_undo_and_clear() {\n        let (mut service, log) = recording_pad();\n\n        draw_one_stroke(&mut service);\n        assert_eq!(log.lock().unwrap().len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(log.lock().unwrap()[0].strokes.len(), 1);\n\n        send_run(&mut service, Event::Undo);\n        assert_eq!(log.lock().unwrap().len(), 2);\n        assert!(log.lock().unwrap()[1].is_empty());\n\n        // Drawing again then clearing fires once more with the emptied data.\n        draw_one_stroke(&mut service);\n        send_run(&mut service, Event::Clear);\n        let calls = log.lock().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(calls.len(), 4);\n        assert!(calls[3].is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn clear_on_blank_pad_does_not_fire_data_change() {\n        let (mut service, log) = recording_pad();\n        send_run(&mut service, Event::Clear);\n        assert!(log.lock().unwrap().is_empty());\n    }"

  "new_string": "    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {\n        // Point-based, not stroke-count-based: data containing only empty\n        // strokes (no points) is blank, matching `to_svg_path`/`point_count`,\n        // so externally supplied empty data never looks like a real signature.\n        self.strokes.iter().all(|stroke| stroke.points.is_empty())\n    }"
}

  "new_string": "            Event::SyncProps => {\n                let disabled = props.disabled;\n                let readonly = props.readonly;\n                let pen_color = props.pen_color.clone();\n                let pen_width = props.pen_width;\n                let min_distance = props.min_distance;\n                // Becoming non-interactive mid-stroke must not strand the machine\n                // in `Drawing` (the gate below would reject the trailing\n                // `DrawEnd`/`Clear`); cancel the in-flight stroke and leave it.\n                let cancel_drawing = (disabled || readonly) && matches!(state, State::Drawing);\n                let target = if cancel_drawing {\n                    if ctx.data.get().is_empty() { State::Idle } else { State::Completed }\n                } else {\n                    *state\n                };\n                return Some(TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(move |ctx| {\n                    ctx.disabled = disabled;\n                    ctx.readonly = readonly;\n                    ctx.pen_color = pen_color;\n                    ctx.pen_width = pen_width;\n                    ctx.min_distance = min_distance;\n                    if cancel_drawing {\n                        ctx.current_stroke = None;\n                    }\n                }));\n            }"
}

{

  "old_string": "    fn connect<'a>(\n        state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api { state, ctx, props, send }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 1.6 Connect / API",
  "new_string": "    fn connect<'a>(\n        state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api { state, ctx, props, send }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Builds the `Effect::DataChange` effect, notifying `Props::on_data_change`\n/// with the value just committed (`pending()`, which in controlled mode is the\n/// new data even though `get()` still returns the stale parent-owned value).\nfn data_change_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {\n    PendingEffect::new(Effect::DataChange, |ctx, props, _send| {\n        if let Some(callback) = &props.on_data_change {\n            callback(ctx.data.pending().clone());\n        }\n        no_cleanup()\n    })\n}\n```\n\n### 1.6 Connect / API"
}

> AGENT

I've addressed all 5 Codex P2 findings with TDD and synced the specs. Summary of the fixes:

1. **Cancel drawing on disable** (mod.rs) — `SyncProps` now cancels the in-flight stroke and leaves `Drawing` when the pad becomes disabled/readonly mid-stroke, so it can't get stuck or commit a stale stroke.
2. **`on_data_change` callback** (mod.rs) — new `Props` callback + `Effect::DataChange` fired on commit/undo/clear, so controlled `data` is actually usable (parent observes changes, round-trips via `SyncData`).
3. **Reject zero-sized raster** (signature_raster.rs) — guards `width/height == 0` before encoding, returning `RasterError::Backend` instead of empty "valid" bytes from the `data:,` sentinel.
4. **Disable hidden input when disabled** (mod.rs) — disabled pad's hidden input gets `disabled` so it's excluded from form submission.
5. **Point-based `is_empty`** (raster/mod.rs) — emptiness counts recorded points, so data with only empty strokes is treated as blank (init→Idle, guide shown, clear/undo disabled).

New tests cover each (incl. a 5th wasm browser test for zero-size); spec synced across signature-pad.md, 11-dom-utilities §13. The second `cargo xci-fast` is running; once green I'll commit, push, reply inline + resolve all 5 threads, and re-trigger `@codex review`, looping until 👍.

Not fired for\n  parent-driven syncs; skipped when clearing an already-blank pad.\n- Reject zero-sized raster specs in `WebSignatureRasterizer` before encoding —\n  a 0×N canvas makes `toDataURL` return the `data:,` sentinel, which would\n  otherwise decode to empty bytes mistakable for a valid image.\n- Mark the hidden input `disabled` when the pad is disabled, so a disabled\n  signature is excluded from form submission (matching other form-backed\n  components).\n- Make `SignatureData::is_empty` point-based (no recorded points) rather than\n  stroke-count-based, so externally supplied data containing only empty\n  strokes is treated as blank (init stays `Idle`, guide shown, clear/undo\n  disabled), consistent with `to_svg_path`/`point_count`.\n\nSpecs synced: signature-pad.md (Props, Effect, transition, hidden input,\nis_empty) and foundation/11-dom-utilities §13 (zero-size rejection).\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.8 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\ngit push 2>&1 | tail -4\necho \"=== head commit ===\"; git log --oneline -1",
  "description": "Commit and push Codex fixes"
}

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gnrih
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs:784  (reply_to=3345657270)
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Base undo state on point emptiness**

When externally supplied/deserialized data contains empty strokes, which `SignatureData::is_empty()` explicitly treats as blank, using only `strokes.len()` can leave the machine in `Completed` after undoing the last real stroke. For example `[empty_stroke, real_stroke]` starts completed, `Undo` pops the real stroke, but this branch keeps `target` as `Completed`, so the root reports `data-ars-state="completed"` while `api.is_empty()` is true and the triggers are disabled; compute the target from the data after the pop using `is_empty()` instead.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gnrij
file: crates/ars-dom/src/signature_raster.rs:140  (reply_to=3345657275)
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Reject toDataURL sentinel exports**

For browser raster exports whose requested canvas dimensions exceed the browser's maximum canvas size, `toDataURL()` returns the sentinel `data:,` just like the zero-size case; this helper currently accepts that string, falls back to the requested format, decodes an empty payload, and returns `Ok(RasterImage { bytes: [] })`. That lets oversized signature exports appear successful with invalid empty image bytes, so reject `data:,` or an empty/untyped payload as a backend error before decoding.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.

=== THREAD PRRT_kwDORp4enM6Gnrik
file: crates/ars-components/src/specialized/signature_pad/mod.rs:764  (reply_to=3345657276)
**<sub><sub>![P2 Badge](https://img.shields.io/badge/P2-yellow?style=flat)</sub></sub>  Preserve pending controlled strokes**

In controlled mode, `get()` keeps returning the last parent-owned value until the parent round-trips `on_data_change`, so a second stroke drawn before that sync clones stale data here and overwrites the first pending stroke (the callback for stroke B reports only `[B]` instead of `[A, B]`). Base read-modify-write transitions on `ctx.data.pending()` when staging user changes so rapid controlled interactions cannot lose prior pending edits.

Useful? React with 👍 / 👎.
```

Three more P2 findings — all real follow-ons from the prior fixes. All valid:
- **A:** `Undo` target uses `strokes.len()`, but with empty-stroke entries that disagrees with point-based `is_empty()` → compute target from post-pop `is_empty()`.
- **B:** zero-size guard doesn't cover *oversized* canvases (also return `data:,`) → reject empty data-URL payload in decode.
- **C:** controlled-mode read-modify-write reads stale `get()` → a second stroke before the parent round-trips overwrites the first pending stroke → build on `pending()`.

  "old_string": "/// Decodes a `data:<mime>;base64,<payload>` URL into the MIME-derived format and\n/// raw bytes via `window.atob`.\n///\n/// The returned format comes from the data URL's own MIME (so a browser's silent\n/// substitution is reported truthfully), falling back to `requested` if the\n/// header is unparseable. `atob` yields a binary string whose code points are\n/// byte values `0..=255`; truncating each `char` to `u8` recovers the bytes.\n#[cfg(all(feature = \"web\", target_arch = \"wasm32\"))]\nfn decode_data_url(\n    window: &Window,\n    data_url: &str,\n    requested: RasterFormat,\n) -> Result<(RasterFormat, Vec<u8>), RasterError> {\n    let (header, base64) = data_url\n        .split_once(',')\n        .ok_or_else(|| RasterError::Backend(\"malformed data URL\".into()))?;\n\n    // header looks like `data:image/png;base64`\n    let format = header\n        .strip_prefix(\"data:\")\n        .and_then(|rest| rest.split(';').next())\n        .and_then(RasterFormat::from_mime)\n        .unwrap_or(requested);\n\n    let binary = window\n        .atob(base64)\n        .map_err(|_| RasterError::Backend(\"atob failed\".into()))?;\n\n    Ok((format, binary.chars().map(|code| code as u8).collect()))\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Parses a `data:<mime>;base64,<payload>` URL into the MIME-derived format and\n/// the base64 payload, rejecting the `data:,` sentinel and any empty payload.\n///\n/// The format comes from the URL's own MIME — so a browser's silent substitution\n/// (e.g. Safari WebP→PNG) is reported truthfully — falling back to `requested`\n/// when the header carries no recognized MIME. A canvas whose dimensions are\n/// zero *or larger than the browser maximum* instead returns the `data:,`\n/// sentinel (empty payload); that is rejected as a backend error rather than\n/// decoded into empty \"valid\" image bytes.\n#[cfg(feature = \"web\")]\nfn parse_data_url_header(\n    data_url: &str,\n    requested: RasterFormat,\n) -> Result<(RasterFormat, &str), RasterError> {\n    let (header, payload) = data_url\n        .split_once(',')\n        .ok_or_else(|| RasterError::Backend(\"malformed data URL\".into()))?;\n\n    if payload.is_empty() {\n        return Err(RasterError::Backend(\n            \"canvas produced no image data (dimensions too large or unsupported)\".into(),\n        ));\n    }\n\n    // header looks like `data:image/png;base64`\n    let format = header\n        .strip_prefix(\"data:\")\n        .and_then(|rest| rest.split(';').next())\n        .and_then(RasterFormat::from_mime)\n        .unwrap_or(requested);\n\n    Ok((format, payload))\n}\n\n/// Decodes a data URL into the MIME-derived format and raw bytes via\n/// `window.atob`, after [`parse_data_url_header`] validates it.\n///\n/// `atob` yields a binary string whose code points are byte values `0..=255`;\n/// truncating each `char` to `u8` recovers the bytes.\n#[cfg(all(feature = \"web\", target_arch = \"wasm32\"))]\nfn decode_data_url(\n    window: &Window,\n    data_url: &str,\n    requested: RasterFormat,\n) -> Result<(RasterFormat, Vec<u8>), RasterError> {\n    let (format, payload) = parse_data_url_header(data_url, requested)?;\n\n    let binary = window\n        .atob(payload)\n        .map_err(|_| RasterError::Backend(\"atob failed\".into()))?;\n\n    Ok((format, binary.chars().map(|code| code as u8).collect()))\n}"
}
```

  "new_string": "        assert_eq!(\n            WebSignatureRasterizer.rasterize(&strokes, &RasterSpec::new(10, 10)),\n            Err(RasterError::Unsupported)\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_data_url_rejects_sentinel_and_empty_payload() {\n        // The `data:,` sentinel (zero-sized OR oversized canvas) and any empty\n        // payload must be a backend error, not decoded into empty bytes.\n        assert!(matches!(\n            parse_data_url_header(\"data:,\", RasterFormat::Png),\n            Err(RasterError::Backend(_))\n        ));\n        assert!(matches!(\n            parse_data_url_header(\"data:image/png;base64,\", RasterFormat::Png),\n            Err(RasterError::Backend(_))\n        ));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_data_url_reads_format_from_mime_with_fallback() {\n        // Honest format detection: the data URL's own MIME wins.\n        let (format, payload) =\n            parse_data_url_header(\"data:image/webp;base64,QQ==\", RasterFormat::Png)\n                .expect(\"valid webp data URL\");\n        assert_eq!(format, RasterFormat::Webp);\n        assert_eq!(payload, \"QQ==\");\n\n        // A header with no recognized MIME falls back to the requested format.\n        let (format, _) = parse_data_url_header(\"data:;base64,QQ==\", RasterFormat::Jpeg)\n            .expect(\"non-empty payload\");\n        assert_eq!(format, RasterFormat::Jpeg);\n    }\n}"
}

{

  "old_string": "        // Parent applies the proposed data back through props.\n        drop(service.set_props(test_props().min_distance(0.0).data(pending)));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().data.get().strokes.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── Api accessors ─────────────────────────",
  "new_string": "        // Parent applies the proposed data back through props.\n        drop(service.set_props(test_props().min_distance(0.0).data(pending)));\n        assert_eq!(service.context().data.get().strokes.len(), 1);\n        assert_eq!(service.state(), &State::Completed);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn controlled_rapid_strokes_accumulate_in_pending() {\n        use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\n\n        // Controlled, with the parent not yet round-tripping: a second stroke\n        // drawn before the sync must build on the pending value, not the stale\n        // controlled one, so the first stroke is not lost.\n        let log: Arc<Mutex<Vec<SignatureData>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));\n        let sink = Arc::clone(&log);\n        let mut service = Service::<Machine>::new(\n            test_props()\n                .min_distance(0.0)\n                .data(SignatureData::default())\n                .on_data_change(ars_core::callback(move |data: SignatureData| {\n                    sink.lock().unwrap().push(data);\n                })),\n            &Env::default(),\n            &Messages::default(),\n        );\n\n        draw_one_stroke(&mut service); // stroke A\n        draw_one_stroke(&mut service); // stroke B, before any parent sync\n\n        let calls = log.lock().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(calls.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(calls[0].strokes.len(), 1, \"first callback reports [A]\");\n        assert_eq!(\n            calls[1].strokes.len(),\n            2,\n            \"second callback reports [A, B], not just [B]\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(service.context().data.pending().strokes.len(), 2);\n    }\n\n    // ───────────────────────── Api accessors ─────────────────────────"
}

  "new_string": "                let commits = ctx\n                    .current_stroke\n                    .as_ref()\n                    .is_some_and(|stroke| stroke.points.len() >= 2);\n                // Build on the pending (internal) working copy, not `get()`: in\n                // controlled mode `get()` returns the stale parent value until it\n                // round-trips, so reading it would drop earlier un-synced strokes.\n                let target = if commits || !ctx.data.pending().is_empty() {\n                    State::Completed\n                } else {\n                    State::Idle\n                };\n                let mut plan = TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(|ctx| {\n                    if let Some(stroke) = ctx.current_stroke.take() {\n                        if stroke.points.len() >= 2 {\n                            let mut data = ctx.data.pending().clone();\n                            data.strokes.push(stroke);\n                            ctx.data.set(data);\n                        }\n                    }\n                });\n                if commits {\n                    plan = plan\n                        .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceProvided))\n                        .with_effect(data_change_effect());\n                }\n                Some(plan)\n            }\n\n            // Undo last stroke. The target is computed from post-pop point\n            // emptiness (not stroke count, which empty-stroke entries break), and\n            // built on the pending working copy to preserve un-synced edits.\n            (State::Completed, Event::Undo) => {\n                let mut data = ctx.data.pending().clone();\n                data.strokes.pop();\n                let target = if data.is_empty() { State::Idle } else { State::Completed };\n                Some(TransitionPlan::to(target).apply(move |ctx| {\n                    ctx.data.set(data);\n                }).with_effect(data_change_effect()))\n            }\n\n            // Clear all strokes. Notify the parent only when there was data.\n            (_, Event::Clear) => {\n                let had_data = !ctx.data.pending().is_empty();"

```
3107	Given `strokes: &[Vec<RasterPoint>]` and a `RasterSpec`, `rasterize`:
3108	
3109	0. Rejects a zero-sized spec (`width == 0 || height == 0`) with
3110	   `RasterError::Backend` — such a canvas makes `toDataURL` return the `data:,`
3111	   sentinel, which would otherwise decode to empty bytes mistakable for a valid
3112	   image.
3113	1. Creates a detached `<canvas>` via `document.createElement("canvas")` sized to
3114	   `spec.width` × `spec.height`, and acquires its `2d` context.
3115	2. Fills `spec.background` (a CSS color) when set; otherwise leaves the surface
3116	   transparent (PNG) — JPEG has no alpha, so an unset background renders as the
3117	   platform default.
3118	3. Strokes each polyline **segment by segment**, setting `lineWidth` per segment
3119	   to `spec.pen_width × pressure_scale`, where `pressure_scale` maps the average
3120	   endpoint pressure to `0.5..=1.0` so a segment is always visible and firmer
3121	   presses render thicker. `lineCap`/`lineJoin` are `round`.
3122	4. Encodes via `canvas.toDataURL` using `spec.format.mime()` (PNG, or JPEG/WebP
3123	   honoring `spec.quality`), then decodes the base64 data URL to raw bytes with
3124	   `window.atob`.
3125	
3126	Returns `RasterImage { format, bytes }`, or `RasterError::Backend(_)` if any
3127	browser step fails. On non-wasm targets the implementation returns
3128	`RasterError::Unsupported`.
3129	
3130	**Honest format reporting.** A browser that cannot encode the requested type
3131	silently substitutes PNG (notably Safari for WebP). The implementation derives
3132	`RasterImage::format` from the returned data URL's own MIME via
3133	`RasterFormat::from_mime`, not from the request — so a WebP request that fell
3134	back to PNG reports `Png` with PNG bytes, never a mislabeled `Webp`.
3135	
3136	### 13.2 Reusers

The parser rejects the `data:,` sentinel / any empty\n   payload as `RasterError::Backend` — a canvas that is zero-sized *or larger\n   than the browser maximum* yields that sentinel, which must not decode into\n   empty \"valid\" image bytes.\n\nReturns `RasterImage { format, bytes }`, or `RasterError::Backend(_)` if any\nbrowser step fails. On non-wasm targets the implementation returns\n`RasterError::Unsupported`."

> AGENT

Codex's second pass found 3 more P2 issues (all real follow-ons from the first round's fixes) — addressed with TDD:

- **Undo target** now computed from post-pop point-`is_empty()`, so empty-stroke entries can't strand the pad in `Completed` while blank.
- **Sentinel rejection**: the data-URL parser (now a native-testable `parse_data_url_header`) rejects the `data:,` sentinel / empty payload, covering *oversized* canvases (not just zero-size).
- **Pending working copy**: all controlled-mode read-modify-writes (DrawEnd/Undo/Clear/SyncProps-cancel) build on `pending()`, so rapid strokes before the parent round-trips no longer drop earlier pending strokes.

Tests green (74 ars-components + 3 ars-dom native + 5 wasm), clippy clean (native + wasm32), specs synced. xci-fast is re-running; once green I'll commit, push, reply + resolve the 3 threads, and re-trigger `@codex review`.
